<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<TQueries>
  <Queries>
    <Query ID="RevisarUsuarios" remarks="">
      SELECT T0."Código","Descripción" FROM ((
      SELECT 'ACTLab' AS "Código",'ACTLaboratorio' AS "Descripción" FROM DUMMY
      UNION ALL SELECT 'ACTTras' AS "Código",'ACTTraslado' AS "Descripción" FROM DUMMY
      UNION ALL SELECT 'ACTParq' AS "Código",'ACTParque' AS "Descripción" FROM DUMMY
      UNION ALL SELECT 'ACTuml' AS "Código",'ACTUml' AS "Descripción" FROM DUMMY
      ))  AS T0 LEFT OUTER JOIN
      (SELECT T3."USER_CODE" AS "Código" FROM OUSR T3
      WHERE
      T3."USER_CODE" IN ('ACTLab') OR
      T3."USER_CODE" IN ('ACTTras') OR
      T3."USER_CODE" IN ('ACTParq') OR
      T3."USER_CODE" IN ('ACTuml')
      ) AS T1 ON T0."Código" = T1."Código" WHERE T1."Código" IS NULL
    </Query>
    <Query ID="ArticulosComprometidosOrdenServicio">

      SELECT
      T0."DocNum" AS "NroSolicitud",
      T2."WhsCode",
      T1."U_Cant" AS "CantSolicitada",
      T2."OnOrder" AS "Pedido",
      T2."OnHand" AS "Stock",
      T2."IsCommited" AS "Comprometido",
      T1."U_docOrd" AS "NroOrden"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T1 ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
      INNER JOIN "OITW" T2 ON T1. "U_ItemCode" = T2."ItemCode"
      AND T1."U_almacen" = T2."WhsCode"
      WHERE T0."Canceled" = 'N'
      AND  T0."U_Estado" = '1'
      AND T1. "U_ItemCode" =  '%itemCode%'
      AND T1."U_almacen" =  '%Almacen%'
      AND T0."DocEntry" &lt;&gt; '%docEntry%'
      AND ifnull(T1."U_Documento" ,'')= ''
      ORDER  BY T0."DocNum"
    </Query>
    <Query ID="oldArticulosDisponiblesOrdenServicio">
      SELECT IFNULL(Sum("Cantidad"), 0) AS "Total"
      FROM   (SELECT IFNULL(Sum("OnHand"),0) AS "Cantidad"
      FROM   OITW
      WHERE  "ItemCode" = '%itemCode%'
      AND "WhsCode" = '%almacen%'
      UNION ALL
      SELECT IFNULL(Sum(IFNULL(T0."U_Cant",0)),0) *- 1 AS "Cantidad"
      FROM   "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T1
      ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
      WHERE  T0."U_ItemCode" = '%itemCode%'
      AND T0."U_almacen" = '%almacen%'
      AND IFNULL(T0."U_Documento", '') = ''
      AND T0."DocEntry" &lt;&gt; '%docEntry%'
      AND T1."U_Estado" = 1) TBLA
    </Query>
    <Query ID="ArticulosDisponiblesOrdenServicio">
      SELECT IFNULL(Sum("OnHand"),0) AS "Cantidad"
      FROM   OITW
      WHERE  "ItemCode" = '%itemCode%'
      AND "WhsCode" = '%almacen%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="ArticulosDisponiblesOrdenTraslado">
      SELECT IFNULL(Sum("Cantidad"), 0) AS "Total"
      FROM   (
      SELECT IFNULL(Sum("OnHand"),0) AS "Cantidad"
      FROM   OITW
      WHERE  "ItemCode" = '%itemCode%'
      AND    "WhsCode" = '%almacen%'

      UNION ALL

      SELECT     IFNULL(Sum(IFNULL(T0."U_cantArt",0)),0) *- 1 AS "Cantidad"
      FROM       "@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA_ELE" T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA" T1
      ON         T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"

      WHERE      T0."U_nomArt" = '%itemCode%'
      AND        T0."U_almacenOri" = '%almacen%'
      AND        IFNULL(T0."U_Documento", '') = ''
      AND        T0."DocEntry" &lt;&gt;'%docEntry%'
      AND          T1."U_estOT" = 'Abierto'

      ) TBLA
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetDetalleFactura">
      SELECT
      T0."ItemCode" AS "itemcode",
      T0."Dscription" AS "descripcion",
      T0."Quantity" AS "cantidad",
      T0."Price"   AS "precio",
      T0."TaxCode" AS "codimpue",
      T0."LineTotal" AS "LineTotal",
      T0."WhsCode" AS "WarehouseCode",
      T0."AcctCode" AS "AccountCode",
      T0."OcrCode" AS "CostingCode",
      T0."OcrCode2" AS "CostingCode2",
      T0."OcrCode3" AS "CostingCode3",
      T0."OcrCode4" AS "CostingCode4",
      T0."LineNum"   AS "LineNum",
      T0."U_destFinal"   AS "DestinoFinal",
      T0."U_linea"   AS "LineIdOSF",
      T0."U_Facturacion",
      T0."U_Condonacion",
      T0."U_Intereses",
      T0."U_IdLinea"
      FROM "INV1" T0
      WHERE T0."DocEntry" = '%docentry%'
      AND IFNULL(T0."Dscription",'N')&lt;&gt;'N'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetEncabezadoFactura">
      SELECT
      T0."DocNum",
      T0."DocDate",
      T0."TaxDate",
      T0."DocDueDate",
      T0."CardCode",
      T0."DocType",
      T0."U_OK1_SF_NSOL",
      T0."U_idFallec",
      T0."U_sucur",
      T0."U_mun",
      T0."U_NomFallec",
      T0."Series",
      T0."CtlAccount",
      IFNULL(T0."Rounding",'N')AS "Rounding",
      T0."RoundDif",
      T0."U_OK1_SF_FACTCONV",
      T0."U_OK1_SF_FACTREC",
      T0."U_OK1_SF_NPRENEC"
      FROM "OINV" T0
      WHERE "DocEntry" = '%docentry%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetDetalleEntrega">
      SELECT
      "ItemCode" AS "itemcode",
      "Dscription" AS "descripcion",
      "Quantity" AS "cantidad",
      "Price"   AS "precio",
      "TaxCode" AS "codimpue",
      "LineTotal" AS "LineTotal",
      "WhsCode" AS "WarehouseCode",
      "AcctCode" AS "AccountCode",
      "OcrCode" AS "CostingCode",
      "OcrCode2" AS "CostingCode2",
      "OcrCode3" AS "CostingCode3",
      "OcrCode4" AS "CostingCode4",
      "LineNum"   AS "LineNum",
      "U_destFinal"   AS "DestinoFinal",
      "U_linea"   AS "LineIdOSF",
      "U_Facturacion",
      "U_Condonacion",
      "U_Intereses",
      "U_IdLinea"
      FROM "DLN1" T0
      WHERE "DocEntry" = '%docentry%'
      AND IFNULL("Dscription",'N')&lt;&gt;'N'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetEncabezadoEntrega">
      SELECT
      T0."DocNum",
      T0."DocDate",
      T0."TaxDate",
      T0."DocDueDate",
      T0."CardCode",
      T0."DocType",
      T0."U_OK1_SF_NSOL",
      T0."U_idFallec",
      T0."U_sucur",
      T0."U_mun",
      T0."U_NomFallec",
      T0."Series",
      T0."CtlAccount",
      IFNULL(T0."Rounding",'N')AS "Rounding",
      T0."RoundDif",
      T0."U_OK1_SF_FACTCONV",
      T0."U_OK1_SF_FACTREC"
      FROM "ODLN" T0
      WHERE "DocEntry" = '%docentry%'
    </Query>

    <Query ID="UpdateQueryDocumentos">
      SELECT "DocEntry" AS "No. Int",
      "DocNum"   AS "No.",
      CASE
      WHEN CANCELED = 'Y' THEN ( 'Factura de clientes - Cancelado' )
      WHEN "DocStatus" = 'O' THEN ( 'Factura de clientes - Abierta' )
      WHEN (select IFNULL(Count(T0."DocEntry"), 0) AS "Cuenta"
      from   ORCT T0
      INNER JOIN RCT2 T1
      ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocNum"
      AND T1."DocEntry" = T2."DocEntry"
      AND "InvType" = '13'
      AND T0."Canceled" = 'N') > 0 THEN (
      'Factura de clientes - Pagada' )
      ELSE ( 'Factura de clientes - Cerrada' )
      END        AS "Tipo",
      "Comments" AS "Comentarios",
      "DocTotal" AS "Total",
      "CardCode" AS "Código",
      "CardName" AS "Nombre",
      '13'       AS "TipoDoc"
      FROM   OINV T2
      WHERE  "U_OK1_SF_NSOL" = '%solicitud%'
      AND "U_OK1_PQC_NOPQ" IS NULL
      UNION ALL
      SELECT "DocEntry"            AS "No. Int",
      "DocNum"              AS "No.",
      'Nota Crédito' AS "Tipo",
      "Comments"           AS "Comentarios",
      "DocTotal"            AS "Total",
      "CardCode"            AS "Código",
      "CardName"            AS "Nombre",
      '14'                  AS "TipoDoc"
      FROM   "ORIN"
      WHERE  "U_OK1_SF_NSOL" = '%solicitud%'
      UNION ALL
      SELECT "DocEntry"            AS "No. Int",
      "DocNum"              AS "No.",
      'Devolución' AS "Tipo",
      "Comments"           AS "Comentarios",
      "DocTotal"            AS "Total",
      "CardCode"            AS "Código",
      "CardName"            AS "Nombre",
      '16'                  AS "TipoDoc"
      FROM   "ORDN"
      WHERE  "U_OK1_SF_NSOL" = '%solicitud%'
      UNION ALL
      SELECT "DocEntry" AS "No. Int",
      "DocNum"   AS "No.",
      CASE
      WHEN CANCELED = 'Y' THEN ( 'Entrega Convenio Particular - Cancelado' )
      WHEN "DocStatus" = 'O' THEN ( 'Entrega Convenio Particular - Abierta' )
      WHEN (select IFNULL(Count(T0."DocEntry"), 0) AS "Cuenta"
      from   ORCT T0
      INNER JOIN RCT2 T1
      ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocNum"
      AND T1."DocEntry" = T2."DocEntry"
      AND "InvType" = '15'
      AND T0."Canceled" = 'N') > 0 THEN (
      'Entrega Convenio Particular - Pagada' )
      ELSE ( 'Entrega Convenio Particular - Cerrada' )
      END        AS "Tipo",
      "Comments" AS "Comentarios",
      "DocTotal" AS "Total",
      "CardCode" AS "Código",
      "CardName" AS "Nombre",
      '15'       AS "TipoDoc"
      FROM   ODLN T2
      WHERE  "U_OK1_SF_NSOL" = '%solicitud%'
      UNION ALL
      SELECT "DocEntry"             AS "No. Int",
      "DocNum"               AS "No.",
      'Factura de proveedor' AS "Tipo",
      "Comments"            AS "Comentarios",
      "DocTotal"             AS "Total",
      "CardCode"             AS "Código",
      "CardName"             AS "Nombre",
      '18'                   AS "TipoDoc"
      FROM   OPCH
      WHERE  "U_OK1_SF_NSOL" = '%solicitud%'
      UNION ALL
      SELECT
      T0."DocEntry"           AS "No. Int",
      T0."DocNum"           AS "No.",
      'Salida de mercancía' AS "Tipo",
      T0."Comments"                 AS "Comentarios",
      0       			   AS "Total",
      ''                     AS "Código",
      ''                     AS "Nombre",
      '60'                   AS "TipoDoc"
      FROM OIGE T0
      WHERE  U_OK1_SF_NSOL = '%solicitud%'
      UNION ALL
      SELECT
      T0."DocEntry"           AS "No. Int",
      T0."DocNum"           AS "No.",
      'Cancelación salida de mercancía' AS "Tipo",
      T0."Comments"                 AS "Comentarios",
      0                    			  AS "Total",
      ''                                  AS "Código",
      ''                                  AS "Nombre",
      '59'                                AS "TipoDoc"
      FROM OIGN T0
      WHERE  U_OK1_SF_NSOL = '%solicitud%'
      UNION ALL
      SELECT "DocEntry"       AS "No. Int",
      "DocNum"         AS "No.",
      'Pago efectuado' AS "Tipo",
      "Comments"      AS "Comentarios",
      "DocTotal"       AS "Total",
      "CardCode"       AS "Código",
      "CardName"       AS "Nombre",
      '204'            AS "TipoDoc"
      FROM   OVPM
      WHERE  "U_OK1_SF_NSOL" = '%solicitud%'
      UNION ALL
      SELECT "DocEntry" AS "No. Int",
      "DocNum"   AS "No.",
      CASE
      WHEN CANCELED = 'Y' THEN ( 'Orden de compra'
      || '-'
      || (SELECT "SeriesName"
      FROM   NNM1
      WHERE  "Series" = T0."Series")
      || '-'
      || 'Cancelado' )
      ELSE ( 'Orden de compra'
      || '-'
      || (SELECT "SeriesName"
      FROM   NNM1
      WHERE  "Series" = T0."Series") )
      END        AS "Tipo",
      "Comments"AS "Comentarios",
      "DocTotal" AS "Total",
      "CardCode" AS "Código",
      "CardName" AS "Nombre",
      '22'       AS "TipoDoc"
      FROM   OPOR T0
      WHERE  "U_OK1_SF_NSOL" = '%solicitud%'
      UNION ALL
      SELECT "DocEntry" AS "No. Int",
      "DocNum"   AS "No.",
      CASE
      WHEN "JrnlMemo" = 'Cancelado' THEN 'Pago Recibido-Cancelado'
      ELSE 'Pago Recibido'
      END        AS "Tipo",
      "Comments" AS "Comentarios",
      "DocTotal" AS "Total",
      "CardCode" AS "Código",
      "CardName" AS "Nombre",
      '24'       AS "TipoDoc"
      FROM   ORCT
      WHERE  "U_OK1_SF_NSOL" = '%solicitud%'
      UNION ALL
      SELECT "DocEntry" AS "No. Int",
      "DocNum"   AS "No.",
      CASE
      WHEN CANCELED = 'Y' THEN ( 'Orden de venta - Cancelado' )
      WHEN "DocStatus" = 'O' THEN ( 'Orden de venta - Abierta' )
      ELSE ( 'Orden de venta - Cerrada' )
      END        AS "Tipo",
      "Comments" AS "Comentarios",
      "DocTotal" AS "Total",
      "CardCode" AS "Código",
      "CardName" AS "Nombre",
      '17'       AS "TipoDoc"
      FROM   ORDR T2
      WHERE  "U_OK1_SF_NSOL" = '%solicitud%'
      UNION ALL
      SELECT "DocEntry" AS "No. Int",
      "DocNum"   AS "No.",
      'Solicitud de Crédito' AS "Tipo",
      'Fecha Solicitud: ' || CAST(T0."U_SFechaSolicitud" AS DATE) || ', Cédula: ' || T0."U_SCedula" || ', Nombre: ' || T0."U_SNombre"  AS "Comentarios",
      T0."U_SValorHipoteca" AS  "Total",
      T0."U_SCedula" AS "Código",
      T0."U_SNombre" AS "Nombre",
      'SC'       AS "TipoDoc"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_SOLCREDITO"T0 WHERE T0."U_NumId" = '%solicitud%'
      UNION ALL
      SELECT "TransId" AS "No. Int",
      "BaseRef"   AS "No.",
      'Registro de Provisión' AS "Tipo",
      "Memo" AS "Comentarios",
      "LocTotal" AS "Total",
      '' AS "Código",
      '' AS "Nombre",
      '30'       AS "TipoDoc"
      FROM   OJDT
      WHERE  "U_OK1_SF_NSOL" =  '%solicitud%'
      AND "TransCode" = 'RPRO'
      UNION ALL
      SELECT "DocEntry" AS "No. Int",
      "DocNum"   AS "No.",
      'Prefactura de clientes' AS "Tipo",
      "Comments" AS "Comentarios",
      "DocTotal" AS "Total",
      "CardCode" AS "Código",
      "CardName" AS "Nombre",
      '112'       AS "TipoDoc"
      FROM   ODRF T2
      WHERE  "U_OK1_SF_NSOL" =  '%solicitud%'
      AND "U_OK1_PQC_NOPQ" IS NULL
      AND "DocEntry" = (SELECT MAX("DocEntry") FROM   ODRF
      WHERE  "U_OK1_SF_NSOL" =  '%solicitud%')

    </Query>
    <Query ID="UpdateQueryCertGas">
      SELECT
      "DocEntry" AS "N° Interno",
      "U_solicita" AS "Quien Solicita",
      "U_fechaAnex" AS "Fecha Anexo",
      "U_solicitud" AS "Solicitud",
      "U_documento" AS "Documento"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_CERTGAST_ANE"
      WHERE "U_solicitud"='%solicitud%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetPrecioArticuloDeConvenio">
      SELECT T1."U_Precio" FROM "@OK1_SF_CNVPRO_HEAD" T0 INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_CNVELEM_LIN" T1 ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry" WHERE T0."U_CodPro"=TRIM('%codProveedor%') AND T1."U_CodEle"=TRIM('%codArticulo%') AND NOW() BETWEEN T0."U_FecIni" AND T0."U_FecFin"
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetParentescoContrato">
      SELECT t0.* FROM "@OK1_EXE_CONT_BENEFI" t0 INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_CONTR_HEAD" t1 ON t0."DocEntry" = t1."DocEntry"
      WHERE t1."U_numcont"=TRIM('%idContrato%') and t0."U_numdoc"=TRIM('%idBeneficiario%')
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetPlanRepatriacionBeneficiario">
      SELECT "U_planr" AS PlanRep FROM "@OK1_EXE_BEN_REPATRI" WHERE "U_numdoc"=TRIM('%idBeneficiario%') and "DocEntry" = TRIM('%idContrato%')
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetInfoBeneficiarioRepatriacion">
      SELECT * FROM "@OK1_EXE_BEN_REPATRI" WHERE "U_numdoc"=TRIM('%idBeneficiario%') and "DocEntry" = TRIM('%idContrato%')
    </Query>
    <Query ID="ExisteBeneficiarioEnContratoExequial">
      SELECT * FROM (
      SELECT t0."LicTradNum", t0."CardName", t0."U_fecnac" AS "Fecha Nacimiento", '' AS "Parentesco", DAYS_BETWEEN(IFNULL(T0."U_fecnac", NOW()), NOW()) AS "Edad", 'Contratante' AS "Tipo" FROM "OCRD" t0 WHERE "CardCode" = (SELECT "U_contrant" FROM "@OK1_EXE_CONTR_HEAD" WHERE "DocEntry" = TRIM('%idContrato%')) UNION ALL
      SELECT "U_numdoc", "U_nombre", "U_fecnaci" AS "Fecha Nacimiento", "U_parent" AS "Parentesco", "U_edad" as "Edad", "U_tdbenef" as "Tipo" FROM "@OK1_EXE_CONT_BENEFI" WHERE "DocEntry" = TRIM('%idContrato%') AND "U_numdoc" IS NOT NULL AND "U_nombre" IS NOT NULL UNION ALL
      SELECT "U_numdoc", "U_nombre", NULL AS "Fecha Nacimiento", '' as "Parentesco", 0 as "Edad", '' as "Tipo" FROM "@OK1_EXE_BEN_REPATRI" WHERE "DocEntry" = TRIM('%idContrato%')
      ) t9 WHERE t9."LicTradNum" = TRIM('%idBeneficiario%')
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetLineasFacturasAuxilios" remarks="Obtiene las lineas de las facturas (de artículos) de proveedor que estás asociadas a una solicitud dado un pago efectuado">
      SELECT T0."DocEntry", T1."ItemCode", T0."U_OK1_SF_NSOL" FROM "OPCH" T0 INNER JOIN "PCH1" T1 ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry" WHERE T0."DocEntry" IN (SELECT "DocEntry" FROM "VPM2" WHERE "DocNum" = TRIM('%docEntryPago%') AND "InvType" = '18') AND T0."DocType" = 'I' AND T0."U_OK1_SF_NSOL" IS NOT NULL
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetLineasPedidoDerechosAuxilios" remarks="Obtiene las lineas de los  Pedidos (de artículos) que estás asociadas a una solicitud servicio funerario">
      SELECT
      T0."DocEntry",
      T1."ItemCode",
      T0."U_OK1_SF_NSOL"
      FROM "OPOR" T0
      INNER JOIN "POR1" T1 ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
      WHERE T0."DocEntry" = TRIM('%docEntry%')
      AND T0."U_OK1_SF_NSOL" IS NOT NULL
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetLineasPedido" remarks="Obtiene las lineas de los  Pedidos (de artículos) que estás asociadas a una solicitud servicio funerario">
      SELECT
      T0."DocEntry",
      T1."ItemCode",
      T1."LineTotal",
      T1."U_linea" AS "Linea"
      FROM "OPOR" T0
      INNER JOIN "POR1" T1 ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
      WHERE T0."DocEntry" = TRIM('%docEntry%')
      AND T0."U_OK1_SF_NSOL" IS NOT NULL
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetLineasPedidoOPQ" remarks="Obtiene las lineas de los  Pedidos (de artículos) que estás asociadas a una solicitud servicio funerario">
      SELECT
      T0."DocEntry",
      T1."ItemCode",
      T1."LineTotal",
      T1."U_linea" AS "Linea"
      FROM "OPOR" T0
      INNER JOIN "POR1" T1 ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
      WHERE T0."DocEntry" = TRIM('%docEntry%')
      AND T0."U_OK1_PQC_NOPQ" IS NOT NULL
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetLineasSolicitudCompraOPQ" remarks="Obtiene las lineas de la solicitud de compra (de artículos) que estás asociadas a una orden de parque">
      SELECT
      T0."DocEntry",
      T1."ItemCode",
      T1."LineTotal",
      T1."U_linea" AS "Linea"
      FROM "OPRQ" T0
      INNER JOIN "PRQ1" T1 ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
      WHERE T0."DocEntry" = TRIM('%docEntry%')
      AND T0."U_OK1_PQC_NOPQ" IS NOT NULL
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetConceptosComisiones" remarks="Obtiene los conceptos de comisiones para cargar en la solicitud de servicio">
      SELECT * FROM "@OK1_SF_CPTCOM" ORDER BY "Code"
    </Query>
    <Query ID="TotalIngresos" remarks="Calcula el total de las facturas de cliente para una solicitud">
      SELECT SUM("DocTotal") AS "DocTotal" FROM "OINV" WHERE "U_OK1_SF_NSOL" = '%solicitud% AND "U_OK1_PQC_NOPQ" IS NULL'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="UpdateFechaRetiroBenef" remarks="actualiza la fecha de retiro de un beneficiario en el contrato">
      UPDATE "@OK1_EXE_CONT_BENEFI" SET "U_fecRet" = '%fecha%' WHERE "U_numdoc" = TRIM('%idBeneficiario%')
      AND "DocEntry" = TRIM('%idContrato%') AND ("U_fecRet" IS  NULL OR "U_fecRet" = '');
    </Query>
    <Query ID="UpdateValorFechaRetiroBenef" remarks="Actualiza valor por retiro de un beneficiario en el contrato">
      UPDATE "@OK1_EXE_CONTR_HEAD" T0 SET T0."U_valor" = T0."U_valor" -
      (SELECT IFNULL(T1."U_valor",0) FROM "@OK1_EXE_CONT_BENEFI" T1  WHERE T1."DocEntry" = TRIM('%idContrato%')
      AND T1."U_numdoc" = TRIM('%idBeneficiario%'))  WHERE T0."DocEntry" = TRIM('%idContrato%');
    </Query>
    <Query ID="UpdateFechaFallecBenef" remarks="actualiza la fecha de fallecimiento de un beneficiario en el contrato">
      UPDATE "@OK1_EXE_CONT_BENEFI" SET "U_fecSin" = '%fecha%', "U_numSolicitud" = '%numSolicitud%' WHERE "U_numdoc" = TRIM('%idBeneficiario%')
    </Query>
    <Query ID="UpdateFechaFallecRetBenef" remarks="actualiza la fecha de fallecimiento y retiro de un beneficiario en el contrato">
      UPDATE "@OK1_EXE_CONT_BENEFI" SET "U_fecSin" = '%fechaSin%',"U_fecRet" = '%fecha%', "U_numSolicitud" = '%numSolicitud%' WHERE "U_numdoc" = TRIM('%idBeneficiario%')
    </Query>
    <Query ID="UpdateFechaFallecBenefPrenec" remarks="actualiza la fecha de fallecimiento de un beneficiario en el contrato">
      UPDATE "%db%" SET "U_fecSin" = '%fecha%', "U_numSolicitud" = '%numSolicitud%' WHERE "U_numdoc" = TRIM('%idBeneficiario%')
    </Query>
    <Query ID="BuscarContratoFunerarios" remarks="Buscar el contrato asociado al servicio">

      || ' ' || IFNULL("U_sape", ''), '  ', ' ') AS "Beneficiario", T1."U_parent" AS "Parentesco", T2."Name" AS "Desc Parentesco",
      T0."U_contrant" AS "Contratante", T0."U_ncontrat" AS "Nombre Contratante", T0."DocEntry" AS "Contrato",
      T0."U_fecIn" AS "Fecha Inicio", T0."U_fecFi" AS "Fecha Fin", T0."DocEntry" AS "Referencia", T0."U_nomcon" AS "Convenio",T0."U_plan" AS "Plan",
      T0."U_nompla" AS "Nombre Plan",	T0."U_estado" AS "Estado",	T0."U_nomuen" AS "UEN", "U_fecnaci" AS "Fecha Nacimiento", T1."U_fecIng" AS "Fecha Ingreso", T1."U_fecTrs" AS "Fecha Traslado",
      DAYS_BETWEEN(IFNULL(T1."U_fecTrs", T0."U_fecIn"), NOW()) AS "Permanencia", T1."U_fecSin" AS "Fecha Siniestro", T1."U_fecRet" AS "Fecha Retiro", T1."U_edad" AS EDAD, T1."U_tdbenef" AS "TipoBenef"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_CONTR_HEAD" AS T0 INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_CONT_BENEFI" AS T1 ON T1."DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry" AND (T0."U_estado"  IN (SELECT "U_EstadoCo" FROM "@OK1_SF_MAPEOEST"))
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_PARENTESCOS" AS T2 ON T2."Code" = T1."U_parent"
      INNER JOIN "@BPCO_TD" AS T3 ON T3."Code" = T1."U_tdd"
      WHERE
    </Query>

    <Query ID="ReporteCertGas">

      INSERT
      INTO "@OK1_SF_CERTGASTEMP" ("DocEntry",
      "U_DiaExp",
      "U_MesExp",
      "U_YearExp",
      "U_NumSolicitud",
      "U_NumCertificacion",
      "U_Documento",
      "U_Nombre",
      "U_NombreFa",
      "U_IdFallec",
      "U_DiaFal",
      "U_MesFal",
      "U_YearFal",
      "U_Name",
      "U_Valor",
      "U_NumContr",
      "U_User") (SELECT
      ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
      ORDER BY T0."DocNum")||T4."DocEntry" AS "DocEntry",
      DAYOFMONTH(NOW()) "DiaExp",
      MONTH(NOW()) "MesExp",
      YEAR(NOW()) "YearExp",
      T0."DocNum" "NumSolicitud",
      T4."DocNum" "NumCertificacion",
      CAST(T4."U_Documento" AS NVARCHAR) AS "U_Documento",
      T4."U_Nombre",
      T0."U_NombreFa",
      T0."U_IdFallec",
      DAYOFMONTH(T0."U_FechaFal") "DiaFal",
      MONTH(T0."U_FechaFal") "MesFal",
      YEAR(T0."U_FechaFal") "YearFal",
      T3."Name",
      T2."U_Valor",
      T0."U_NumContr",
      (select
      "U_NAME"
      FROM OUSR
      where "USERID" = T0."UserSign") "User"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T0
      LEFT JOIN "@OK1_EXE_CONTR_HEAD" T1 ON T0."U_NumContr" = TO_VARCHAR(T1."DocEntry")
      LEFT JOIN "@OK1_SF_CERTGAS_LIN" T2 ON T2."U_Year" = YEAR(T0."U_FechaSol")
      AND (CASE WHEN T1."U_conve" IN (SELECT
      "DocNum"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_CONV_HEAD"
      WHERE "U_certGastos" = 'Y')
      THEN 'Especial'
      ELSE (CASE T0."U_TipoCubr" WHEN 1
      THEN 'Integral' WHEN 2
      THEN 'Tradicional'
      ELSE 'Especial'
      END )
      END) = T2."U_TipoCubr"
      LEFT JOIN "@OK1_SF_CERTGAS_HE" T3 ON T3."Code" = T2."Code"
      LEFT JOIN "@OK1_SF_REPCERTGAS" T4 ON T0."DocNum" = T4."U_Solicitud"
      WHERE T0."DocNum" = '%Solicitud%'
      AND T4."DocNum" ='%NumCert%')
    </Query>
    <Query ID="ReporteCertGas1">


      SELECT
      ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY T0."DocNum")||T4."DocEntry" AS "DocEntry",
      DAYOFMONTH(NOW()) "DiaExp",
      MONTH(NOW()) "MesExp",
      YEAR(NOW()) "YearExp",
      T0."DocNum" "NumSolicitud",
      T4."DocNum" "NumCertificacion",
      T4."U_Documento", T4."U_Nombre",
      T0."U_NombreFa", T0."U_IdFallec",
      DAYOFMONTH(T0."U_FechaFal") "DiaFal",
      MONTH(T0."U_FechaFal") "MesFal",
      YEAR(T0."U_FechaFal") "YearFal",
      T3."Name", T2."U_Valor", T0."U_NumContr",
      (select "U_NAME" FROM OUSR where "USERID" = T0."UserSign") "User"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T0
      LEFT JOIN "@OK1_EXE_CONTR_HEAD" T1 ON T0."U_NumContr" = TO_VARCHAR(T1."DocEntry")
      LEFT JOIN "@OK1_SF_CERTGAS_LIN" T2 ON T2."U_Year" = YEAR(T0."U_FechaSol")
      AND (CASE WHEN T1."U_conve" IN (SELECT "DocNum" FROM "@OK1_EXE_CONV_HEAD" WHERE "U_certGastos" = 'Y') THEN 'Especial'
      ELSE (CASE T0."U_TipoCubr" WHEN 1 THEN 'Integral' WHEN 2 THEN 'Tradicional' ELSE 'Especial' END ) END) = T2."U_TipoCubr"
      LEFT JOIN "@OK1_SF_CERTGAS_HE" T3 ON T3."Code" = T2."Code"
      LEFT JOIN "@OK1_SF_REPCERTGAS" T4 ON T0."DocNum" = T4."U_Solicitud"
      WHERE T0."DocNum" = '%Solicitud%'
      AND T4."DocNum" = '%NumCert%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetDerechoValidaAdicionalFallecido">

      WHERE "Code" = (SELECT "U_plan" FROM "@OK1_EXE_CONTR_HEAD" WHERE "DocEntry" =  %numContrato%) AND "U_codaux" =  '%idAuxilio%'
    </Query>

    <Query ID="BuscarActividadesTraslado">
      SELECT T0."ClgCode" AS "Código", CASE T0."Action" WHEN 'C' THEN 'Llamada' WHEN 'M' THEN 'Reunion' WHEN 'T' THEN 'Tarea' WHEN 'E' THEN 'Nota' ELSE 'Otro' END AS "Actividad", T1."U_NAME" AS "Asignado a", T0."Recontact" AS "Fecha", T0."Details" AS "Comentario"
      FROM OCLG T0 INNER JOIN OUSR T1
      ON T0."AttendUser" = T1.USERID
      WHERE T0."U_DocEntry" = '%idSolicitud%' AND "CntctType" = (SELECT "Code" FROM OCLT WHERE "U_tipoAct" = 'T')
    </Query>
    <Query ID="BuscarOrdenesTraslado">
      SELECT
      "DocEntry" AS "Nº Orden",
      "U_numSolTras" AS "Nº Solicitud Traslado",
      "U_fecCooTras" AS "Fecha",
      "U_horCooTras" AS "Hora",
      "U_tipoCond" AS "Tipo Conductor",
      "U_condAsig" AS "Conductor Asignado",
      (SELECT T0."CardName" FROM OCRD T0 WHERE T0."CardCode" = "U_condAsig") AS "Nombre",
      (SELECT USER_CODE FROM OUSR WHERE USERID = "UserSign") AS "Usuario",
      "U_estOT"  AS "Estado"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA" WHERE "U_nroOrdenSF" = '%idSolicitud%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="BuscarActividadesLaboratorio">

      FROM OCLG T0 INNER JOIN OUSR T1
      ON T0."AttendUser" = T1.USERID
      WHERE T0."U_DocEntry" = '%idSolicitud%' AND "CntctType" = (SELECT "Code" FROM OCLT WHERE "U_tipoAct" = 'L')

    <Query ID="LoadSeriesporSede" remarks="">

      AND T0."Series" IN
      (
      SELECT "U_serieCotizSF" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE "U_sede" ='%idSede%'
      UNION ALL
      SELECT "U_serieOrdenSF" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE "U_sede" ='%idSede%'
      UNION ALL
      SELECT "U_serieAP" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE "U_sede" ='%idSede%'
      )
    </Query>

    <Query ID="CambiarEstadoCotiza" remarks="">
      UPDATE  "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" SET
      "U_Estado" = '%Estado%' WHERE ("U_IdFallec" = '%idFallec%' OR "DocEntry" = '%solAnte%') AND "DocEntry" != '%idSolic%'
      AND  "Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieCotizSF" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE "U_sede" = "U_VSede"
      UNION ALL SELECT "U_serieAP" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE "U_sede" = "U_VSede")
      AND "U_Estado" != '6'
    </Query>

      "U_MotCierre" = '%motCierre%', "U_nOSFadd" = %idSolic% WHERE "DocEntry" = '%solAnte%'
    </Query>

    <Query ID="GetElementosBasicos" remarks="">
      SELECT TBLA1."Code", TBLA1."Name", TBLA2."U_cant",TBLA1."U_TipoOrden" AS "Tipo Orden",'P' AS "BASICO"  FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" TBLA1
      INNER JOIN (    SELECT "U_elemCode" AS "U_OK1_SF_CODELEM", "U_cantidad" AS "U_cant" FROM "@OK1_EXE_VIGPLNSPREL" T2
      WHERE T2."Code" = '%idConvenio%' || '-' ||  '%idPlan%' || '-' || (
      SELECT CAST("U_IdLinea" AS NVARCHAR) FROM "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEFEC" T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEM" T1 ON T0."Code"= T1."Code"
      WHERE T1."U_codConv" = '%idConvenio%' AND T1."U_codPlan" = '%idPlan%'
      AND '%Fecha%' BETWEEN T0."U_fecini"  AND T0."U_fecfin" AND T0."U_nivServ" = '%nivelServicio%' limit 1)  %filtro% )TBLA2
      ON TBLA1."Code"=TBLA2."U_OK1_SF_CODELEM"
      WHERE TBLA1."Code"  IN (SELECT "Code" FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" WHERE "U_elemBasico" = 'Y')
      ORDER BY  TBLA1."Code"
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetElementosConvenio" remarks="">
      SELECT TBLA1."Code", TBLA1."Name", TBLA2."U_cant",TBLA1."U_TipoOrden" AS "Tipo Orden",'N' AS "BASICO"  FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" TBLA1
      INNER JOIN (    SELECT "U_elemCode" AS "U_OK1_SF_CODELEM", "U_cantidad" AS "U_cant" FROM "@OK1_EXE_VIGPLNSPREL" T2 WHERE T2."Code" = '%idConvenio%' || '-' ||  '%idPlan%' || '-' || (
      SELECT CAST("U_IdLinea" AS NVARCHAR) FROM "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEFEC" T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEM" T1 ON T0."Code"= T1."Code"
      WHERE T1."U_codConv" = '%idConvenio%' AND T1."U_codPlan" = '%idPlan%'
      AND '%Fecha%' BETWEEN T0."U_fecini"  AND T0."U_fecfin" AND T0."U_nivServ" = '%nivelServicio%' limit 1) %filtro% )TBLA2
      ON TBLA1."Code"=TBLA2."U_OK1_SF_CODELEM"
      WHERE TBLA1."Code" NOT IN (SELECT "Code" FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" WHERE "U_elemBasico" = 'Y')
      ORDER BY  TBLA1."Code"
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetVlrTopeElementoConvenio" remarks="">
      SELECT IFNULL("U_vlrTope",0) FROM "@OK1_EXE_VIGPLNSPREL" T2 WHERE T2."Code" = '%idConvenio%' || '-' ||  '%idPlan%' || '-' || (
      SELECT CAST("U_IdLinea" AS NVARCHAR) FROM "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEFEC" T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEM" T1 ON T0."Code"= T1."Code"
      WHERE T1."U_codConv" = '%idConvenio%' AND T1."U_codPlan" = '%idPlan%'
      AND '%Fecha%' BETWEEN T0."U_fecini"  AND T0."U_fecfin" AND T0."U_nivServ" = '%nivelServicio%' limit 1)
      AND "U_elemCode" = '%idElemento%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetVlrCubiertoElementoConvenio" remarks="">
      SELECT  SUM(IFNULL("U_vlrTope" * T3."U_Cant",0))  FROM "@OK1_EXE_VIGPLNSPREL" T2 INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T3
      ON T2."U_elemCode" = T3."U_Codigo" WHERE T2."Code" = '%idConvenio%' || '-' ||  '%idPlan%' || '-' || (
      SELECT CAST("U_IdLinea" AS NVARCHAR) FROM "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEFEC" T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEM" T1 ON T0."Code"= T1."Code"
      WHERE T1."U_codConv" = '%idConvenio%' AND T1."U_codPlan" = '%idPlan%'
      AND '%fechaOSF%' BETWEEN T0."U_fecini"  AND T0."U_fecfin" AND T0."U_nivServ" = '%nivelServicio%' limit 1)  %filtro%
      AND T3."DocEntry" =  '%nOSF%'
      AND T3."U_tipLinea" NOT IN ('H','C')
      AND T3."U_ArtAdMe" != 'A'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetVlrExcedenteArticuloConvenio" remarks="">
      SELECT IFNULL("U_vlrExce",0) FROM "@OK1_EXE_VIGPLNSPRAR" T2 WHERE T2."Code" = '%idConvenio%' || '-' ||  '%idPlan%' || '-' || (
      SELECT CAST("U_IdLinea" AS NVARCHAR) FROM "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEFEC" T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEM" T1 ON T0."Code"= T1."Code"
      WHERE T1."U_codConv" = '%idConvenio%' AND T1."U_codPlan" = '%idPlan%'
      AND '%Fecha%' BETWEEN T0."U_fecini"  AND T0."U_fecfin" AND T0."U_nivServ" = '%nivelServicio%' limit 1)
      AND "U_ItemCode" = '%idArticulo%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetVlrArticuloConvenio" remarks="">
      SELECT IFNULL("U_vlrVtaArt",0) FROM "@OK1_EXE_VIGPLNSPRAR" T2 WHERE T2."Code" = '%idConvenio%' || '-' ||  '%idPlan%' || '-' || (
      SELECT CAST("U_IdLinea" AS NVARCHAR) FROM "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEFEC" T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEM" T1 ON T0."Code"= T1."Code"
      WHERE T1."U_codConv" = '%idConvenio%' AND T1."U_codPlan" = '%idPlan%'
      AND '%Fecha%' BETWEEN T0."U_fecini"  AND T0."U_fecfin" AND T0."U_nivServ" = '%nivelServicio%' limit 1)
      AND "U_ItemCode" = '%idArticulo%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetVlrBasicoConvenio" remarks="">
      SELECT IFNULL("U_vlrBasico",0) FROM "@OK1_EXE_VIGPLNSPRBA" T2 WHERE T2."Code" = '%idConvenio%' || '-' ||  '%idPlan%' || '-' || (
      SELECT CAST("U_IdLinea" AS NVARCHAR) FROM "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEFEC" T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEM" T1 ON T0."Code"= T1."Code"
      WHERE T1."U_codConv" = '%idConvenio%' AND T1."U_codPlan" = '%idPlan%'
      AND CURRENT_DATE BETWEEN T0."U_fecini"  AND T0."U_fecfin"  AND T0."U_nivServ" = '%nivelServicio%' limit 1)
      AND "U_Sede" = '%idSede%' AND "U_ItemCode" = '%idArticulo%'
    </Query>

    </Query>
    <Query ID="cabeceraOrdenTras">
      SELECT
      T0."DocEntry" AS "nro Sol Tras",
      T2."U_IdFallec" AS "IdFallecido",
      T2."U_NombreFa" AS "NombreFa",
      CASE  WHEN IFNULL(T0."U_DocEntryOPC",'') != '' THEN (SELECT "U_numListCh" FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" WHERE  "DocEntry" = T0."U_DocEntryOPC") ELSE T2."U_numListCh" END AS "nro LCH",
      CASE  WHEN IFNULL(T0."U_DocEntryOPC",'') != '' THEN T0."U_DocEntryOPC" ELSE T0."U_DocEntry" END AS "NumeroIntSF",
      T0."CntctSbjct" AS "Tipo Traslado",
      T1."U_NAME" AS "Asignado a",
      T0."Details" AS "Comentario",
      T2."U_VSede" AS "SedeServicio",
      CASE T0."Action" WHEN 'C'
      THEN 'Llamada' WHEN 'M'
      THEN 'Reunion' WHEN 'T'
      THEN 'Tarea' WHEN 'E'
      THEN 'Nota'
      ELSE 'Otro'
      END AS "Actividad",
      T0."U_identAcompa",
      T0."U_nombreAcompa",
      T0."U_telefonoAcompa",
      T0."U_parentescoAcompa",
      T0."Duration",
      T0."Recontact",
      T0."endDate",
      T0."BeginTime",
      T0."ENDTime",
      T0."U_horSolic",
      T0."U_tipoSolic",
      T2."U_HomEsp",
      T0."U_estadoVias",
      T0."U_tipoTraslOrg",
      T0."U_tipoTraslDes",
      "U_fallMult",
      (SELECT COUNT(TT0."DocEntry") FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA" TT0 WHERE TT0."U_numSolTras" = '%docEntry%' ) "Asignado",
      T0."AssignedBy",
      T0."Notes",
      CASE  WHEN IFNULL(T0."U_DocEntryOPC",'') != '' THEN (SELECT "DocNum" FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" WHERE  "DocEntry" = T0."U_DocEntryOPC") ELSE T2."DocNum" END AS "DocNum",
      CASE  WHEN IFNULL(T0."U_DocEntryOPC",'') != '' THEN (SELECT "U_tipListCh" FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" WHERE  "DocEntry" = T0."U_DocEntryOPC") ELSE  T2."U_tipListCh"  END AS "U_tipListCh"

      FROM OCLG T0
      INNER JOIN OUSR T1 ON T0."AttendUser" = T1.USERID
      INNER JOIN "%TablaInner%" T2 ON T0."%OnTablaInner%" = T2."DocEntry"
      WHERE T0."ClgCode" = %docEntry%
      AND "CntctType" = (SELECT "Code" FROM OCLT WHERE "U_tipoAct" = 'T')

    </Query>
    <Query ID="acompanante">
      SELECT
      T0."U_identAcompa",
      T0."U_nombreAcompa",
      T0."U_telefonoAcompa",
      T0."U_parentescoAcompa",
      T0."U_estadoVias"
      FROM OCLG T0
      WHERE T0."ClgCode" = %docEntry%
    </Query>
    <Query ID="cabeceraOrdenTrasElementos">
      SELECT       T0."U_Codigo",       T0."U_Nombre",       T0."U_ItemCode",       T0."U_ItemName",       T0."U_Uso",       T0."LineId",       T0."U_IdLinea",       T0."DocEntry",       T0."U_Renuncia",       T0."U_Cant",
      T3."InvntItem",
      T0."U_ArtAdMe",       T0."U_almacen",       T0."U_tipOrden",

      T0."U_Documento",
      T0."U_DescDoc",
      T0."U_TipDocB1",
      T0."U_IdLinea",
      T0."U_IdLineaPF",
      T0."U_NoContr_Prenec",
      T0."U_ItemName",
      T0."U_LPagadaPrenec",
      T0."U_NoFacturaPrenec",
      T0."U_Linea",
      T0."U_LineaName",
      T0."U_tipLinea",
      T0."U_LineaPadre",
      T0."U_ItemCodePB",
      T0."U_LineaPadrePN",
      T0."U_CardCode",
      T0."U_ValorCom"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"   T0
      INNER JOIN "OCLG" T1
      ON T0."U_ItemCode" = T1."U_ItemCodeTras"
      LEFT JOIN  OITM T3 ON T0."U_ItemCode" = T3."ItemCode"
      WHERE T1."ClgCode" = %SolicTras%
      AND T0."DocEntry" ='%DocEntry%'
      AND IFNULL(T0."U_Renuncia",'N')='N'
      AND IFNULL (T0."U_Uso",'') &lt;&gt; 'Y'
      ORDER BY T0."LineId"
    </Query>

    <Query ID="guardarDispoRecurso">
      CALL "OK1_SF_DISPONIBILIDAD_RECURSO"('%nroDoc%', '%CodeRecurso%', '%ObjectType%', '%fechaIni%', '%horaIni%', '%fechaFin%',
      '%horaFin%', '%tipoRecuros%', '%disponible%', '%consulta%');
    </Query>
    <Query ID="verificarDispoRecurso">
      CALL "OK1_SF_DISPONIBILIDAD_RECURSO"('%nroDoc%', '%CodeRecurso%', '%ObjectType%', '%fechaIni%', '%horaIni%', '%fechaFin%',
      '%horaFin%', '%tipoRecuros%', '%disponible%', '%consulta%');
    </Query>
    <Query ID="verificarDispoRecursoSimul">
      SELECT "U_nroDoc", "U_ObjectType", "U_tipoRecurso", "U_dispo"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_DISP_RECURSO"
      WHERE (('%fechaIni%' BETWEEN "U_fechaIni" AND "U_fechaFin")
      OR ('%fechaFin%' BETWEEN "U_fechaIni" AND "U_fechaFin")
      OR ("U_fechaIni" &gt; '%fechaIni%' AND "U_fechaFin" &lt; '%fechaFin%'))
      AND IFNULL("U_CodeRecurso", '') = '%CodeRecurso%'
      AND "U_nroDoc" NOT IN ('%nroDoc%')
    </Query>
    <Query ID="verificarDiponibilidadFechas">
      SELECT "U_nroDoc" AS "Nro Doc", "U_CodeRecurso" AS "Recurso", "U_fechaIni" As "Fecha Inicial",
      "U_horaIni" AS "Hora Inicial", "U_fechaFin" AS "Fecha Final",  "U_horaFin" AS "Hora Final", (CASE WHEN "U_dispo"  = '' THEN 'Si' ELSE 'No' END) AS "Simultaneo"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_DISP_RECURSO"
      WHERE (('%fechaInicial%' BETWEEN "U_fechaIni" AND "U_fechaFin")
      OR ('%fechaFinal%' BETWEEN "U_fechaIni" AND "U_fechaFin")
      OR ("U_fechaIni" &gt; '%fechaInicial%' AND "U_fechaFin" &lt; '%fechaFinal%'))
      AND ("U_dispo"  = 'N' OR "U_dispo"  = '')
      AND "U_tipoRecurso" = %tipoRecurso%
    </Query>
    <Query ID="verificarDiponibilidadFechasConductores">
      SELECT "U_nroDoc" AS "Nro Doc", "U_CodeRecurso" AS "Recurso","CardName" AS "Nombre Recurso", "U_fechaIni" As "Fecha Inicial",
      "U_horaIni" AS "Hora Inicial", "U_fechaFin" AS "Fecha Final",  "U_horaFin" AS "Hora Final", (CASE WHEN "U_dispo"  = '' THEN 'Si' ELSE 'No' END) AS "Simultaneo"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_DISP_RECURSO" INNER JOIN "OCRD" ON "U_CodeRecurso"="CardCode"
      WHERE (('%fechaInicial%' BETWEEN "U_fechaIni" AND "U_fechaFin")
      OR ('%fechaFinal%' BETWEEN "U_fechaIni" AND "U_fechaFin")
      OR ("U_fechaIni" &gt; '%fechaInicial%' AND "U_fechaFin" &lt; '%fechaFinal%'))
      AND ("U_dispo"  = 'N' OR "U_dispo"  = '')
      AND "U_tipoRecurso" = %tipoRecurso%
    </Query>
    <Query ID="liberarRecurso">
      CALL "OK1_SF_DISPONIBILIDAD_RECURSO"('%nroDoc%', '%CodeRecurso%', '%ObjectType%', '%fechaIni%', '%horaIni%', '%fechaFin%',
      '%horaFin%', '%tipoRecuros%', '%disponible%', '%consulta%');
    </Query>
    <Query ID="verificarDocEntryDisp">
      SELECT TOP 1 "DocEntry", (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM "@OK1_SF_DISP_VEH_CON" T1 WHERE T0."DocEntry" =  T1."U_nroDoc"  AND "U_ObjectType" = '%objectType%' AND T1."U_dispo" = 'N') AS "Disponible"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA" T0
      ORDER BY "DocEntry" DESC;
    </Query>

    </Query>
    <Query ID="seleccionarConductor">
      SELECT
      T0."CardCode",
      T0."CardName"
      FROM OCRD T0
      WHERE T0."frozenFor" = 'N'AND CURRENT_DATE BETWEEN  IFNULL(T0."frozenFrom", CURRENT_DATE) AND IFNULL(T0."frozenTo", CURRENT_DATE)
      AND T0."U_tipProv" = '%tipo%'
      AND T0."U_fecVenLic" &gt;= CURRENT_DATE
    </Query>
    <Query ID="seleccionarVehiculo">
      SELECT "Code", "Code"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_VEHIC_HEAD"
      WHERE "U_Estado" = '1' AND
      (CURRENT_DATE  &lt;= IFNULL("U_vencCertFumig", CURRENT_DATE) AND
      CURRENT_DATE  &lt;= IFNULL("U_vencConcSalud", CURRENT_DATE) AND
      CURRENT_DATE  &lt;= IFNULL("U_vencRevTecn", CURRENT_DATE) AND
      CURRENT_DATE  &lt;= IFNULL("U_vencSeguro", CURRENT_DATE) AND
      CURRENT_DATE  &lt;= IFNULL("U_vencSoat", CURRENT_DATE) AND
      CURRENT_DATE  &lt;= IFNULL("U_fecVencBater", CURRENT_DATE) AND
      CURRENT_DATE  &lt;= IFNULL("U_fecVencExt", CURRENT_DATE) AND
      CURRENT_DATE  &lt;= IFNULL("U_vencCertFumig", CURRENT_DATE) AND
      CURRENT_DATE  &lt;= IFNULL("U_vencCertFumig", CURRENT_DATE)) AND
      "U_tipVehiculo" = '%tipo%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="seleccionarVehiConductor">
      SELECT T1."Code", T1."U_tipVehiculo"
      FROM OCRD T0
      LEFT JOIN "@OK1_SF_VEHIC_HEAD" T1 ON (T1."Code" = T0."U_vehiAsg" AND "U_Estado" = '1' AND
      CURRENT_DATE  &lt;= IFNULL("U_vencCertFumig", CURRENT_DATE) AND
      CURRENT_DATE  &lt;= IFNULL("U_vencConcSalud", CURRENT_DATE) AND
      CURRENT_DATE  &lt;= IFNULL("U_vencRevTecn", CURRENT_DATE) AND
      CURRENT_DATE  &lt;= IFNULL("U_vencSeguro", CURRENT_DATE) AND
      CURRENT_DATE  &lt;= IFNULL("U_vencSoat", CURRENT_DATE) AND
      CURRENT_DATE  &lt;= IFNULL("U_fecVencBater", CURRENT_DATE) AND
      CURRENT_DATE  &lt;= IFNULL("U_fecVencExt", CURRENT_DATE) AND
      CURRENT_DATE  &lt;= IFNULL("U_vencCertFumig", CURRENT_DATE) AND
      CURRENT_DATE  &lt;= IFNULL("U_vencCertFumig", CURRENT_DATE))
      WHERE T0."frozenFor" = 'N'AND CURRENT_DATE BETWEEN  IFNULL(T0."frozenFrom", CURRENT_DATE) AND IFNULL(T0."frozenTo", CURRENT_DATE)
      AND T0."CardCode" = '%conductor%'
    </Query>

    <Query ID="elementosOrdenLaboratorioListaMateriales">
      SELECT
      T0."Code" AS "Articulo",
      T0."Qauntity" As "Cantidad",
      T3."ItemName" As "NombreArticulo",
      T3."CardCode" AS "Proveedor",
      T3."InvntItem" AS "InvntItem",
      T0."ToWH" AS "WhsCode",
      (SELECT "OnOrder" FROM OITW WHERE "ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%' AND "WhsCode" =T0."ToWH") AS "Pedido",
      (SELECT "OnHand" FROM OITW WHERE "ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%' AND "WhsCode" =T0."ToWH") AS "Stock",
      (SELECT "IsCommited" FROM OITW WHERE "ItemCode"= '%ItemCode%' AND "WhsCode" =T0."ToWH") AS "Comprometido",
      T3."U_OK1_SF_ELEM" AS "Elemento",
      (SELECT "Name" FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" WHERE "Code" = T3."U_OK1_SF_ELEM")AS "NomElemento"
      ,(SELECT "U_cofre" FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" WHERE "Code" = T3."U_OK1_SF_ELEM")AS "Cofre"
      FROM OITT T0
      INNER JOIN OITM T3 ON T0."Code" = T3."ItemCode"
      WHERE "U_tipListSF" = 'T'
      AND T0."Code"= '%ItemCode%'
      union
      (SELECT
      T0."Code"AS "Articulo",
      T0."Quantity"As "Cantidad",
      T3."ItemName" As "NombreArticulo",
      T3."CardCode" AS "Proveedor",
      T3."InvntItem" AS "InvntItem",
      T0."Warehouse" AS "WhsCode",
      (SELECT "OnOrder" FROM OITW WHERE "ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%' AND "WhsCode" =T0."Warehouse") AS "Pedido",
      (SELECT "OnHand" FROM OITW WHERE "ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%' AND "WhsCode" =T0."Warehouse") AS "Stock",
      (SELECT "IsCommited" FROM OITW WHERE "ItemCode"= '%ItemCode%' AND "WhsCode" =T0."Warehouse") AS "Comprometido",
      T3."U_OK1_SF_ELEM" AS "Elemento",
      (SELECT "Name" FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" WHERE "Code" = T3."U_OK1_SF_ELEM")AS "NomElemento"
      ,(SELECT "U_cofre" FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" WHERE "Code" = T3."U_OK1_SF_ELEM")AS "Cofre"
      FROM ITT1 T0
      INNER JOIN OITM T3 ON T0."Code" = T3."ItemCode"
      WHERE T0."Father"= '%ItemCode%')
    </Query>
    <Query ID="ArticuloElementosOrdenlaboratorio">

    <Query ID="VlrPendienteCapInteCredito">
      SELECT SUM(TINFOF."vlrCap") AS "pendiCap", SUM(TINFOF."vlrInt") AS "pendiInt"
      FROM (SELECT CASE WHEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendCa", 0) != 0 THEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendCa", 0) ELSE T1."U_Capital" END AS "vlrCap",
      CASE WHEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendInt", 0) != 0 THEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendInt", 0) ELSE T1."U_Intereses" END AS "vlrInt"
      FROM "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" T1 WHERE T1."DocEntry" = (SELECT  "U_NroCredFinan" FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD" WHERE "DocEntry" = %numPrenecFinc%)
      AND T1."U_NroTabla" = (SELECT MAX(T0."U_NroTabla") FROM "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" T0 WHERE T0."DocEntry" =(SELECT  "U_NroCredFinan" FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD" WHERE "DocEntry" = %numPrenecFinc%))
      AND IFNULL(T1."U_Pagado", 'N') = 'N') AS TINFOF;
    </Query>

    <Query ID="VlrCapInteCredito">
      SELECT SUM(TINFOF."vlrCap") AS "pendiCap", SUM(TINFOF."vlrInt") AS "pendiInt"
      FROM (SELECT CASE WHEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendCa", 0) != 0 THEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendCa", 0) ELSE T1."U_Capital" END AS "vlrCap",
      CASE WHEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendInt", 0) != 0 THEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendInt", 0) ELSE T1."U_Intereses" END AS "vlrInt"
      FROM "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" T1 WHERE T1."DocEntry" = (SELECT  "U_NroCredFinan" FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD" WHERE "DocEntry" = %numPrenecFinc%)
      AND T1."U_NroTabla" = (SELECT MAX(T0."U_NroTabla") FROM "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" T0 WHERE T0."DocEntry" =(SELECT  "U_NroCredFinan" FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD" WHERE "DocEntry" = %numPrenecFinc%))) AS TINFOF;
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetMoraContrato">
      SELECT * FROM (SELECT T1."DocEntry" AS "Afiliación", T1."U_contrant" AS "Contratante", T1."U_ncontrat" AS "Nombre", T1."CreateDate" AS "Fecha", T1."U_conve" AS "Convenio",  T1."U_nomcon" AS "Nombre Convenio",  T1."U_plan" AS "Plan" ,T1."U_nomuen" AS "Uen",  T1."U_nomsuen" AS "Suen",T1."U_diaCorte" AS "Día Facturación", T0."TransId" AS "Factura",DAYS_BETWEEN(T0."DueDate", CURRENT_DATE) AS "Mora" ,'Y' AS "Crear",T1."U_fecAcPag" AS "Fecha Acuerdo Pago" FROM JDT1 T0 INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_CONTR_HEAD" T1 ON T0."U_contra" = CAST(T1."DocEntry" AS NVARCHAR) INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_ESTADOCONTR" T2 ON T1."U_estado" = T2."Code"
      WHERE (T0."TransCode"='OKEX') AND T0."BalDueDeb"> 0 AND (T2."U_factur" = 'Y' OR T1."U_estado" = 'ACTSREN')  AND T1."U_respon" = 'C'
      AND T1."DocEntry" = %numContrato%
      GROUP BY T1."DocEntry",T1."U_contrant",T1."U_ncontrat",T1."CreateDate", T1."U_conve", T1."U_nomcon",T1."U_plan",T1."U_nomuen", T1."U_nomsuen",T1."U_diaCorte",T1."U_fecAcPag",T0."TransId",T0."DueDate") TABLA WHERE TABLA."Mora" > (SELECT "U_cancMora" FROM "@OK1_EXE_CONV_HEAD" WHERE "DocEntry" = "Convenio")
      UNION ALL
      SELECT * FROM (SELECT T1."DocEntry" AS "Afiliación", T1."U_contrant" AS "Contratante", T1."U_ncontrat" AS "Nombre", T1."CreateDate" AS "Fecha", T1."U_conve" AS "Convenio",  T1."U_nomcon" AS "Nombre Convenio",  T1."U_plan" AS "Plan" ,T1."U_nomuen" AS "Uen",  T1."U_nomsuen" AS "Suen",T1."U_diaCorte" AS "Día Facturación",T0."TransId" AS "Factura",DAYS_BETWEEN(T0."DueDate", CURRENT_DATE) AS "Mora",'Y' AS "Crear",T1."U_fecAcPag" AS "Fecha Acuerdo Pago" FROM JDT1 T0 INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_CONTR_HEAD" T1 ON T0."U_contra" =  CAST(T1."DocEntry" AS NVARCHAR) INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_CONV_PLANES" T2 ON T1."U_conve" = T2."DocEntry" WHERE (MONTH(CURRENT_DATE) > (MONTH(T1."U_fecIn")) AND (YEAR(T1."U_fecIn") &gt;=  YEAR(CURRENT_DATE)))
      AND (T0."TransCode"='OKEX') AND T0."BalDueDeb"> 0 AND  T2."U_codPlan" = T1."U_plan" AND T2."U_activpor" = 'F' AND T1."U_estado" = 'PREG'  AND T1."U_respon" = 'C'
      AND T1."DocEntry" = %numContrato%
      GROUP BY T1."DocEntry",T1."U_contrant",T1."U_ncontrat",T1."CreateDate", T1."U_conve", T1."U_nomcon",T1."U_plan",T1."U_nomuen", T1."U_nomsuen",T1."U_diaCorte",T1."U_fecAcPag",T0."TransId",T0."DueDate") TABLA WHERE TABLA."Mora" > (SELECT "U_cancMora" FROM "@OK1_EXE_CONV_HEAD" WHERE "DocEntry" = "Convenio")
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetMoraConvenio">
      SELECT * FROM (SELECT T0."TransId" AS "Factura",DAYS_BETWEEN(T0."DueDate", CURRENT_DATE) AS "Mora",T1."U_conve" AS "Convenio"   FROM JDT1 T0
      INNER JOIN OJDT T1 ON T0."TransId" =  T1."TransId"
      WHERE T0."TransCode"='OKEX' AND T0."BalDueDeb"> 0
      AND T1."U_conve" = '%numConve%'
      GROUP BY T0."TransId",T0."DueDate",T1."U_conve") TABLA WHERE TABLA."Mora" > (SELECT "U_cancMora" FROM "@OK1_EXE_CONV_HEAD" WHERE "DocEntry" = "Convenio")
    </Query>

    <Query ID="VerDisponibilidadSalas">
      SELECT DISTINCT * FROM (
      SELECT T0."U_sedeVel" "Sede",T0."U_sedeVelNom" "Nombre Sede",T0."U_VLugar" "Sala",(SELECT "Name" FROM "@OK1_SF_SALAVELAC"  WHERE "Code" = T0."U_VLugar") "Nombre Sala",T0."U_VFecha" "Fecha de Entrada",T0."U_horVelEntr" "Hora de Entrada",T0."U_VFechaS" "Fecha de Salida",T0."U_horVelSal" "Hora de Salida",T0."DocEntry" "OSF",T0."U_UsuarioAsigSala" "Asignado por",IFNULL(T0."U_fallMulPpal",'N') "Fallecimiento Multiple",'Velación Estandar' "Tipo Velación"

      WHERE 			T0."U_sedeVel" =  '%idSede%'
      AND 			T0."U_VFecha" = '%Fecha%' OR  T0."U_VFechaS" = '%FechaS%'

      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T0 INNER JOIN  "@OK1_SF_MULTSALASLI" T1 ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."Code"
      WHERE 			T0."U_sedeVel" =  '%idSede%'
      AND 			T0."U_VFecha" = '%Fecha%' OR T0."U_VFechaS" = '%FechaS%'

      UNION ALL


      SELECT T0."U_sedeVel" "Sede",T0."U_sedeVelNom" "Nombre Sede",T0."U_VSalaExc" "Sala",(SELECT "Name" FROM "@OK1_SF_SALAVELAC"  WHERE "Code" = T0."U_VSalaExc") "Nombre Sala",T0."U_VFechaExc" "Fecha de Entrada",T0."U_horVelEntExc" "Hora de Entrada",T0."U_VFechaSalExc" "Fecha de Salida",T0."U_horVelSalExc" "Hora de Salida",T0."DocEntry" "OSF",T0."U_UsuarioAsigSalaExc" "Asignado por",IFNULL(T0."U_fallMulPpal",'N') "Fallecimiento Multiple",'Velación Excedente' "Tipo Velación"

      AND     T0."U_VFechaExc" = '%FechaExc%' OR  T0."U_VFechaSalExc" = '%FechaExcS%'
      UNION ALL

      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T0 INNER JOIN  "@OK1_SF_MULTSALASELI" T1 ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."Code"
      WHERE       T0."U_sedeVel" =  '%idSede%'
      AND       T0."U_VFechaExc" = '%FechaExc%' OR  T0."U_VFechaSalExc" = '%FechaExcS%'

      UNION ALL

      SELECT T0."U_sedeVel" "Sede",T0."U_sedeVelNom" "Nombre Sede",T0."U_VSalaR" "Sala",(SELECT "Name" FROM "@OK1_SF_SALAVELAC"  WHERE "Code" = T0."U_VSalaR") "Nombre Sala",T0."U_VFechaR" "Fecha de Entrada",T0."U_horVelEntRe" "Hora de Entrada",T0."U_VFechaSalR" "Fecha de Salida",T0."U_horVelSalRe" "Hora de Salida",T0."DocEntry" "OSF",T0."U_UsuarioAsigSalaRe" "Asignado por",IFNULL(T0."U_fallMulPpal",'N') "Fallecimiento Multiple",'Sala de Reencuentro' "Tipo Velación"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T0
      WHERE
      T0."U_sedeVel" =  '%idSede%' AND
      T0."U_VFechaR" = '%FechaReS%' OR T0."U_VFechaSalR" = '%FechaReS%'

      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T0 INNER JOIN  "@OK1_SF_MULTSALASRLI" T1 ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."Code"
      WHERE
      T0."U_sedeVel" =  '%idSede%' AND

      ) TB

    </Query>

    <Query ID="VerDisponibilidadExequias">
      SELECT * FROM (SELECT 'Orden SF - '||T0."DocEntry" "Documento", T0."U_sedeMisa" "Sede",T0."U_sedeExeNom" "Nombre Sede",T0."U_ELugar" "Sala",
      (SELECT "U_Nombre" FROM "@OK1_SF_IGLESIAS"  WHERE "Code" = T0."U_ELugar") "Nombre Sala",
      T0."U_EFecha" "Fecha de Entrada",T0."U_EHora" "Hora de Entrada",T0."U_horFiMisa" "Hora de Salida"
      ,T0."U_UsuarioAsigExe" "Asignado por",IFNULL(T0."U_fallMulPpal",'N') "Fallecimiento Multiple"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T0 WHERE T0."U_EFecha"='%Fecha%') TB
      UNION ALL

      SELECT 'Orden Parque - '||T0."DocEntry" "Documento", T0."U_sedeMisa" "Sede",(SELECT "descript" FROM "OTER" WHERE "territryID" = T0."U_sedeMisa") "Nombre Sede",T0."U_ELugar" "Sala",
      (SELECT "U_Nombre" FROM "@OK1_SF_IGLESIAS"  WHERE "Code" = T0."U_ELugar") "Nombre Sala",
      T0."U_EFecha" "Fecha de Entrada",T0."U_EHora" "Hora de Entrada",T0."U_horFiMisa" "Hora de Salida"
      ,T0."U_UsuarioAsigExe" "Asignado por",IFNULL(T0."U_fallMulPpal",'N') "Fallecimiento Multiple"
      FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" T0 WHERE T0."U_EFecha"='%Fecha%'
      UNION ALL

      SELECT * FROM (SELECT 'Misa Privada - '||T1."DocEntry" "Documento", (SELECT "U_sedeMisa" FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" WHERE
      "DocEntry"=(select "U_NoOSF" from "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" where "DocEntry"=T1."U_OrdenPQ")) "Sede",
      (SELECT "U_sedeExeNom" FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" WHERE
      "DocEntry"=(select "U_NoOSF" from "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" where "DocEntry"=T1."U_OrdenPQ")) "Nombre Sede",
      T1."U_Iglesia" "Sala", (SELECT "U_Nombre" FROM "@OK1_SF_IGLESIAS"  WHERE "Code" = T1."U_Iglesia") "Nombre Sala",
      T1."U_FechaMisa" "Fecha de Entrada",T1."U_HoraIni" "Hora de Entrada",T1."U_HoraFin" "Hora de Salida",
      (select "USER_CODE" from "OUSR" WHERE "USERID"=T1."UserSign") "Asignado por", (select '' from "DUMMY") "Fallecimiento Multiple"
      FROM "@OK1_PQC_MISASPR" T1 WHERE T1."U_FechaMisa"='%Fecha%') MP
      UNION ALL
      SELECT * FROM (SELECT 'Misa Conmemorativa - '||T2."DocEntry" "Documento",(select '' from "DUMMY") "Sede",(select '' from "DUMMY") "Nombre Sede",
      T2."U_Iglesia" "Sala", (SELECT "U_Nombre" FROM "@OK1_SF_IGLESIAS"  WHERE "Code" = T2."U_Iglesia") "Nombre Sala",
      T2."U_FechaMisa" "Fecha de Entrada",T2."U_HoraIni" "Hora de Entrada",T2."U_HoraFin" "Hora de Salida",
      T2."Creator" "Asignado por",(select '' from "DUMMY") "Fallecimiento Multiple"
      FROM "@OK1_PQC_MISACONMTVA" T2 WHERE T2."U_FechaMisa"='%Fecha%' ) MC
      ORDER BY TB."Sala",TB."Hora de Entrada",TB."Documento"
    </Query>
    <Query ID="VerNovedadesSalas">
      SELECT
      T0."Code" "Sala",
      T1."Name" "Nombre Sala",
      T0."U_Actividad" "Actividad",
      T0."U_fechaInicial" "Fecha Inicial",
      T0."U_horaInicial" "Hora Inicial",
      T0."U_fechaFinal" "Fecha Final",
      T0."U_horaFinal" "Hora Final",
      T0."U_comentario" "Comentario"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_NOVSALASVEL" T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_SALAVELAC" T1
      ON T0."Code" = T1."Code"
      WHERE ('%Fecha%' BETWEEN T0."U_fechaInicial" AND T0."U_fechaFinal")  OR  ('%FechaS%' BETWEEN T0."U_fechaInicial" AND T0."U_fechaFinal" )
      AND T1."U_Sede" = '%idSede%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="guardarDispoRecurso">
      CALL "OK1_SF_DISPONIBILIDAD_RECURSO"('%nroDoc%', '%CodeRecurso%', '%ObjectType%', '%fechaIni%', '%horaIni%', '%fechaFin%',
      '%horaFin%', '%tipoRecuros%', '%disponible%', '%consulta%');
    </Query>
    <Query ID="verificarDispoRecurso">
      CALL "OK1_SF_DISPONIBILIDAD_RECURSO"('%nroDoc%', '%CodeRecurso%', '%ObjectType%', '%fechaIni%', '%horaIni%', '%fechaFin%',
      '%horaFin%', '%tipoRecuros%', '%disponible%', '%consulta%');
    </Query>
    <Query ID="liberarRecurso">
      CALL "OK1_SF_DISPONIBILIDAD_RECURSO"('%nroDoc%', '%CodeRecurso%', '%ObjectType%', '%fechaIni%', '%horaIni%', '%fechaFin%',
      '%horaFin%', '%tipoRecuros%', '%disponible%', '%consulta%');
    </Query>
    <Query ID="verificarNovedadesSalas">
      SELECT
      T0."Code" "Sala",
      T0."LineId" "Line",
      T0."U_fechaInicial" "fecIn",
      T0."U_horaInicial" "horIn",
      T0."U_fechaFinal" "fecFi",
      T0."U_horaFinal" "horFi"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_NOVSALASVEL" T0
      WHERE T0."U_fechaInicial" &gt;= CURRENT_DATE
    </Query>

    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetLineaPrecioConvenio" remarks="">
      SELECT CAST("U_IdLinea" AS NVARCHAR) FROM "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEFEC" T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEM" T1 ON T0."Code"= T1."Code"
      WHERE T1."U_codConv" = '%idConvenio%' AND T1."U_codPlan" = '%idPlan%'
      AND '%FecSolic%' BETWEEN T0."U_fecini"  AND T0."U_fecfin"  AND T0."U_nivServ" = '%nivelServicio%' limit 1
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetCementerioVinculado" remarks="">
      SELECT T0."Code",T0."Name",(SELECT "U_tipUbicCem" FROM OITM WHERE "ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%') AS "Tipo" FROM "@OK1_SF_CEMENTERIOS" T0 INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_CEMTIPUBIC" T1
      ON T0."Code" = T1."Code" WHERE T1."U_ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetSalaVinculada" remarks="">
      SELECT "Code", "Name" FROM "@OK1_SF_SALAVELAC" WHERE  "U_ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetArticulosDestinoFinal" remarks="">
      SELECT "U_ItemCode" FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"  WHERE "DocEntry" =  %idSolic% AND "U_Codigo" IN (SELECT "Code" FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" WHERE "U_destFinal" = 'Y')
    </Query>
    <Query ID="AplicaIva">
      select "U_AplicaIva" from "@OK1_SF_GESTION_IVA"
      where "U_TipoElem"='%TipoElemento%' and "U_proceso"='%proceso%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="ConsultarPQRFactura">

    </Query>
    <Query ID="EstadoDevolucion">
      select "statusID" from "OSCS"
      where "U_DevPago"='Y'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="RetornarSaldoTotalCarteraCreditoPrenecesidad">
      SELECT
      IFNULL(SUM(TINFOF."vlrCap") + SUM(TINFOF."vlrInt"),0)as "TotDeudaCapInt"
      FROM (SELECT
      CASE WHEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendCa", 0) != 0
      THEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendCa",0)
      ELSE T1."U_Capital"
      END AS "vlrCap",
      CASE WHEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendInt",0) != 0
      THEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendInt",0)
      ELSE T1."U_Intereses"
      END AS "vlrInt"
      FROM "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" T1
      WHERE T1."DocEntry" = (SELECT
      "U_NroCredFinan"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD"
      WHERE "DocEntry" = '%NroPrenecesidad%')
      AND T1."U_NroTabla" = (SELECT
      MAX(T0."U_NroTabla")
      FROM "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" T0
      WHERE T0."DocEntry" =(SELECT"U_NroCredFinan"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD"
      WHERE "DocEntry" = '%NroPrenecesidad%'))
      AND IFNULL(T1."U_Pagado",'N') = 'N'
      ) AS TINFOF
    </Query>
    <Query ID="RetornarSaldoCarteraCuotasCreditoPrenecesidad">
      SELECT
      IFNULL(SUM(TINFOF."vlrCap") + SUM(TINFOF."vlrInt"),0)as "TotDeudaCapInt"
      FROM (SELECT
      CASE WHEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendCa", 0) != 0
      THEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendCa",0)
      ELSE T1."U_Capital"
      END AS "vlrCap",
      CASE WHEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendInt",0) != 0
      THEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendInt",0)
      ELSE T1."U_Intereses"
      END AS "vlrInt"
      FROM "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" T1
      WHERE T1."DocEntry" = (SELECT
      "U_NroCredFinan"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD"
      WHERE "DocEntry" = '%NroPrenecesidad%')
      AND T1."U_NroTabla" = (SELECT
      MAX(T0."U_NroTabla")
      FROM "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" T0
      WHERE T0."DocEntry" =(SELECT"U_NroCredFinan"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD"
      WHERE "DocEntry" = '%NroPrenecesidad%'))
      AND IFNULL(T1."U_Pagado",'N') = 'N'
      AND  ("U_FechPPag" &lt; CURRENT_DATE)) AS TINFOF
    </Query>
    <Query ID="retornarElementosmatrizSF">
      select  Distinct IFNULL("U_NoContr_Prenec",'')AS "U_NoContr_Prenec" from "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" where "DocEntry" =  '%DocEntry%'
    </Query>

    </Query>
    <Query ID="retornarTotalRecaudointeresCreditoPrenecesidad">
      SELECT
      *
      FROM "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" T1

      AND T1."U_NroTabla" = (SELECT MAX(T0."U_NroTabla") FROM "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" T0 WHERE T0."DocEntry" =(SELECT "U_NroCredFinan" FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD" WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntryPrenecesidad%'))
      ORDER BY T1."LineId"
    </Query>
    <Query ID="OLD_retornarTotalRecaudointeresCreditoPrenecesidad">

      INNER JOIN  "@OK1_FINAN_PAGO_LIN1" T1 ON T0."DocEntry" =T1."DocEntry"
      WHERE T0."U_EntrCred" = (SELECT "U_NroCredFinan" FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD" WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntryPrenecesidad%')
    </Query>

    <Query ID="retornarTotalRecaudointeresCreditoPrenecesidadPagados">
      SELECT IFNULL(Sum(T1."InteresPago"), 0) AS "TotalInteres"
      FROM   (SELECT T0."NroPago",
      IFNULL((SELECT "U_VlrPgCte"
      FROM   "@OK1_FINAN_PAGO_HEAD"
      WHERE  "DocNum" = T0."NroPago"), 0)AS "InteresPago"
      FROM   (SELECT DISTINCT ( "U_NroPagoAddin" ) AS "NroPago"
      FROM   "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" T1
      WHERE  T1."DocEntry" = (SELECT "U_NroCredFinan"
      FROM   "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD"
      WHERE  "DocEntry" = '%DocEntryPrenecesidad%')
      AND T1."U_NroTabla" = (SELECT Max(T0."U_NroTabla")
      FROM   "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" T0
      WHERE
      T0."DocEntry" = (SELECT "U_NroCredFinan"
      FROM   "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD"
      WHERE  "DocEntry" = '%DocEntryPrenecesidad%'))) T0) T1
    </Query>
    <Query ID="retornarLineasAFacturarPrenecesidadFromSF">
      SELECT
      T10."U_Codigo",
      T10."U_Nombre",
      T10."U_IdLinea",
      T10."U_IdLineaPF",
      T10."U_ItemCode",
      T10."U_Renuncia",
      IFNULL( T10."U_Cant",0),
      T10."U_cenCosto",
      T10."U_cenCosto1",
      T10."U_cenCosto2",
      T10."U_cenCosto3"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T10
      WHERE "DocEntry" =  '%DocEntrySF%'
      AND "U_NoContr_Prenec" = '%DocEntryPrenecesidad%'
      AND IFNULL( "U_LPagadaPrenec",'N') IN('N' ,'')
      AND IFNULL("U_tipLinea",'N') IN ( 'P', 'B' ,'','N')
      AND (SELECT "U_Cant" FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM" WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntryPrenecesidad%' AND "U_IdLinea" = T10."U_IdLineaPF")  >
      (
      SELECT  SUM("Cant") FROM (
      SELECT
      COUNT(T1."DocEntry") AS "Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T1 ON T0."DocEntry" =T1."DocEntry"
      WHERE T1."U_NoContr_Prenec" = '%DocEntryPrenecesidad%'
      AND "U_IdLineaPF" = T10."U_IdLineaPF"
      AND IFNULL( T1."U_LPagadaPrenec",'N') IN('Y' ,'')
      AND IFNULL(T1."U_tipLinea",'N') IN ( 'P','B' ,'')

      UNION ALL

      SELECT
      COUNT(T1."DocEntry") "Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_PQC_OPARELM_LIN" T1 ON T0."DocEntry" =T1."DocEntry"
      WHERE T1."U_NoContr_Prenec" = '%DocEntryPrenecesidad%'
      AND "U_IdLineaPF" = T10."U_IdLineaPF"
      AND IFNULL( T1."U_LPagadaPrenec",'N') IN('Y' ,'')
      AND IFNULL(T1."U_tipLinea",'N') IN ( 'P','B' ,'')
      AND IFNULL("U_LineaSF",'0') = '0' )
      )
      ORDER BY  T10."U_IdLineaPF"
    </Query>
    <Query ID="old__retornarLineasAFacturarPrenecesidadFromSF">
      SELECT
      T10."U_Codigo",
      T10."U_Nombre",
      T10."U_IdLinea",
      T10."U_IdLineaPF",
      T10."U_ItemCode",

      T10."U_Renuncia"

      FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T10
      WHERE "DocEntry" =  '%DocEntrySF%'
      AND "U_NoContr_Prenec" = '%DocEntryPrenecesidad%'
      AND IFNULL( "U_LPagadaPrenec",'N') IN('N' ,'')
      AND IFNULL("U_tipLinea",'N') IN ( 'P', 'B' ,'','N')
      AND  "U_IdLineaPF"  NOT IN
      (
      SELECT
      T1."U_IdLineaPF"
      FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" T0 INNER JOIN
      "@OK1_PQC_OPARELM_LIN" T1
      ON T0."DocEntry" =T1."DocEntry"
      WHERE  T0."U_NoOSF" = '%DocEntrySF%'
      AND  T1."U_NoContr_Prenec" = '%DocEntryPrenecesidad%'
      AND  "U_IdLineaPF" = T10."U_IdLineaPF"
      AND IFNULL( T1."U_LPagadaPrenec",'N') IN('Y' ,'')
      AND IFNULL( "U_tipLinea",'')  NOT IN( 'H', 'C' )
      )
      ORDER BY  T10."U_IdLineaPF"
    </Query>

    <Query ID="OLD_retornarLineasAFacturarPrenecesidadFromSF">
      SELECT
      "U_Codigo",
      "U_Nombre",
      "U_IdLinea",
      "U_IdLineaPF",
      "U_ItemCode"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
      WHERE "DocEntry" =  '%DocEntrySF%'
      AND "U_NoContr_Prenec" = '%DocEntryPrenecesidad%'
      <!--AND "U_LPagadaPrenec" = 'N'
      AND ("U_tipLinea" = 'P' OR "U_tipLinea" = 'B' OR IFNULL("U_tipLinea",'') = '')-->
      AND IFNULL( "U_LPagadaPrenec",'N') IN('N' ,'')
      AND IFNULL("U_tipLinea",'N') IN ( 'P', 'B' ,'')

      ORDER BY  "U_IdLineaPF"
    </Query>
    <Query ID="retornarInfoElementoPrenecesidadTOSF">
      select
      T2."U_Cant",
      T1."U_vlrUniDesc",
      T1."U_CantDispo",
      T1."U_VlrDto",
      T1."U_Cant",
      T1."U_totalLinea",
      T2."U_CantDispo",
	  T1."U_CantDGP"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM" T1
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T2
      ON  CAST(T1."DocEntry" AS NVARCHAR) = T2."U_NoContr_Prenec"
      AND   T1."U_IdLinea" = T2."U_IdLineaPF"
      WHERE T1."DocEntry" = '%DocEntryPrenecesidad%'
      AND T1."U_Codigo" = '%CodigoElemento%'
      AND T1."U_IdLinea" = '%LineaPreneF%'
      AND T2."DocEntry" = '%DocEntrySF%'

    </Query>
    <Query ID="old_retornarInfoElementoPrenecesidadTOSF">
      select
      T1."U_Cant",
      T1."U_vlrUniDesc",
      T1."U_CantDispo"
      FROM  "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM" T1
      WHERE T1."DocEntry" = '%DocEntryPrenecesidad%'
      AND T1."U_Codigo" = '%CodigoElemento%'
      AND T1."U_IdLinea" = '%LineaPreneF%'

    </Query>
    <Query ID="ActualizarlineaMatrizElementosSF">
      UPDATE "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
      SET "U_LPagadaPrenec" = 'Y',
      "U_NoFacturaPrenec" = '%DocEntryFactura%'
      WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntrySF%'
      AND "U_IdLinea" = '%U_IdLinea%'
    </Query>

    <Query ID="ActualizarlineaMatrizElementosSFHijosFP">
      UPDATE "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
      SET "U_LPagadaPrenec" = 'Y',
      "U_NoFacturaPrenec" = '%DocEntryFactura%'
      WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntrySF%'
      AND "U_NoContr_Prenec"  = '%U_NoContr_Prenec%'
      AND "U_tipLinea" IN ('C','H')
    </Query>

    <Query ID="ConsultarlineaMatrizElementosServicioFunerario">
      SELECT
      T10."U_IdLineaPF",
      T10."U_Codigo",
      T10."U_NoContr_Prenec"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T10
      WHERE "DocEntry" =  '%DocEntrySF%'
      and   T10."U_IdLinea" ='%U_IdLinea%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="ActualizarlineaMatrizElementosParqueCementerio">
      UPDATE "@OK1_PQC_OPARELM_LIN" T0
      SET T0."U_LPagadaPrenec" = 'Y',
      T0."U_NoFacturaPrenec" = '%DocEntryFactura%'
      FROM  "@OK1_PQC_OPARELM_LIN" T0 INNER JOIN "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" T1 ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
      WHERE T1."U_NoOSF" = '%DocEntrySF%'
      AND T0."U_IdLineaPF" = '%U_IdLineaPF%'
      AND T0."U_NoContr_Prenec" = '%U_NoContr_Prenec%'
      AND T0."U_Codigo" = '%U_Codigo%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="LimpiarlineaMatrizElementosParqueCementerio">
      UPDATE "@OK1_PQC_OPARELM_LIN" T0
      SET
      T0."U_NoFacturaPrenec" = '',
      T0."U_LPagadaPrenec" = 'N'
      FROM  "@OK1_PQC_OPARELM_LIN" T0 INNER JOIN "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" T1 ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
      WHERE T1."U_NoOSF" = '%DocEntrySF%'
      AND T0."U_IdLineaPF" = '%U_IdLineaPF%'
      AND T0."U_NoContr_Prenec" = '%U_NoContr_Prenec%'
      AND T0."U_Codigo" = '%U_Codigo%'
      AND T0."U_NoFacturaPrenec" = '%DocEntryFactura%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="ActualizarlineaMatrizFacturaPrenecidad">
      UPDATE "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
      SET "U_NoFacturaPrenec" = '%DocEntryFactura%'
      WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntrySF%'
      AND "U_IdLinea" = '%U_IdLinea%'
    </Query>

    <Query ID="RetornarCantidadComponentesUsadosPaquetePrenecesidad">
      SELECT
      (
      SELECT COUNT ("DocEntry")
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM"
      WHERE "DocEntry" = '%NroPrenecesidad%'
      AND  "U_IdLineaPB" = '%NroLinea%'
      AND "U_tipLinea" = 'C'
      )
      -
      (
      SELECT COUNT ("DocEntry")
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM"
      WHERE "DocEntry" = '%NroPrenecesidad%'
      AND  "U_IdLineaPB" = '%NroLinea%'
      AND "U_tipLinea" = 'C'
      AND "U_CantDispo" = 0
      )AS "Cant"
      FROM  DUMMY
    </Query>

    <Query ID="RetornarCantidadElementosPaqueteXServicioPRF">
      SELECT SUM("U_Cant")
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM"
      WHERE "DocEntry" =  '%NroPrenecesidad%'
      AND  "U_IdLineaPB" = '%NroLinea%'
      AND "U_tipLinea" = 'C'
    </Query>

    <Query ID="RetornarCantidaElementosHijoPRF">
      SELECT COUNT("U_tipLinea") AS "cantidaElementosHijo"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM"
      WHERE "DocEntry" =  '%NroPrenecesidad%'
      AND  "U_IdLineaPB" = '%NroLinea%'
      AND "U_tipLinea" = 'C'
    </Query>

    </Query>

    </Query>

    </Query>

    <Query ID="ReponerCantidaDisponiblePadrePaquetePrenecesidad">
      UPDATE  "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM"
      SET "U_CantDispo" =  "U_CantDispo"+1
      WHERE "DocEntry" = '%NroPrenecesidad%'
      AND  "U_IdLinea" = '%NroLinea%'
    </Query>

    <Query ID="ActualizarCancelarDesistePadrePaquetePrenecesidad">
      UPDATE  "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM"
      SET "U_desiste" = 'N'
      WHERE "DocEntry" = '%NroPrenecesidad%'
      AND  "U_IdLinea" = '%NroLinea%'
    </Query>

      AND  "U_Codigo"='%IdElemento%'
      AND "U_IdLinea" = '%NroLinea%'
    </Query>

    <Query ID="DescontarElementodisponiblePrenecesidad">
      UPDATE "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM"
      SET  "U_CantDispo"= "U_CantDispo"- %Cantidad%,
      "U_EntryDocSF" = '%DocEntrySF%'
      WHERE "DocEntry"='%NroPrenecesidad%'
      AND "U_IdLinea" = '%NroLinea%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="SumarFromDescuentoElementodisponiblePrenecesidad">
      UPDATE "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM"
      SET  "U_CantDispo"= "U_CantDispo" + %Cantidad%,
      "U_EntryDocSF" = ''
      WHERE "DocEntry"='%NroPrenecesidad%'
      AND "U_IdLinea" = '%NroLinea%'
    </Query>

    <Query ID="RetornarElementosCedidosPendientesDeUsoFromSF">
      SELECT
      T0."DocNum" AS "NroSolicitud",
      T1."U_Cant" AS "CantSolicitada",
      T1."U_IdLinea" AS "Linea",
      T1."U_Codigo" AS "Elemento"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T1 ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
      WHERE T0."Canceled" = 'N'
      AND  T0."U_Estado" = '1'
      AND T1. "U_Codigo" =  '%IdElemento%'
      AND T0."DocEntry" &lt;&gt; '%DocEntrySF%'
      AND T1."U_NoContr_Prenec" = '%NroPrenecesidad%'
      AND T1."U_Uso"='Y'
      ORDER  BY T0."DocNum"
    </Query>
    <Query ID="EliminarUsoTablaPrenecesidadHistorico">
      DELETE FROM   "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HIST"
      WHERE "U_IdOrden"  ='%DocEntrySF%'
      AND  "U_Codigo" = '%ElementoID%'
      AND   "U_NroLineaPF" = %linea%
    </Query>
    <Query ID="RetonarSedeSF">
      SELECT "U_VSede" FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD"
      WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntrySF%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="ExistenActividadesWithIgualOrigenDestinoForTraslado">
      SELECT
      COUNT (IFNULL(T0."ClgCode",0)) AS "Código"
      FROM OCLG T0
      INNER JOIN OUSR T1 ON T0."AttendUser" = T1.USERID
      WHERE T0."U_DocEntry" = '%DocEntrySF%'
      AND "CntctType" = (SELECT "Code" FROM OCLT WHERE "U_tipoAct" = 'T')
      AND  IFNULL (T0."U_tipoTraslOrg",'') = '%Origen%'
      AND  IFNULL (T0."U_tipoTraslDes",'') = '%Destino%'
      AND  IFNULL (T0."U_rechaz",'N') = 'N'
      AND  IFNULL (T0."U_DocEntry",'') = '%DocEntrySF%'
      AND T0."Closed" != 'Y'
    </Query>

    <Query ID="ExistenActividadesWithIgualTipoForLaboratorio">
      SELECT
      COUNT (IFNULL(T0."ClgCode",0)) AS "Código"
      FROM OCLG T0
      INNER JOIN OUSR T1 ON T0."AttendUser" = T1.USERID
      WHERE T0."U_DocEntry" = '%DocEntrySF%'
      AND "CntctType" = (SELECT "Code" FROM OCLT WHERE "U_tipoAct" = 'L')
      AND  IFNULL (T0."CntctSbjct",0) = '%asunto%'
      AND  IFNULL (T0."U_tipoSolic",'') = '%tipo%'
      AND  IFNULL (T0."U_DocEntry",'') ='%DocEntrySF%'
      AND T0."Closed" != 'Y'
    </Query>

    <Query ID="Retornar_IdTipo_Traslado">
      SELECT "Code" FROM OCLT WHERE "U_tipoAct" = 'T'
    </Query>

    <Query ID="Retornar_IdTipo_Traslado_laboratorio">
