    </Query>

    <Query ID="Retornar_IdTipo_Traslado_Parque">
      SELECT "Code" FROM OCLT WHERE "U_tipoAct" = 'P'
    </Query>

    <Query ID="okkRetornar_PrenecesidadesQuePuedenCederElementos">
      SELECT
      T4."DocEntry" AS "Prenecesidad",
      T4."DocEntry" AS "NoInterno",
      T5."U_contrant" || '-' || T5."U_ncontrat" AS "Contratante",
      T4."U_Codigo" AS "Elemento",
      T4."U_Nombre" AS "Nombre Elemento",
      T4."U_ItemCode" AS "Artículo",
      T4."U_ItemName" AS "Descripción",
      T4."U_CantDispo" AS "Cantidad",
      CASE WHEN T4. "U_CantDispo" &lt; T4."U_CantDGP"  THEN T4. "U_CantDispo" ELSE  T4."U_CantDGP" END  AS "Cantidad Por Servicio",
      T4."U_IdLinea" AS "Linea",
      T4."U_Linea" AS "Linea Elemento",
      T4."U_LineaName" AS "Linea Elemento Nombre",

      (  SELECT
      IFNULL(Sum(TINFOF."vlrCap") + Sum(TINFOF."vlrInt"),0)as "TotDeudaCapInt"
      FROM

      (
      SELECT
      CASE WHEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendCa",0) != 0 THEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendCa", 0) ELSE T1."U_Capital" END AS "vlrCap",
      CASE WHEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendInt",0) != 0 THEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendInt",0) ELSE T1."U_Intereses" END AS "vlrInt"
      FROM "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" T1
      WHERE T1."DocEntry" =

      (
      SELECT "U_NroCredFinan"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD"
      WHERE "DocEntry" = T5."DocEntry") AND T1."U_NroTabla" =
      (
      SELECT Max(T0."U_NroTabla")
      FROM "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" T0
      WHERE T0."DocEntry" =

      (
      SELECT"U_NroCredFinan"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD"
      WHERE "DocEntry" = T5."DocEntry"
      )
      )
      AND IFNULL(T1."U_Pagado",'N') = 'N'
      ) AS TINFOF
      )AS "SaldoPendiente",

      T4. "U_CantDispo" AS "Cantidad Disponible",

      (

      select
      sum(TF."U_Cant") AS "CANSF"
      FROM (

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T11. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T10
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T11 ON T10."DocEntry" = T11."DocEntry"
      WHERE T10."Canceled" = 'N'


      AND  ( T10."U_Estado" NOT IN('8','3'))
      AND T10."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOrdenSF" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T10."U_VSede")
      AND T11. "U_Codigo" = T4."U_Codigo"
      AND T11."U_IdLineaPF" = T4."U_IdLinea"
      AND T11."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T5."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND (T11."U_tipLinea" = 'P' OR T11."U_tipLinea" = 'B' OR IFNULL(T11."U_tipLinea",'') = '')
      AND ( IFNULL(T11."U_Uso",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T11."U_Uso",'') = '' )

      union all

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T8. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" T7
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_PQC_OPARELM_LIN" T8 ON T7."DocEntry" = T8."DocEntry"
      WHERE T7."Canceled" = 'N'
      AND ( T7."U_Estado" NOT IN('08','03'))
      AND T7."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOP" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T7."U_SedeParque")

      AND T8. "U_Codigo" = T4."U_Codigo"
      AND T8."U_IdLineaPF" = T4."U_IdLinea"
      AND T8."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T5."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND (T8."U_tipLinea" = 'P' OR T8."U_tipLinea" = 'B'
      OR IFNULL(T8."U_tipLinea",'') = '') AND ( IFNULL(T8."U_Uso",'N') = 'N'
      OR IFNULL(T8."U_Uso",'') = '' ) )AS TF

      ) AS "Cantidad Usos",
      T4."U_IdLineaPB" AS "LineaPadrePN",
      T4."U_tipLinea" AS "TipoLinea",
      'N' AS "Seleccionar"


      FROM   "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM" T4
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD" T5
      ON T4."DocEntry" = T5."DocEntry"
      WHERE  T4."U_CantDispo" > 0

      AND T5."U_estado" = 'PREABIFIN'

      AND (  SELECT
      IFNULL(Sum(TINFOF."vlrCap") + Sum(TINFOF."vlrInt"),0)as "TotDeudaCapInt"
      FROM

      (
      SELECT
      CASE WHEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendCa",0) != 0 THEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendCa", 0) ELSE T1."U_Capital" END AS "vlrCap",
      CASE WHEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendInt",0) != 0 THEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendInt",0) ELSE T1."U_Intereses" END AS "vlrInt"
      FROM "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" T1
      WHERE T1."DocEntry" =

      (
      SELECT "U_NroCredFinan"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD"
      WHERE "DocEntry" = T5."DocEntry") AND T1."U_NroTabla" =
      (
      SELECT Max(T0."U_NroTabla")
      FROM "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" T0
      WHERE T0."DocEntry" =

      (
      SELECT"U_NroCredFinan"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD"
      WHERE "DocEntry" = T5."DocEntry"
      )
      )
      AND IFNULL(T1."U_Pagado",'N') = 'N'
      ) AS TINFOF
      ) &gt;= 0

      AND (
      IFNULL( T4."U_Uso",'N') &lt;&gt; 'Y'
      AND

      T4."U_CantDispo" >
      (

      select
      sum(TF."U_Cant") AS "CANSF"
      FROM (

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T11. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T10
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T11 ON T10."DocEntry" = T11."DocEntry"
      WHERE T10."Canceled" = 'N'

      AND       ( T10."U_Estado" NOT IN(8,3,10))
      AND T10."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOrdenSF" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T10."U_VSede")
      AND T11. "U_Codigo" = T4."U_Codigo"
      AND T11."U_IdLineaPF" = T4."U_IdLinea"
      AND T11."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T5."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND (T11."U_tipLinea" = 'P' OR T11."U_tipLinea" = 'B' OR IFNULL(T11."U_tipLinea",'') = '')
      AND ( IFNULL(T11."U_Uso",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T11."U_Uso",'') = '' )

      union all

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T8. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" T7
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_PQC_OPARELM_LIN" T8 ON T7."DocEntry" = T8."DocEntry"
      WHERE T7."Canceled" = 'N'

      AND ( T7."U_Estado" NOT IN('08','03'))
      AND T7."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOP" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T7."U_SedeParque")

      AND T8. "U_Codigo" = T4."U_Codigo"
      AND T8."U_IdLineaPF" = T4."U_IdLinea"
      AND T8."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T5."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND (T8."U_tipLinea" = 'P' OR T8."U_tipLinea" = 'B'
      OR IFNULL(T8."U_tipLinea",'') = '') AND ( IFNULL(T8."U_Uso",'N') = 'N'
      OR IFNULL(T8."U_Uso",'') = '' )

      )AS TF

      ))
      AND IFNULL(T4."U_tipLinea",'') != 'C'
      <!--ORDER BY T4."DocEntry"-->
    </Query>

    <Query ID="Retornar_Cantidad_PrenecesidadesQuePuedenCederElementos">
      SELECT
      Count (T4."DocEntry") as "cant"
      FROM   "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM" T4
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD" T5
      ON T4."DocEntry" = T5."DocEntry"
      WHERE  T4."U_CantDispo" > 0

      AND T5."U_estado" = 'PREABIFIN'

      AND (  SELECT
      IFNULL(Sum(TINFOF."vlrCap") + Sum(TINFOF."vlrInt"),0)as "TotDeudaCapInt"
      FROM

      (
      SELECT
      CASE WHEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendCa",0) != 0 THEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendCa", 0) ELSE T1."U_Capital" END AS "vlrCap",
      CASE WHEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendInt",0) != 0 THEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendInt",0) ELSE T1."U_Intereses" END AS "vlrInt"
      FROM "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" T1
      WHERE T1."DocEntry" =

      (
      SELECT "U_NroCredFinan"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD"
      WHERE "DocEntry" = T5."DocEntry") AND T1."U_NroTabla" =
      (
      SELECT Max(T0."U_NroTabla")
      FROM "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" T0
      WHERE T0."DocEntry" =

      (
      SELECT"U_NroCredFinan"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD"
      WHERE "DocEntry" = T5."DocEntry"
      )
      )
      AND IFNULL(T1."U_Pagado",'N') = 'N'
      ) AS TINFOF
      ) &gt;= 0

      AND (
      IFNULL( T4."U_Uso",'N') &lt;&gt; 'Y'
      AND

      T4."U_CantDispo" >
      (

      select
      sum(TF."U_Cant") AS "CANSF"
      FROM (

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T11. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T10
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T11 ON T10."DocEntry" = T11."DocEntry"
      WHERE T10."Canceled" = 'N'

      AND       ( T10."U_Estado" NOT IN(8,3,10))
      AND T10."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOrdenSF" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T10."U_VSede")
      AND T11. "U_Codigo" = T4."U_Codigo"
      AND T11."U_IdLineaPF" = T4."U_IdLinea"
      AND T11."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T5."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND (T11."U_tipLinea" = 'P' OR T11."U_tipLinea" = 'B' OR IFNULL(T11."U_tipLinea",'') = '')
      AND ( IFNULL(T11."U_Uso",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T11."U_Uso",'') = '' )
      AND ( IFNULL(T11."U_Renuncia",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T11."U_Renuncia",'') = '' )

      union all

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T8. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" T7
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_PQC_OPARELM_LIN" T8 ON T7."DocEntry" = T8."DocEntry"
      WHERE T7."Canceled" = 'N'

      AND ( T7."U_Estado" NOT IN('08','03'))
      AND T7."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOP" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T7."U_SedeParque")

      AND T8. "U_Codigo" = T4."U_Codigo"
      AND T8."U_IdLineaPF" = T4."U_IdLinea"
      AND T8."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T5."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND (T8."U_tipLinea" = 'P' OR T8."U_tipLinea" = 'B' OR IFNULL(T8."U_tipLinea",'') = '')
      AND ( IFNULL(T8."U_Uso",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T8."U_Uso",'') = '' )
      AND ( IFNULL(T8."U_Renuncia",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T8."U_Renuncia",'') = '' )

      )AS TF

      ))
      AND IFNULL(T4."U_tipLinea",'') != 'C'
      <!--ORDER BY T4."DocEntry"-->
    </Query>

    <Query ID="Retornar_PrenecesidadesQuePuedenCederElementos">
      SELECT
      T4."DocEntry" AS "Prenecesidad",
      T4."DocEntry" AS "NoInterno",
      T5."U_contrant" || '-' || T5."U_ncontrat" AS "Contratante",
      T4."U_Codigo" AS "Elemento",
      T4."U_Nombre" AS "Nombre Elemento",
      T4."U_ItemCode" AS "Artículo",
      T4."U_ItemName" AS "Descripción",
      T4."U_CantDispo" AS "Cantidad",
      CASE WHEN T4. "U_CantDispo" &lt; T4."U_CantDGP"  THEN T4. "U_CantDispo" ELSE  IFNULL(T4."U_CantDGP", T4."U_CantDispo") END  AS "Cantidad Por Servicio",
      T4."U_IdLinea" AS "Linea",
      T4."U_Linea" AS "Linea Elemento",
      T4."U_LineaName" AS "Linea Elemento Nombre",

      (  SELECT
      IFNULL(Sum(TINFOF."vlrCap") + Sum(TINFOF."vlrInt"),0)as "TotDeudaCapInt"
      FROM

      (
      SELECT
      CASE WHEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendCa",0) != 0 THEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendCa", 0) ELSE T1."U_Capital" END AS "vlrCap",
      CASE WHEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendInt",0) != 0 THEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendInt",0) ELSE T1."U_Intereses" END AS "vlrInt"
      FROM "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" T1
      WHERE T1."DocEntry" =

      (
      SELECT "U_NroCredFinan"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD"
      WHERE "DocEntry" = T5."DocEntry") AND T1."U_NroTabla" =
      (
      SELECT Max(T0."U_NroTabla")
      FROM "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" T0
      WHERE T0."DocEntry" =

      (
      SELECT"U_NroCredFinan"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD"
      WHERE "DocEntry" = T5."DocEntry"
      )
      )
      AND IFNULL(T1."U_Pagado",'N') = 'N'
      ) AS TINFOF
      )AS "SaldoPendiente",

      T4."U_Cant" AS "Cantidad Total",

      T4. "U_CantDispo" AS "Cantidad Pendiente",

      (

      select
      sum(TF."U_Cant") AS "CANSF"
      FROM (

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T11. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T10
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T11 ON T10."DocEntry" = T11."DocEntry"
      WHERE T10."Canceled" = 'N'


      AND  ( T10."U_Estado" NOT IN('8','3'))
      AND T10."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOrdenSF" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T10."U_VSede")
      AND T11. "U_Codigo" = T4."U_Codigo"
      AND T11."U_IdLineaPF" = T4."U_IdLinea"
      AND T11."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T5."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND (T11."U_tipLinea" = 'P' OR T11."U_tipLinea" = 'B' OR IFNULL(T11."U_tipLinea",'') = '')
      AND ( IFNULL(T11."U_Uso",'N') = 'Y')

      union all

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T8. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" T7
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_PQC_OPARELM_LIN" T8 ON T7."DocEntry" = T8."DocEntry"
      WHERE T7."Canceled" = 'N'
      AND ( T7."U_Estado" NOT IN('08','03'))
      AND T7."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOP" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T7."U_SedeParque")

      AND T8. "U_Codigo" = T4."U_Codigo"
      AND T8."U_IdLineaPF" = T4."U_IdLinea"
      AND T8."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T5."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND (T8."U_tipLinea" = 'P' OR T8."U_tipLinea" = 'B'
      OR IFNULL(T8."U_tipLinea",'') = '') 
      AND ( IFNULL(T8."U_Uso",'N') = 'Y') )AS TF

      ) AS "Cantidad Usos",

      (

      select
      sum(TF."U_Cant") AS "CANSF"
      FROM (

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T11. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T10
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T11 ON T10."DocEntry" = T11."DocEntry"
      WHERE T10."Canceled" = 'N'


      AND       ( T10."U_Estado" NOT IN('8','3'))
      AND T10."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOrdenSF" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T10."U_VSede")
      AND T11. "U_Codigo" = T4."U_Codigo"
      AND T11."U_IdLineaPF" = T4."U_IdLinea"
      AND T11."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T5."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND (T11."U_tipLinea" = 'P' OR T11."U_tipLinea" = 'B' OR IFNULL(T11."U_tipLinea",'') = '')
      AND ( IFNULL(T11."U_Uso",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T11."U_Uso",'') = '' )
 	    AND ( IFNULL(T11."U_Renuncia", 'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T11."U_Renuncia", '') = '' ) 
 	  
      union all

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T8. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" T7
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_PQC_OPARELM_LIN" T8 ON T7."DocEntry" = T8."DocEntry"
      WHERE T7."Canceled" = 'N'

      AND ( T7."U_Estado" NOT IN('08','03'))
      AND T7."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOP" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T7."U_SedeParque")

      AND T8. "U_Codigo" = T4."U_Codigo"
      AND T8."U_IdLineaPF" = T4."U_IdLinea"
      AND T8."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T5."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND (T8."U_tipLinea" = 'P' OR T8."U_tipLinea" = 'B'
      OR IFNULL(T8."U_tipLinea",'') = '') 
      AND ( IFNULL(T8."U_Uso",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T8."U_Uso",'') = '' ) 
      AND ( IFNULL(T8."U_Renuncia", 'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T8."U_Renuncia", '') = '' )
      )AS TF

      ) AS "Cantidad Comprometidos",

      T4."U_IdLineaPB" AS "LineaPadrePN",
      T4."U_tipLinea" AS "TipoLinea",

          (

      select
      sum(TF."U_Cant") AS "CANSF"
      FROM (

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T11. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T10
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T11 ON T10."DocEntry" = T11."DocEntry"
      WHERE T10."Canceled" = 'N'


      AND  ( T10."U_Estado" NOT IN('8','3'))
      AND T10."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOrdenSF" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T10."U_VSede")
      AND T11. "U_Codigo" = T4."U_Codigo"
      AND T11."U_IdLineaPF" = T4."U_IdLinea"
      AND T11."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T5."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND (T11."U_tipLinea" = 'P' OR T11."U_tipLinea" = 'B' OR IFNULL(T11."U_tipLinea",'') = '')
      AND ( IFNULL(T11."U_Renuncia",'N') = 'Y')

      union all

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T8. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" T7
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_PQC_OPARELM_LIN" T8 ON T7."DocEntry" = T8."DocEntry"
      WHERE T7."Canceled" = 'N'
      AND ( T7."U_Estado" NOT IN('08','03'))
      AND T7."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOP" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T7."U_SedeParque")

      AND T8. "U_Codigo" = T4."U_Codigo"
      AND T8."U_IdLineaPF" = T4."U_IdLinea"
      AND T8."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T5."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND (T8."U_tipLinea" = 'P' OR T8."U_tipLinea" = 'B'
      OR IFNULL(T8."U_tipLinea",'') = '') 
      AND ( IFNULL(T8."U_Renuncia",'N') = 'Y') )AS TF

      ) AS "Cantidad Desistidos",

      'N' AS "Seleccionar"


      FROM   "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM" T4
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD" T5
      ON T4."DocEntry" = T5."DocEntry"
      WHERE  T4."U_CantDispo" > 0

      AND T5."U_estado" = 'PREABIFIN'

      AND (  SELECT
      IFNULL(Sum(TINFOF."vlrCap") + Sum(TINFOF."vlrInt"),0)as "TotDeudaCapInt"
      FROM

      (
      SELECT
      CASE WHEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendCa",0) != 0 THEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendCa", 0) ELSE T1."U_Capital" END AS "vlrCap",
      CASE WHEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendInt",0) != 0 THEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendInt",0) ELSE T1."U_Intereses" END AS "vlrInt"
      FROM "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" T1
      WHERE T1."DocEntry" =

      (
      SELECT "U_NroCredFinan"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD"
      WHERE "DocEntry" = T5."DocEntry") AND T1."U_NroTabla" =
      (
      SELECT Max(T0."U_NroTabla")
      FROM "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" T0
      WHERE T0."DocEntry" =

      (
      SELECT"U_NroCredFinan"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD"
      WHERE "DocEntry" = T5."DocEntry"
      )
      )
      AND IFNULL(T1."U_Pagado",'N') = 'N'
      ) AS TINFOF
      ) &gt;= 0

      AND (
      IFNULL( T4."U_Uso",'N') &lt;&gt; 'Y'
      AND

      T4."U_CantDispo" >
      (

      select
      sum(TF."U_Cant") AS "CANSF"
      FROM (

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T11. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T10
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T11 ON T10."DocEntry" = T11."DocEntry"
      WHERE T10."Canceled" = 'N'

      AND       ( T10."U_Estado" NOT IN(8,3,10))
      AND T10."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOrdenSF" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T10."U_VSede")
      AND T11. "U_Codigo" = T4."U_Codigo"
      AND T11."U_IdLineaPF" = T4."U_IdLinea"
      AND T11."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T5."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND (T11."U_tipLinea" = 'P' OR T11."U_tipLinea" = 'B' OR IFNULL(T11."U_tipLinea",'') = '')
      AND ( IFNULL(T11."U_Uso",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T11."U_Uso",'') = '' )
      AND ( IFNULL(T11."U_Renuncia",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T11."U_Renuncia",'') = '' )

      union all

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T8. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" T7
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_PQC_OPARELM_LIN" T8 ON T7."DocEntry" = T8."DocEntry"
      WHERE T7."Canceled" = 'N'

      AND ( T7."U_Estado" NOT IN('08','03'))
      AND T7."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOP" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T7."U_SedeParque")

      AND T8. "U_Codigo" = T4."U_Codigo"
      AND T8."U_IdLineaPF" = T4."U_IdLinea"
      AND T8."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T5."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND (T8."U_tipLinea" = 'P' OR T8."U_tipLinea" = 'B'
      OR IFNULL(T8."U_tipLinea",'') = '')
      AND ( IFNULL(T8."U_Uso",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T8."U_Uso",'') = '' )
      AND ( IFNULL(T8."U_Renuncia",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T8."U_Renuncia",'') = '' )

      )AS TF

      ))
      AND IFNULL(T4."U_tipLinea",'') != 'C'
      <!--ORDER BY T4."DocEntry"-->
    </Query>

    <Query ID="Retornar_ComponentesNoUsadosEnPrenecesidad">

      T4."U_IdLinea" AS "Linea",
      T4."U_Linea" AS "Linea Elemento",
      T4."U_LineaName" AS "Linea Elemento Nombre",

      (  SELECT
      IFNULL(Sum(TINFOF."vlrCap") + Sum(TINFOF."vlrInt"),0)as "TotDeudaCapInt"
      FROM

      AND ( IFNULL(T11."U_Uso",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T11."U_Uso",'') = '' )

      union all

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T8. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" T7
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_PQC_OPARELM_LIN" T8 ON T7."DocEntry" = T8."DocEntry"
      WHERE T7."Canceled" = 'N'

      AND ( T7."U_Estado" NOT IN('08','03'))
      AND T7."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOP" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T7."U_SedeParque")

      AND T8. "U_Codigo" = T4."U_Codigo"
      AND T8."U_IdLineaPF" = T4."U_IdLinea"
      AND T8."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T5."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND (T8."U_tipLinea" = 'P' OR T8."U_tipLinea" = 'B'
      OR IFNULL(T8."U_tipLinea",'') = '') AND ( IFNULL(T8."U_Uso",'N') = 'N'
      OR IFNULL(T8."U_Uso",'') = '' )

      )AS TF

      ))
      AND T4."U_tipLinea" = 'C'

    </Query>

    <Query ID="ULT_Retornar_ComponentesNoUsadosEnPrenecesidad">
      SELECT
      T4."DocEntry" AS "Prenecesidad",
      T4."DocEntry" AS "NoInterno",
      T5."U_contrant" || '-' || T5."U_ncontrat" AS "Contratante",
      T4."U_Codigo" AS "Elemento",
      T4."U_Nombre" AS "Nombre Elemento",
      T4."U_ItemCode" AS "Artículo",
      T4."U_ItemName" AS "Descripción",
      T4."U_CantDispo" AS "Cantidad",
      T4."U_IdLinea" AS "Linea",
      T4."U_Linea" AS "Linea Elemento",
      T4."U_LineaName" AS "Linea Elemento Nombre",

      (  SELECT
      IFNULL(Sum(TINFOF."vlrCap") + Sum(TINFOF."vlrInt"),0)as "TotDeudaCapInt"
      FROM

      (
      SELECT
      CASE WHEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendCa",0) != 0 THEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendCa", 0) ELSE T1."U_Capital" END AS "vlrCap",
      CASE WHEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendInt",0) != 0 THEN IFNULL(T1."U_VlPendInt",0) ELSE T1."U_Intereses" END AS "vlrInt"
      FROM "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" T1
      WHERE T1."DocEntry" =

      (
      SELECT "U_NroCredFinan"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD"
      WHERE "DocEntry" = T5."DocEntry") AND T1."U_NroTabla" =
      (
      SELECT Max(T0."U_NroTabla")
      FROM "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" T0
      WHERE T0."DocEntry" =

      (
      SELECT"U_NroCredFinan"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD"
      WHERE "DocEntry" = T5."DocEntry"
      )
      )
      AND IFNULL(T1."U_Pagado",'N') = 'N'
      ) AS TINFOF
      )AS "SaldoPendiente",

      T4."U_Cant" AS "Cantidad",

      T4."U_CantDispo" AS "Cantidad Disponible",

      (

      select
      sum(TF."U_Cant") AS "CANSF"
      FROM (

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T11. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T10
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T11 ON T10."DocEntry" = T11."DocEntry"
      WHERE T10."Canceled" = 'N'


      AND       ( T10."U_Estado" NOT IN('8','3'))
      AND T10."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOrdenSF" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T10."U_VSede")
      AND T11. "U_Codigo" = T4."U_Codigo"
      AND T11."U_IdLineaPF" = T4."U_IdLinea"
      AND T11."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T5."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND (T11."U_tipLinea" = 'P' OR T11."U_tipLinea" = 'B' OR IFNULL(T11."U_tipLinea",'') = '')
      AND ( IFNULL(T11."U_Uso",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T11."U_Uso",'') = '' )

      union all

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T8. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" T7
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_PQC_OPARELM_LIN" T8 ON T7."DocEntry" = T8."DocEntry"
      WHERE T7."Canceled" = 'N'

      AND ( T7."U_Estado" NOT IN('08','03'))
      AND T7."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOP" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T7."U_SedeParque")

      AND T8. "U_Codigo" = T4."U_Codigo"
      AND T8."U_IdLineaPF" = T4."U_IdLinea"
      AND T8."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T5."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND (T8."U_tipLinea" = 'P' OR T8."U_tipLinea" = 'B'
      OR IFNULL(T8."U_tipLinea",'') = '') AND ( IFNULL(T8."U_Uso",'N') = 'N'
      OR IFNULL(T8."U_Uso",'') = '' ) )AS TF

      ) AS "Cantidad Comprometidos",

      T4."U_IdLineaPB" AS "LineaPadrePN",
      T4."U_tipLinea" AS "TipoLinea",

      (SELECT COUNT("LineId")
      FROM(
      SELECT DISTINCT "LineId"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HIST" T0
      INNER JOIN OUSR T1
      ON T1."USERID" = T0."U_IdUsuario"
      WHERE "DocEntry" = T4."DocEntry" ))AS "Cantidad Usos",

      (SELECT COUNT("U_IdOrden")
      FROM
      "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HDES" T0  INNER JOIN OUSR T1 ON T1."USERID" = T0."U_IdUsuario"
      WHERE
      "DocEntry" = T4."DocEntry"
      AND "U_NroLineaPF" in ( SELECT "U_IdLinea" FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM" T0 WHERE "DocEntry" =  T4."DocEntry" ))AS "Cantidad Desistidos",

      'N' AS "Seleccionar"


      FROM   "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM" T4
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD" T5
      ON T4."DocEntry" = T5."DocEntry"
      WHERE  T4."U_CantDispo" > 0

      AND T5."U_estado" = 'PREABIFIN'


      <!--saldo pendiente-->

    </Query>

    </Query>

    <Query ID="RetornarArticulosComprometidosSF">
      SELECT
      T0."DocNum" AS "NroSolicitud",
      T1."U_Cant" AS "CantSolicitada",
      T1."U_IdLinea" AS "Linea",
      T1."U_Codigo" AS "Elemento",
      T1."U_ItemCode" AS "Articulo"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T1 ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
      WHERE T0."Canceled" = 'N'
      AND  T0."U_Estado" = '1'
      AND T1. "U_ItemCode" =  '%IdArticulo%'
      AND T1."U_NoContr_Prenec" = '%NroPrenecesidad%'
      AND T1."U_Uso"='N'
      ORDER  BY T0."DocNum"
    </Query>

    <Query ID="verificarOrdenesObligatorias">
      SELECT DISTINCT T1."U_ItemCode"
      FROM   "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T1
      ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
      LEFT JOIN OITM T2
      ON T1."U_ItemCode" = T2."ItemCode"
      WHERE  IFNULL(T2."U_OK1_SF_ObligaOC", 'N') = 'Y'
      AND IFNULL(T1."U_Documento", '') = ''
      AND IFNULL(T1."U_docOrd", '') = ''
      AND IFNULL(T1."U_Renuncia", 'N') = 'N'
      AND T0."DocEntry" = '%numOSF%'
      AND T1."U_Codigo" IN ( SELECT  "Code" FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC"
      WHERE "U_traslado" = 'N'  AND  "U_destFinal" = 'N')
    </Query>

    <Query ID="verificarOrdenesCompraObligatoriasInTraslado">
      SELECT DISTINCT T1."U_nomArt", T1."LineId"
      FROM   "@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA" T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA_ELE" T1
      ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
      LEFT JOIN OITM T2
      ON T1."U_nomArt" = T2."ItemCode"
      WHERE  IFNULL(T2."U_OK1_SF_ObligaOC", 'N') = 'Y'
      AND IFNULL(T1."U_Documento", '') = ''
      AND IFNULL(T1."U_docOrd", '') = ''
      AND T0."DocEntry" = '%DoEntryOTraslado%'

    </Query>

    <Query ID="RetornarCantElementosOrdenTraslado">
      SELECT IFNULL (COUNT (T1."U_Codigo"),0)AS "Cant"
      FROM   "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T0

      ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
      WHERE
      T0."DocEntry" = '%DocEntrySF%'
      AND T1."U_Codigo" IN ( SELECT  "Code" FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC"
      WHERE "U_traslado" = 'Y')
      AND T1."U_Codigo" = '%IdElemento%'
    </Query>

    <Query ID="RevisarCodigosTransaccion" remarks="">
      SELECT
      T0."Codigo",
      T0."Descr"
      FROM (( SELECT
      'SFCI' AS "Codigo",
      'SF Costeo de Inventario' AS "Descr"
      FROM DUMMY )) AS T0
      LEFT OUTER JOIN (SELECT
      T3."TrnsCode" AS "Codigo",
      T3."TrnsCodDsc" AS "Descr"
      FROM OTRC T3
      WHERE T3."TrnsCode" IN ('SFCI') ) AS T1 ON T0."Codigo" = T1."Codigo"
      WHERE T1."Codigo" IS NULL
    </Query>

    </Query>

    </Query>
    <Query ID="getElementosPrenecFinan">
      SELECT T0."Code",
      T0."Name",
      T1."LineId",
      T1."U_SeFactu",
      CASE
      WHEN T1."U_ItemCode" =
      (
      SELECT "ItemCode"
      FROM   OITM
      WHERE  "validFor" = 'N'
      AND    "ItemCode" = T1."U_ItemCode") THEN ''
      ELSE T1."U_ItemCode"
      END AS "U_ItemCode",
      CASE
      WHEN T1."U_ItemCode" =
      (
      SELECT "ItemCode"
      FROM   OITM
      WHERE  "validFor" = 'N'
      AND    "ItemCode" = T1."U_ItemCode") THEN ''
      ELSE T1."U_ItemName"
      END AS "U_ItemName",
      T1."U_Cant" -
      (

      select
      sum(TF."U_Cant") AS "CANSF"
      FROM (

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T11. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T10
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T11 ON T10."DocEntry" = T11."DocEntry"
      WHERE T10."Canceled" = 'N'

      AND       ( T10."U_Estado" NOT IN(8,3,10))
      AND T10."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOrdenSF" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T10."U_VSede")
      AND T11. "U_Codigo" = T1."U_Codigo"
      AND T11."U_IdLineaPF" = T1."U_IdLinea"
      AND T11."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T1."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND ( T11."U_tipLinea" = 'B' OR IFNULL(T11."U_tipLinea",'') = '')
      AND ( IFNULL(T11."U_Uso",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T11."U_Uso",'') = '' )

      union all

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T8. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" T7
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_PQC_OPARELM_LIN" T8 ON T7."DocEntry" = T8."DocEntry"
      WHERE T7."Canceled" = 'N'

      AND ( T7."U_Estado" NOT IN('08','03'))
      AND T7."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOP" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T7."U_SedeParque")

      AND T8. "U_Codigo" = T1."U_Codigo"
      AND T8."U_IdLineaPF" = T1."U_IdLinea"
      AND T8."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T1."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND ( T8."U_tipLinea" = 'B' OR IFNULL(T8."U_tipLinea",'') = '')
      AND ( IFNULL(T8."U_Uso",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T8."U_Uso",'') = '' )

      )AS TF

      ) AS "U_Cant",
      T1."U_Valor" AS "U_Valor",
      T1."U_tipLinea",
      "U_TipoOrden" AS "Tipo Orden",
      T1."U_IdLinea",
      T1."U_Linea",
      T1."U_LineaName",
      T1."U_ItemCodePB",
      T1."U_IdLineaPB",
      T1."U_CantDGP"


      FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" T0 INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM" T1 ON T0."Code" = T1."U_Codigo" WHERE T1."DocEntry" = '%idContrato%'
      AND
      T1."U_CantDispo" > 0
      AND
      T1."%campo%" = 'Y'
      AND
      T1."U_Cant" -
      (
      select
      sum(TF."U_Cant") AS "CANSF"
      FROM (

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T11. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T10
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T11 ON T10."DocEntry" = T11."DocEntry"
      WHERE T10."Canceled" = 'N'

      AND       ( T10."U_Estado" NOT IN(8,3,10))
      AND T10."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOrdenSF" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T10."U_VSede")
      AND T11. "U_Codigo" = T1."U_Codigo"
      AND T11."U_IdLineaPF" = T1."U_IdLinea"
      AND T11."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T1."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND ( T11."U_tipLinea" = 'B' OR IFNULL(T11."U_tipLinea",'') = '')
      AND ( IFNULL(T11."U_Uso",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T11."U_Uso",'') = '' )

      union all

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T8. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" T7
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_PQC_OPARELM_LIN" T8 ON T7."DocEntry" = T8."DocEntry"
      WHERE T7."Canceled" = 'N'

      AND ( T7."U_Estado" NOT IN('08','03'))
      AND T7."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOP" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T7."U_SedeParque")

      AND T8. "U_Codigo" = T1."U_Codigo"
      AND T8."U_IdLineaPF" = T1."U_IdLinea"
      AND T8."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T1."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND ( T8."U_tipLinea" = 'B' OR IFNULL(T8."U_tipLinea",'') = '')
      AND ( IFNULL(T8."U_Uso",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T8."U_Uso",'') = '' )

      )AS TF

      ) > 0
      AND
      (
      T1."U_tipLinea" = 'B'
      OR
      IFNULL(T1."U_tipLinea", '') = ''
      )

      UNION
      (
      SELECT T0."Code",
      T0."Name",
      T1."LineId",
      T1."U_SeFactu",
      CASE
      WHEN T1."U_ItemCode" =
      (
      SELECT "ItemCode"
      FROM   OITM
      WHERE  "validFor" = 'N'
      AND    "ItemCode" = T1."U_ItemCode") THEN ''
      ELSE T1."U_ItemCode"
      END AS "U_ItemCode",
      CASE
      WHEN T1."U_ItemCode" =
      (
      SELECT "ItemCode"
      FROM   OITM
      WHERE  "validFor" = 'N'
      AND    "ItemCode" = T1."U_ItemCode") THEN ''
      ELSE T1."U_ItemName"
      END AS "U_ItemName",
      T1."U_Cant" -
      (

      FROM (

    <Query ID="getElementosPrenecFinan_old">
      SELECT
      T0."Code",
      T0."Name",
      CASE WHEN T1."U_ItemCode" = ( SELECT
      "ItemCode"
      FROM OITM
      WHERE "validFor" = 'N'
      AND "ItemCode" = T1."U_ItemCode" )
      THEN ''
      ELSE T1."U_ItemCode"
      END AS "U_ItemCode",
      CASE WHEN T1."U_ItemCode" = ( SELECT
      "ItemCode"
      FROM OITM
      WHERE "validFor" = 'N'
      AND "ItemCode" = T1."U_ItemCode" )
      THEN ''
      ELSE T1."U_ItemName"
      END AS "U_ItemName",
      T1."U_Cant"      - (

      SELECT
      IFNULL(SUM (T6. "U_Cant"),0) AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T5
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T6 ON T5."DocEntry" = T6."DocEntry"
      WHERE T5."Canceled" = 'N'
      AND ( T5."U_Estado" &lt;&gt; '8' OR T5."U_Estado" &lt;&gt; '3')
      AND T5."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOrdenSF" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T5."U_VSede")
      AND T6. "U_Codigo" = T1."U_Codigo"
      AND T6."U_IdLineaPF" = T1."U_IdLinea"
      AND T6."U_NoContr_Prenec" =  CAST(T1."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND (T6."U_tipLinea" = 'P' OR T1."U_tipLinea" = 'B' OR IFNULL(T1."U_tipLinea",'') = '')
      AND ( IFNULL(T6."U_Uso",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T6."U_Uso",'') = '' )

      )     AS "U_Cant",

      T1."U_Valor" AS "U_Valor",
      T1."U_tipLinea",
      "U_TipoOrden" AS "Tipo Orden",
      T1."U_IdLinea",
      T1."U_Linea",
      T1."U_LineaName"

      FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM" T1 ON T0."Code" = T1."U_Codigo"
      WHERE T1."DocEntry" = '%idContrato%'
      AND T1."U_CantDispo" > 0
      AND T1."%campo%" = 'Y'

      AND  T1."U_Cant" - (
      SELECT
      IFNULL(SUM (T6. "U_Cant"),0) AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T5
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T6 ON T5."DocEntry" = T6."DocEntry"
      WHERE T5."Canceled" = 'N'
      AND ( T5."U_Estado" &lt;&gt; '8' OR T5."U_Estado" &lt;&gt; '3')
      AND T5."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOrdenSF" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T5."U_VSede")
      AND T6. "U_Codigo" = T1."U_Codigo"
      AND T6."U_IdLineaPF" = T1."U_IdLinea"
      AND T6."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T1."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND (T6."U_tipLinea" = 'P' OR T6."U_tipLinea" = 'B' OR IFNULL(T6."U_tipLinea",'') = '')
      AND ( IFNULL(T6."U_Uso",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T6."U_Uso",'') = '' )
      ) > 0
      ORDER BY T1."LineId"
    </Query>

    <Query ID="OK_getElementosPrenecFinan">
      SELECT T0."Code",
      T0."Name",
      T1."LineId",
      T1."U_SeFactu",
      CASE
      WHEN T1."U_ItemCode" =
      (
      SELECT "ItemCode"
      FROM   OITM
      WHERE  "validFor" = 'N'
      AND    "ItemCode" = T1."U_ItemCode") THEN ''
      ELSE T1."U_ItemCode"
      END AS "U_ItemCode",
      CASE
      WHEN T1."U_ItemCode" =
      (
      SELECT "ItemCode"
      FROM   OITM
      WHERE  "validFor" = 'N'
      AND    "ItemCode" = T1."U_ItemCode") THEN ''
      ELSE T1."U_ItemName"
      END AS "U_ItemName",
      T1."U_Cant" -
      (

      select
      sum(TF."U_Cant") AS "CANSF"
      FROM (

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T11. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T10
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T11 ON T10."DocEntry" = T11."DocEntry"
      WHERE T10."Canceled" = 'N'

      AND       ( T10."U_Estado" NOT IN(8,3,10))
      AND T10."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOrdenSF" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T10."U_VSede")
      AND T11. "U_Codigo" = T1."U_Codigo"
      AND T11."U_IdLineaPF" = T1."U_IdLinea"
      AND T11."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T1."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND ( T11."U_tipLinea" = 'B' OR IFNULL(T11."U_tipLinea",'') = '')
      AND ( IFNULL(T11."U_Uso",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T11."U_Uso",'') = '' )

      union all

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T8. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" T7
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_PQC_OPARELM_LIN" T8 ON T7."DocEntry" = T8."DocEntry"
      WHERE T7."Canceled" = 'N'

      AND ( T7."U_Estado" NOT IN('08','03'))
      AND T7."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOP" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T7."U_SedeParque")

      AND T8. "U_Codigo" = T1."U_Codigo"
      AND T8."U_IdLineaPF" = T1."U_IdLinea"
      AND T8."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T1."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND ( T8."U_tipLinea" = 'B' OR IFNULL(T8."U_tipLinea",'') = '')
      AND ( IFNULL(T8."U_Uso",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T8."U_Uso",'') = '' )

      )AS TF

      ) AS "U_Cant",
      T1."U_Valor" AS "U_Valor",
      T1."U_tipLinea",
      "U_TipoOrden" AS "Tipo Orden",
      T1."U_IdLinea",
      T1."U_Linea",
      T1."U_LineaName",
      T1."U_ItemCodePB",
      T1."U_IdLineaPB"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" T0 INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM" T1 ON T0."Code" = T1."U_Codigo" WHERE T1."DocEntry" = '%idContrato%'
      AND
      T1."U_CantDispo" > 0
      AND
      T1."%campo%" = 'Y'
      AND
      T1."U_Cant" -
      (
      select
      sum(TF."U_Cant") AS "CANSF"
      FROM (

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T11. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T10
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T11 ON T10."DocEntry" = T11."DocEntry"
      WHERE T10."Canceled" = 'N'

      AND       ( T10."U_Estado" NOT IN(8,3,10))
      AND T10."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOrdenSF" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T10."U_VSede")
      AND T11. "U_Codigo" = T1."U_Codigo"
      AND T11."U_IdLineaPF" = T1."U_IdLinea"
      AND T11."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T1."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND ( T11."U_tipLinea" = 'B' OR IFNULL(T11."U_tipLinea",'') = '')
      AND ( IFNULL(T11."U_Uso",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T11."U_Uso",'') = '' )

      union all

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T8. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" T7
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_PQC_OPARELM_LIN" T8 ON T7."DocEntry" = T8."DocEntry"
      WHERE T7."Canceled" = 'N'

      AND ( T7."U_Estado" NOT IN('08','03'))
      AND T7."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOP" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T7."U_SedeParque")

      AND T8. "U_Codigo" = T1."U_Codigo"
      AND T8."U_IdLineaPF" = T1."U_IdLinea"
      AND T8."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T1."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND ( T8."U_tipLinea" = 'B' OR IFNULL(T8."U_tipLinea",'') = '')
      AND ( IFNULL(T8."U_Uso",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T8."U_Uso",'') = '' )

      )AS TF

      ) > 0
      AND
      (
      T1."U_tipLinea" = 'B'
      OR
      IFNULL(T1."U_tipLinea", '') = ''
      )

      UNION
      (
      SELECT T0."Code",
      T0."Name",
      T1."LineId",
      T1."U_SeFactu",
      CASE
      WHEN T1."U_ItemCode" =
      (
      SELECT "ItemCode"
      FROM   OITM
      WHERE  "validFor" = 'N'
      AND    "ItemCode" = T1."U_ItemCode") THEN ''
      ELSE T1."U_ItemCode"
      END AS "U_ItemCode",
      CASE
      WHEN T1."U_ItemCode" =
      (
      SELECT "ItemCode"
      FROM   OITM
      WHERE  "validFor" = 'N'
      AND    "ItemCode" = T1."U_ItemCode") THEN ''
      ELSE T1."U_ItemName"
      END AS "U_ItemName",
      T1."U_Cant" -
      (
      select
      sum(TF."U_Cant") AS "CANSF"
      FROM (

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T11. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T10
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T11 ON T10."DocEntry" = T11."DocEntry"
      WHERE T10."Canceled" = 'N'

      AND       ( T10."U_Estado" NOT IN(8,3,10))
      AND T10."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOrdenSF" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T10."U_VSede")
      AND T11. "U_Codigo" = T1."U_Codigo"
      AND T11."U_IdLineaPF" = T1."U_IdLinea"
      AND T11."U_LineaPadrePN"=T1."U_IdLineaPB"
      AND T11."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T1."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND ( T11."U_tipLinea" = 'C' )
      AND ( IFNULL(T11."U_Uso",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T11."U_Uso",'') = '' )

      union all

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T8. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" T7
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_PQC_OPARELM_LIN" T8 ON T7."DocEntry" = T8."DocEntry"
      WHERE T7."Canceled" = 'N'

      AND ( T7."U_Estado" NOT IN('08','03'))
      AND T7."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOP" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T7."U_SedeParque")

      AND T8. "U_Codigo" = T1."U_Codigo"
      AND T8."U_IdLineaPF" = T1."U_IdLinea"
      AND T8."U_LineaPadrePN"=T1."U_IdLineaPB"
      AND T8."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T1."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND ( T8."U_tipLinea" = 'C' )
      AND ( IFNULL(T8."U_Uso",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T8."U_Uso",'') = '' )

      )AS TF)
      AS "U_Cant",
      T1."U_Valor" AS "U_Valor",
      T1."U_tipLinea",
      "U_TipoOrden" AS "Tipo Orden",
      T1."U_IdLinea",
      T1."U_Linea",
      T1."U_LineaName",
      T1."U_ItemCodePB",
      T1."U_IdLineaPB"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" T0 INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM" T1 ON T0."Code" = T1."U_Codigo" WHERE T1."DocEntry" = '%idContrato%'
      AND
      T1."U_CantDispo" > 0
      AND
      T1."%campo%" = 'Y'
      AND
      T1."U_Cant" -
      (
      select
      sum(TF."U_Cant") AS "CANSF"
      FROM (

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T11. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T10
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T11 ON T10."DocEntry" = T11."DocEntry"
      WHERE T10."Canceled" = 'N'

      AND       ( T10."U_Estado" NOT IN(8,3,10))
      AND T10."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOrdenSF" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T10."U_VSede")
      AND T11. "U_Codigo" = T1."U_Codigo"
      AND T11."U_IdLineaPF" = T1."U_IdLinea"
      AND T11."U_LineaPadrePN"=T1."U_IdLineaPB"
      AND T11."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T1."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND ( T11."U_tipLinea" = 'C' )
      AND ( IFNULL(T11."U_Uso",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T11."U_Uso",'') = '' )

      union all

      SELECT
      IFNULL( SUM (T8. "U_Cant") ,0)AS "U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" T7
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_PQC_OPARELM_LIN" T8 ON T7."DocEntry" = T8."DocEntry"
      WHERE T7."Canceled" = 'N'

      AND ( T7."U_Estado" NOT IN('08','03'))
      AND T7."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOP" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE  "U_sede" = T7."U_SedeParque")

      AND T8. "U_Codigo" = T1."U_Codigo"
      AND T8."U_IdLineaPF" = T1."U_IdLinea"
      AND T8."U_LineaPadrePN"=T1."U_IdLineaPB"
      AND T8."U_NoContr_Prenec" = CAST(T1."DocEntry" as nvarchar(10))
      AND ( T8."U_tipLinea" = 'C' )
      AND ( IFNULL(T8."U_Uso",'N') = 'N' OR IFNULL(T8."U_Uso",'') = '' )

      )AS TF
      ) > 0
      AND
      (
      T1."U_tipLinea" = 'C'
      OR
      T1."U_tipLinea" = 'P'
      ) ) ORDER BY T1."LineId" ASC
    </Query>

    </Query>

    </Query>

    <Query ID="ArticuloFacturaIndependiente">
      SELECT T0."ItemCode" FROM INV1 T0
      INNER JOIN OITM T1 ON T0."ItemCode" = T1."ItemCode"
      INNER JOIN OINV T2 ON T0."ItemCode" = T1."ItemCode"
      WHERE T0."DocEntry" = '%docFact%'
      AND T2."U_OK1_SF_NSOL" = '%nOSF%'

    </Query>
    <Query ID="ActualizarArticuloIndependienteOSF">
      UPDATE  "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"  T0  SET T0."U_LPagadaPrenec" = 'Y',"U_NoFacturaPrenec" = '%docFact%' WHERE T0."DocEntry" ='%nOSF%' AND  IFNULL(T0."U_NoFacturaPrenec",'N') = 'N'
      AND  T0."U_ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="ActualizarComponentesIndependienteOSF">
      UPDATE  "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"  T0  SET T0."U_LPagadaPrenec" = 'Y',"U_NoFacturaPrenec" = '%docFact%' WHERE T0."DocEntry" = '%nOSF%' AND  IFNULL(T0."U_NoFacturaPrenec",'N') = 'N'
      AND  T0."U_ItemCodePB" = '%ItemCode%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="trasladarOrdenesTraslado">

    </Query>

    </Query>
    <Query ID="AgregarComentarioOrdenesTraslado">

    </Query>

    <Query ID="esValidoTipoCambio">
      SELECT T0."DocEntry" FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD"  T0
      WHERE T0."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOrdenSF" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR") AND T0."DocEntry" = '%nOSF%';
    </Query>
    <Query ID="esValidoTipoBasadoEn">
      SELECT T0."DocEntry"  FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD"  T0
      WHERE T0."Series" IN (	SELECT "U_serieCotizSF" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE "U_sede" = '%idSede%' UNION ALL SELECT "U_serieAP" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE "U_sede" ='%idSede%' )
      AND (T0."U_Estado" = '1' OR (T0."U_Estado" = '6' AND T0."U_MotCierre" IN (SELECT "Code" FROM "@OK1_SF_MOTCANCSERV" WHERE "U_supFecVenc" = 'Y')))
      AND T0."DocEntry" = '%nOSF%';
    </Query>
    <Query ID="verificarOSFBase">
      SELECT T0."DocEntry" FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD"  T0
      WHERE T0."U_SolAnte" = '%nOSF%' AND "Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOrdenSF" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR");
    </Query>

    <Query ID="ExisteSolicitudTraServiciosCerrados">
      SELECT
      IFNULL (COUNT (T2."U_Estado"),0) AS "Estado"
      FROM OCLG T0
      INNER JOIN OUSR T1 ON T0."AttendUser" = T1.USERID
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T2 ON T0."U_DocEntry" = T2."DocEntry"
      WHERE T0."ClgCode" = '%NroSolicitud%'
      AND "CntctType" = (SELECT
      "Code"
      FROM OCLT
      WHERE "U_tipoAct" = 'T')
      AND T2."U_Estado" = 10
    </Query>

    <Query ID="Retornar_Codigo_Impuesto_Prenecesidad">
      select "U_Codigo_Iva" from "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM"
      where "DocEntry" = '%DocEntyPrenecesidad%'
      AND "U_IdLinea" = '%lineaPrenecesidad%'

    <Query ID="retornarValorRevalorizado_From_SumatoriaRevalorizacion">

      SELECT     IFNULL(Sum(T3."U_VlrRevalUnitario"),0) AS "ValorRevalorizado"

      FROM       "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_REV_L" T3

      INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_REV_H" T4
      ON         T4."DocEntry" = T3."DocEntry"

      WHERE      T3."U_CodElem" = '%CodigoElemento%'
      AND        T3."U_IdLineaPN" = '%LineaPrenecesidad%'
      AND        T3."U_Sel" = 'Y'
      AND        T3."U_IdPreNecFin" = '%DocEntryPrenecesidad%'
      AND        T4."DocEntry" &lt;
      (
      SELECT IFNULL(Max(T5."DocEntry"),0)+1 AS "DoEntryReval"
      FROM   "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_REV_H" T5
      )
      AND IFNULL(T4."U_JE_Rev", 0) &lt;&gt;0
      AND IFNULL(T4."U_JE_CanRev", 0) = 0
    </Query>

    <Query ID="old_retornarValorRevalorizado_From_SumatoriaRevalorizacion">

      SELECT     IFNULL(Sum(T3."U_VlrAplicado"),0) AS "ValorRevalorizado"

      FROM       "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_REV_L" T3

      INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_REV_H" T4
      ON         T4."DocEntry" = T3."DocEntry"

      WHERE      T3."U_CodElem" = '%CodigoElemento%'
      AND        T3."U_ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%'
      AND        T3."U_IdLineaPN" = '%LineaPrenecesidad%'
      AND        T3."U_Sel" = 'Y'
      AND        T3."U_IdPreNecFin" = '%DocEntryPrenecesidad%'
      AND        T4."DocEntry" &lt;
      (
      SELECT IFNULL(Max(T5."DocEntry"),0)+1 AS "DoEntryReval"
      FROM   "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_REV_H" T5
      )
      AND IFNULL(T4."U_JE_Rev", 0) &lt;&gt;0
    </Query>

    <Query ID="RetornarCuentaImpuestoArticulo" remarks="RetornarCuentaImpuestoArticulo">
      select IFNULL("SalesTax",'') FROM OSTA WHERE "Code" = '%CodImpuesto%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="descuentoDiferenteLog">

      "U_Descrip"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_LOGALELLAMAD"
      WHERE "DocEntry" = %nOSF%
      AND "U_Descrip" LIKE '%Valor Descuento Aplicado -%'
      AND "U_Descrip" LIKE '% Id. Línea (%idlinea%).%'
      ORDER BY "LineId" DESC
    </Query>
    <Query ID="descuentoPorcentajeDiferenteLog">

    <Query ID="Consultar_Retenciones_FUENTE_SocioNegocio">
      SELECT
      T0."WTCode" AS "CodigoRetencion",
      T1."WTName" AS "NombreRetencion",
      T1."PrctBsAmnt" AS "PrctajeRetencion",
      T1."BaseType" AS "TipoBase",
      T1."U_BP_Base_Mi" AS "BaseMinima",
      IFNULL (T1."U_Tipo",'') AS "TipoRetencion"

      FROM  CRD4 T0 INNER JOIN  OWHT T1 ON T0."WTCode"=T1."WTCode"

      WHERE  "CardCode" = '%CardCode%'
      AND "U_Tipo" = 'FUENTE'
      AND  "U_exe_aplServ" = 'Y'
    </Query>

    <Query ID="Consultar_Retenciones_iCA_SocioNegocio">
      SELECT
      T0."WTCode" AS "CodigoRetencion",
      T1."WTName" AS "NombreRetencion",
      T1."PrctBsAmnt" AS "PrctajeRetencion",
      T1."BaseType" AS "TipoBase",
      T1."U_BP_Base_Mi" AS "BaseMinima",
      IFNULL (T1."U_Tipo",'') AS "TipoRetencion"
      FROM  CRD4 T0 INNER JOIN  OWHT T1 ON T0."WTCode"=T1."WTCode"

      WHERE
      "U_exe_aplServ" = 'Y'
      AND "U_Tipo" = 'ICA'
      AND "U_BPCO_MUN" = '%municipio%'
      AND "CardCode" = '%CardCode%'
    </Query>

    <Query ID="DetalleRetenciones" remarks="obtiene las retenciones de una factura">
      SELECT
      "WTCode" AS "CodigoRetencion",
      ("U_Base_ML" - T1."DiscSum") AS "U_Base_ML",
      "U_Tarifa" AS "U_Tarifa",
      "U_Fuente" AS "U_Fuente",
      "U_Ret_ML" AS "U_Ret_ML"
      FROM  INV5 T0
      INNER JOIN OINV T1
      ON  T0."AbsEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
      WHERE
      "AbsEntry" = '%DocEntryFactura%'
      AND
      IFNULL("WTCode",'') &lt;&gt; ''
    </Query>

    <Query ID="infoRecobro">
      SELECT
      T0."Name",

      T0."U_SMMLV_Cub",

    <Query ID="ArtAdcMejOSF">
      SELECT
      T0."U_ItemCode" AS "Cod Artículo",
      T0."U_ItemName" AS "Nombre Artículo",
      T0."U_ArtAdMe" AS "Cód. Artículo Adic/Mejora",
      (CASE WHEN T0."U_ArtAdMe" = 'A'
      THEN 'Adicional' WHEN T0."U_ArtAdMe" = 'M'
      THEN 'Mejora' WHEN T0."U_ArtAdMe" = 'E'
      THEN 'Excedente'
      END) AS "Articulo Adic/Mejora",
      T0."U_totalLinea"  AS "Total Linea",
      T0."U_IdLinea" AS "Id. Línea",
      T0."U_almacen" AS "Almacen",
      T0."U_codIva" AS "Cod. Iva"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T0
      WHERE T0."DocEntry" =  %nOSF%
      AND T0."U_ItemCode" = '%itemCode%'
      AND T0."U_IdLinea" = '%idLinea%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="ArtAdcMejOSFArt">
      SELECT
      T0."U_ItemCode" AS "Cod Artículo",
      T0."U_ItemName" AS "Nombre Artículo",
      T0."U_ArtAdMe" AS "Cód. Artículo Adic/Mejora",
      (CASE WHEN T0."U_ArtAdMe" = 'A'
      THEN 'Adicional' WHEN T0."U_ArtAdMe" = 'M'
      THEN 'Mejora' WHEN T0."U_ArtAdMe" = 'E'
      THEN 'Excedente'
      END) AS "Articulo Adic/Mejora",
      T0."U_totalLinea"  AS "Total Linea",
      T0."U_IdLinea" AS "Id. Línea",
      T0."U_almacen" AS "Almacen",
      T0."U_codIva" AS "Cod. Iva"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T0
      WHERE T0."DocEntry" =  %nOSF%
      AND T0."U_ItemCode" = '%itemCode%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="ArtAdcMejOSFALL">
      SELECT
      T0."U_ItemCode" AS "Cod Artículo",
      T0."U_ItemName" AS "Nombre Artículo",
      T0."U_ArtAdMe" AS "Cód. Artículo Adic/Mejora",
      (CASE WHEN T0."U_ArtAdMe" = 'A'
      THEN 'Adicional' WHEN T0."U_ArtAdMe" = 'M'
      THEN 'Mejora' WHEN T0."U_ArtAdMe" = 'E'
      THEN 'Excedente'
      END) AS "Articulo Adic/Mejora",
      T0."U_totalLinea"  AS "Total Linea",
      T0."U_IdLinea" AS "Id. Línea",
      T0."U_almacen" AS "Almacen",
      T0."U_codIva" AS "Cod. Iva"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T0
      WHERE T0."DocEntry" =  %nOSF%
      AND (T0."U_ArtAdMe" = 'A'
      OR T0."U_ArtAdMe" = 'M'
      OR T0."U_ArtAdMe" = 'E')
      AND T0."U_seFactu" != 'Y'
    </Query>

    <Query ID="cantidadDocumentosCreadosSF">
      SELECT SUM("No. Int")AS "TotalDocumentos"
      FROM(
      SELECT
      COUNT ("DocEntry") AS "No. Int"
      FROM   OINV
      WHERE  "U_OK1_SF_NSOL" = '%solicitud%'
      AND "U_OK1_PQC_NOPQ" IS NULL

      UNION ALL

      SELECT
      COUNT("DocEntry") AS "No. Int"
      FROM   "ORIN"
      WHERE  "U_OK1_SF_NSOL" = '%solicitud%'

      UNION ALL

      SELECT
      COUNT ("DocEntry") AS "No. Int"
      FROM   ODLN T2
      WHERE  "U_OK1_SF_NSOL" = '%solicitud%'

      UNION ALL

      SELECT
      COUNT ("DocEntry") AS "No. Int"
      FROM   OPCH
      WHERE  "U_OK1_SF_NSOL" = '%solicitud%'

      UNION ALL

      SELECT
      COUNT ("DocEntry") AS "No. Int"
      FROM OIGE
      WHERE  U_OK1_SF_NSOL = '%solicitud%'

      UNION ALL

      SELECT
      COUNT ("DocEntry") AS "No. Int"
      FROM OIGN
      WHERE  U_OK1_SF_NSOL = '%solicitud%'

      UNION ALL

      SELECT
      COUNT ("DocEntry") AS "No. Int"
      FROM   OPOR
      WHERE  "U_OK1_SF_NSOL" = '%solicitud%'

      UNION ALL

      SELECT
      COUNT ("DocEntry") AS "No. Int"
      FROM   ORCT
      WHERE  "U_OK1_SF_NSOL" = '%solicitud%'

      UNION ALL

      SELECT
      COUNT ("DocEntry") AS "No. Int"
      FROM   ORDR T2
      WHERE  "U_OK1_SF_NSOL" = '%solicitud%') AS TBL
    </Query>

    <Query ID="RetornarSerieOrdenesCompra">
      select "U_serieOC" from "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR"
      WHERE "U_sede" = '%sede%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="existeCotizacionAbierta" remarks="">

      SELECT
      "DocEntry"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD"
      WHERE "U_IdFallec" = '%id%'
      AND ("Series" IN (SELECT
      "U_serieCotizSF"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR"))
      AND "DocEntry" != '%nOSF%'
      AND IFNULL("U_MotCierre",'-1')   NOT IN (SELECT "Code" FROM "@OK1_SF_MOTCANCSERV" WHERE "U_cierreCotizacion"='Y')
      ORDER BY "DocEntry" DESC

    </Query>

      WHERE "U_Usuario" IN

    <Query ID="retonar_CantidadUsosRenuncias">
      SELECT
      (SELECT COUNT("U_Renuncia") AS "CantidadRenuncias" from "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
      WHERE "DocEntry" ='%DocEntry%'
      AND "U_tipLinea" = 'H'
      AND "U_LineaPadre" = '%LineaPadre%'
      AND  IFNULL("U_Renuncia",'N')='Y')
      +
      (SELECT COUNT("U_Uso") AS "CantidadUsos" from "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
      WHERE "DocEntry" ='%DocEntry%'
      AND "U_tipLinea" = 'H'
      AND "U_LineaPadre" = '%LineaPadre%'
      AND  IFNULL("U_Uso",'N')='Y')AS "CantidadUsos" from DUMMY;
    </Query>

    <Query ID="retonar_CantidadUsosRenunciasPaquetes">
      SELECT
      (
      SELECT COUNT("U_Renuncia") AS "CantidadRenuncias" from "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
      WHERE "DocEntry" ='%DocEntry%'
      AND "U_tipLinea" = 'C'
      AND "U_LineaPadre" = '%LineaPadre%'
      AND  IFNULL("U_Renuncia",'N')='Y'
      )
      +
      (
      SELECT COUNT("U_Uso") AS "CantidadUsos" from "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
      WHERE "DocEntry" ='%DocEntry%'
      AND "U_tipLinea" = 'C'
      AND "U_LineaPadre" = '%LineaPadre%'
      AND  IFNULL("U_Uso",'N')='Y'
      )AS "CantidadUsos" FROM DUMMY;
    </Query>

    <Query ID="consultarCalculoBaseIVA" remarks="">

    <Query ID="RetornarSumatoriaRevalorizacionesElementosFacturados" remarks="">
      SELECT
      SUM(T1."Credit")AS "ValorRevalorizado"

      FROM OJDT T0 INNER JOIN JDT1 T1 ON T0."TransId" = T1."TransId"
      WHERE T0."Ref1" = '%DocEntryPNF%'
      AND T0."TransCode" = 'PRIN'
      AND T0."Ref3" = 'REVSALD'
      AND T1."Ref2" IN ('%LineaPNF%','%DocEntryCredito%')

      AND T1."TransId" NOT IN
      (

      IFNULL( T1."U_TransId",0)
      FROM OJDT T0
      INNER JOIN JDT1 T1 ON T0."TransId" = T1."TransId"
      WHERE T0."Ref1" = '%DocEntryPNF%'
      AND T0."TransCode" = 'PRIN'
      AND T0."Ref3" = 'REVSRV'
      AND T1."Ref2" IN ('%LineaPNF%','%DocEntryCredito%')
      )

    </Query>

    <Query ID="RetornarTransIdAsientosReversadosdeRevalorizacion" remarks="">
      SELECT "TransId" FROM OJDT
      WHERE "U_nFact" = '%U_nFact%'
      AND "Ref3" = 'REVSALD'
      AND "Ref1" = '%Prenecesidad%'
    </Query>

    <Query ID="TipoInhumacionCementerio" remarks="">
      SELECT * FROM (
      SELECT 'B' AS "Tipo Inhumación",'Bóveda' AS "Nombre","U_Boveda" AS "Valor" FROM "@OK1_SF_CEMENTERIOS" WHERE "Code" = '%codCement%'
      UNION ALL
      SELECT 'C' AS "Tipo Inhumación",'Campo Cremación' AS "Nombre","U_Campo_Cremacion" AS "Valor" FROM "@OK1_SF_CEMENTERIOS" WHERE "Code" = '%codCement%'
      UNION ALL
      SELECT 'L' AS "Tipo Inhumación",'Lote' AS "Nombre","U_Lote" AS "Valor" FROM "@OK1_SF_CEMENTERIOS" WHERE "Code" = '%codCement%'
      UNION ALL
      SELECT 'F' AS "Tipo Inhumación",'Lote Familiar' AS "Nombre","U_LoteFamiliar" AS "Valor" FROM "@OK1_SF_CEMENTERIOS" WHERE "Code" = '%codCement%'
      UNION ALL
      SELECT 'O' AS "Tipo Inhumación",'Osario' AS "Nombre","U_Osario" AS "Valor" FROM "@OK1_SF_CEMENTERIOS" WHERE "Code" = '%codCement%'
      UNION ALL
      SELECT 'U' AS "Tipo Inhumación",'Columbario' AS "Nombre","U_Columbario" AS "Valor" FROM "@OK1_SF_CEMENTERIOS" WHERE "Code" = '%codCement%'
      UNION ALL
      SELECT 'M' AS "Tipo Inhumación",'Mausoleo' AS "Nombre","U_Mausoleo" AS "Valor" FROM "@OK1_SF_CEMENTERIOS" WHERE "Code" = '%codCement%'
      UNION ALL
      SELECT 'E' AS "Tipo Inhumación",'Cenizario' AS "Nombre","U_Cenizario" AS "Valor" FROM "@OK1_SF_CEMENTERIOS" WHERE "Code" = '%codCement%'
      ) WHERE "Valor" = 'Y'
    </Query>

    <Query ID="RetornaCantArticulosIgualesOL_OT">
      SELECT
      (
      SELECT COUNT ("U_nomArt")AS "Cant" from  "@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA" T0 INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA_ELE" T1 on T0."DocEntry" = T1. "DocEntry"
      WHERE T0."U_nroOrdenSF"  = '%DocEntrySF%'
      AND  T1."U_Documento" = '%DocEntrySM%'
      AND  T1."U_IdLinea" = '%Linea%'
      AND  T1."U_nomArt" =
      (
      SELECT "U_ItemCode" FROM  "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
      WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntrySF%'
      AND  "U_IdLinea" = '%Linea%'
      )
      )
      +
      (
      SELECT COUNT ("U_ItemCode")AS "Cant" from  "@OK1_SF_ORDENLAB" T0 INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ORDELEMLAB" T1 on T0."DocEntry" = T1. "DocEntry"
      WHERE T0."U_numSolSF"  ='%DocEntrySF%'
      AND  T1."U_Documento" = '%DocEntrySM%'
      AND  T1."U_ID_Linea" = '%Linea%'
      AND  T1."U_ItemCode" =
      (
      SELECT "U_ItemCode" FROM  "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
      WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntrySF%'
      AND  "U_IdLinea" = '%Linea%'
      )
      ) AS "cant"	FROM DUMMY
    </Query>



    <Query ID="ConsultarBasicoPaqueteAntesBorrar">

      FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"   T0

      AND  T0."U_ItemCodeP" =  '%codArtPadre%'
      AND T0."U_tipLinea"  IN ('H','C')
      ORDER BY "LineId"
    </Query>


    <Query ID="consultarOrdenTrasladoDirectas">
	  SELECT "DocEntry" AS "Nº Orden",
      "U_fecCooTras" AS "Fecha",
      "U_horCooTras" AS "Hora",
      "U_idFallec" AS "Id. Fallecido",
      "U_nomFallec" AS "Nombre Fallecido",
      "U_tipoCond" AS "Tipo Conductor",
      "U_condAsig" AS "Conductor Asignado",
      (SELECT T0."CardName" FROM OCRD T0 WHERE T0."CardCode" = "U_condAsig") AS "Nombre",
      (SELECT USER_CODE FROM OUSR WHERE USERID = "UserSign") AS "Usuario",
      "U_estOT"  AS "Estado" FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA" WHERE "U_tipoOrd" = 'D' 
	  AND "U_estOT" NOT IN ('Cerrado', 'Anulado', 'Cancelado') 
	  AND "U_vinculadoOSF" = 'N'
      ORDER BY "Fecha" ASC
    </Query>
    <Query ID="consultarElementosTrasladoOSF">

      "U_ItemCode" "Artículo",

      "U_IdLinea" "Id Linea"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
      WHERE "DocEntry" ='%DocEntryOSF%'
      AND "U_Codigo" IN (SELECT "Code" FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" WHERE "U_traslado" = 'Y')
      AND IFNULL("U_ordenTrasVinc",'') = ''
      AND IFNULL("U_Renuncia", 'N') = 'N'
      ORDER BY "LineId"
    </Query>

    <Query ID="GetElementosConvenioCambioPlan" remarks="">
      SELECT TBLA1."Code", TBLA1."Name", TBLA2."U_cant",TBLA1."U_TipoOrden" AS "Tipo Orden",'N' AS "BASICO"  FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" TBLA1
      INNER JOIN (    SELECT "U_elemCode" AS "U_OK1_SF_CODELEM", "U_cantidad" AS "U_cant" FROM "@OK1_EXE_VIGPLNSPREL" T2 WHERE T2."Code" = '%idConvenio%' || '-' ||  '%idPlan%' || '-' || (
      SELECT CAST("U_IdLinea" AS NVARCHAR) FROM "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEFEC" T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEM" T1 ON T0."Code"= T1."Code"
      WHERE T1."U_codConv" = '%idConvenio%' AND T1."U_codPlan" = '%idPlan%'
      AND '%Fecha%' BETWEEN T0."U_fecini"  AND T0."U_fecfin" AND T0."U_nivServ" = '%nivelServicio%' limit 1) %filtro% )TBLA2
      ON TBLA1."Code"=TBLA2."U_OK1_SF_CODELEM"
      WHERE TBLA1."Code" NOT IN (SELECT "Code" FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" WHERE "U_elemBasico" = 'Y')
      AND 	TBLA1."Code" NOT IN (SELECT "U_Codigo" FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" WHERE "DocEntry" = %nOSF% AND "U_ArtAdMe" != 'A')
      ORDER BY  TBLA1."Code"
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetCtaIngElementoConvenio" remarks="">
      SELECT IFNULL(T2."U_ctaIngSYS",'') FROM "@OK1_EXE_VIGPLNSPREL" T2 WHERE T2."Code" = '%idConvenio%' || '-' ||  '%idPlan%' || '-' || (
      SELECT CAST("U_IdLinea" AS NVARCHAR) FROM "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEFEC" T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEM" T1 ON T0."Code"= T1."Code"
      WHERE T1."U_codConv" = '%idConvenio%' AND T1."U_codPlan" = '%idPlan%'
      AND '%Fecha%' BETWEEN T0."U_fecini"  AND T0."U_fecfin" AND T0."U_nivServ" = '%nivelServicio%' limit 1)
      AND "U_elemCode" = '%idElemento%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="GetCtaIngArticuloConvenio" remarks="">
      SELECT IFNULL(T2."U_ctaIngSYS",'') FROM "@OK1_EXE_VIGPLNSPRAR" T2 WHERE T2."Code" = '%idConvenio%' || '-' ||  '%idPlan%' || '-' || (
      SELECT CAST("U_IdLinea" AS NVARCHAR) FROM "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEFEC" T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEM" T1 ON T0."Code"= T1."Code"
      WHERE T1."U_codConv" = '%idConvenio%' AND T1."U_codPlan" = '%idPlan%'
      AND '%Fecha%' BETWEEN T0."U_fecini"  AND T0."U_fecfin" AND T0."U_nivServ" = '%nivelServicio%' limit 1)
      AND "U_ItemCode" = '%idArticulo%'
    </Query>

    <Query ID="Retornar_ValorCondonadoPrenecesidadAS">

      SELECT SUM("Credit") FROM  JDT1 WHERE "TransId" IN
      (
      SELECT TB1."U_TransId"
      FROM  "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" TB1
      WHERE   IFNULL("U_Motivo",'') &lt;&gt; ''
      AND  IFNULL("U_TransId",0) &lt;&gt; 0
      AND "DocEntry" = '%DocEntryCredito%'
      )
      AND  "Credit" > 0

    </Query>

    <Query ID="ListasMateriales_tipoArtTraslado">
      SELECT T0."Code",T1."ItemName" FROM OITT T0
      INNER JOIN OITM T1 ON T0."Code" = T1."ItemCode"
      WHERE T0."U_tipListSF" = 'T'
      ORDER BY T1."ItemName"
    </Query>

    <Query ID="Existe_ListaMaterialesEnTraslado">
      SELECT  COUNT("Code") FROM  OITT WHERE "U_tipListSF" = 'T' AND "Code" = '%ItemCode%'
    </Query>

    <Query ID="elementosOrdenTrasladoListaMateriales">
      SELECT
      T0."Code" AS "Articulo",
      T0."Qauntity" As "Cantidad",
      T3."ItemName" As "NombreArticulo",
      T3."CardCode" AS "Proveedor",
      T3."InvntItem" AS "InvntItem",
      T0."ToWH" AS "WhsCode",
      (SELECT "OnOrder" FROM OITW WHERE "ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%' AND "WhsCode" =T0."ToWH") AS "Pedido",
      (SELECT "OnHand" FROM OITW WHERE "ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%' AND "WhsCode" =T0."ToWH") AS "Stock",
      (SELECT "IsCommited" FROM OITW WHERE "ItemCode"= '%ItemCode%' AND "WhsCode" =T0."ToWH") AS "Comprometido",

      T3."U_OK1_SF_ELEM" AS "Elemento",
      (SELECT "Name" FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" WHERE "Code" = T3."U_OK1_SF_ELEM")AS "NomElemento",
      (SELECT "U_cofre" FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" WHERE "Code" = T3."U_OK1_SF_ELEM")AS "Cofre"

      FROM OITT T0
      INNER JOIN OITM T3 ON T0."Code" = T3."ItemCode"
      WHERE "U_tipListSF" = 'T'
      AND T0."Code"= '%ItemCode%'

      UNION ALL

      ( SELECT
      T0."Code"AS "Articulo",
      T0."Quantity"As "Cantidad",
      T3."ItemName" As "NombreArticulo",
      T3."CardCode" AS "Proveedor",
      T3."InvntItem" AS "InvntItem",
      T0."Warehouse" AS "WhsCode",
      (SELECT "OnOrder" FROM OITW WHERE "ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%' AND "WhsCode" =T0."Warehouse") AS "Pedido",
      (SELECT "OnHand" FROM OITW WHERE "ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%' AND "WhsCode" =T0."Warehouse") AS "Stock",
      (SELECT "IsCommited" FROM OITW WHERE "ItemCode"= '%ItemCode%' AND "WhsCode" =T0."Warehouse") AS "Comprometido",
      T3."U_OK1_SF_ELEM" AS "Elemento",
      (SELECT "Name" FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" WHERE "Code" = T3."U_OK1_SF_ELEM")AS "NomElemento",
      (SELECT "U_cofre" FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" WHERE "Code" = T3."U_OK1_SF_ELEM")AS "Cofre"

      FROM ITT1 T0
      INNER JOIN OITM T3 ON T0."Code" = T3."ItemCode"
      WHERE T0."Father"= '%ItemCode%')

    </Query>

    <Query ID="ArticulosDisponiblesOrdenTrasladoTipoArticulo">
      SELECT IFNULL(Sum("OnHand"),0) AS "Cantidad"
      FROM   OITW
      WHERE  "ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%'
      AND "WhsCode" = '%Almacen%'
    </Query>

    <Query ID="ObtenerCuentaProvisionGrupoArticulos" remarks="">
      SELECT IFNULL(T1."U_OK1_PQC_CtaProvision",'0') AS CUENTAPROVISION, T1."ExpensesAc"  FROM OITM T0
      INNER JOIN OITB T1 ON T0."ItmsGrpCod" LIKE T1."ItmsGrpCod"
      WHERE T0."ItemCode"='%ItemCode%' AND T0."InvntItem" = 'N' AND T0."U_OK1_SF_ObligaOC" = 'Y'
    </Query>

      INNER JOIN "OBPL" T1 ON T0."U_SucProv"=T1."BPLName"
    </Query>

    <Query ID="ObtenerCuentaProvisionAsiento" remarks="">

    <Query ID="UltimoDocEntryOSF">

      SELECT TOP 1 "DocEntry" FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" ORDER BY "DocEntry" DESC

    </Query>

    <Query ID="AlmacenarRegistroLOG">

      DO
      BEGIN
      INSERT INTO "%EsquemaLOG%"."%TablaLOG%"
      (
      %Campos%
      )
      SELECT
      %Campos%
      FROM "%EsquemaOrigen%"."%TablaOrigen%" WHERE "DocEntry"='%DocEntry%';
      UPDATE "%EsquemaLOG%"."%TablaLOG%" SET
      "U_FechaLog" = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, "U_ConsecLog" ='%Consecutivo%'+1, "U_EventLog" = '%Evento%', "U_UsuLog" ='%Usuario%'
      WHERE IFNULL("U_ConsecLog",0)='0';
      END;

    </Query>

    <Query ID="ObtenerCamposTabla">

      SELECT "COLUMN_NAME"
      FROM "SYS"."M_CS_COLUMNS"
      WHERE SCHEMA_NAME = '%EsquemaLOG%' AND TABLE_NAME = '%Tabla%';

    </Query>

    <Query ID="ConsecutivoLOG">
      SELECT IFNULL(MAX("U_ConsecLog"),0) AS CONSECUTIVO FROM "%EsquemaLOG%"."%TablaLOG%"
    </Query>

    <Query ID="existeCancelacionSalidaMercancia" remarks="">
      SELECT
      T0."DocNum" as "DocNum"
      FROM "OIGN" T0
      INNER JOIN "IGN1" T1 ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"

      WHERE T0."U_OK1_SF_NSOL" = '%NSF%'
      AND T1."U_linea" ='%Linea%'
      AND T1."ItemCode" ='%itemCode%'
      AND T0."U_OK1_SF_EntrySM" = '%entrySalidaMercancia%'

    </Query>
    <Query ID="validarDerechoExclusionesFallecido">
      SELECT T1."DocEntry", T0."U_Codigo"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_AUXILIO"  T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD"  T1
      ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
      WHERE T0."U_Codigo" = '%codDer%' AND  T1."U_IdFallec" = '%idFallec%'
      AND T1."DocEntry" NOT IN ('%docEntry%')

      UNION ALL


      SELECT T1."DocEntry", T0."U_Codigo"
      FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARDER_LIN"  T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD"  T1
      ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
      WHERE T0."U_Codigo" = '%codDer%' AND  T1."U_IdFallec" = '%idFallec%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="validarDerechoExclusionesContrato">
      SELECT T1."DocEntry", T0."U_Codigo"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_AUXILIO"  T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD"  T1
      ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
      WHERE T0."U_Codigo" = '%codDer%' AND   T1."U_NumContr" = '%numContrato%'
      AND T1."DocEntry" NOT IN ('%docEntry%')

      UNION ALL

      SELECT T1."DocEntry", T0."U_Codigo"
      FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARDER_LIN"  T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD"  T1
      ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
      WHERE T0."U_Codigo" = '%codDer%' AND   T1."U_NumContr" =  '%numContrato%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="eliminarDisponibilidadRecursoSalas">
      DELETE FROM  "@OK1_SF_DISP_RECURSO" WHERE "U_nroDoc" ='%nroOSF%' AND "U_CodeRecurso" IN (
      SELECT "U_CodeRecurso" FROM "@OK1_SF_DISP_RECURSO"
      WHERE "U_nroDoc" = '%nroOSF%'
      AND  "U_ObjectType" = 'OK1_SF_LLAMADA'
      AND "U_tipoRecurso" = '6'
      AND "U_CodeRecurso" NOT IN (
      SELECT IFNULL("U_VLugar",'') FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" WHERE "DocEntry" = '%nroOSF%'
      UNION ALL
      SELECT IFNULL("U_VSalaExc",'') FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" WHERE "DocEntry" = '%nroOSF%'
      UNION ALL
      SELECT IFNULL("U_VSalaR",'') FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" WHERE "DocEntry" = '%nroOSF%'
      UNION ALL
      SELECT IFNULL("U_VLugar",'') FROM "@OK1_SF_MULTSALASLI" WHERE "Code" =  '%nroOSF%'
      )
      )
    </Query>
    <Query ID="eliminarDisponibilidadRecursoExequias">
      DELETE FROM  "@OK1_SF_DISP_RECURSO" WHERE "U_nroDoc" ='%nroOSF%' AND "U_CodeRecurso" IN (
      SELECT "U_CodeRecurso" FROM "@OK1_SF_DISP_RECURSO"
      WHERE "U_nroDoc" = '%nroOSF%'
      AND  "U_ObjectType" = 'OK1_SF_LLAMADA'
      AND "U_tipoRecurso" = '5'
      AND "U_CodeRecurso" NOT IN (
      SELECT IFNULL("U_ELugar",'') FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" WHERE "DocEntry" = '%nroOSF%'
      )
      )
    </Query>

    <Query ID="InfoBeneficiarioEnContratoExequial">
      SELECT
      "U_numdoc",
      "U_nombre",
      "U_fecnaci" AS "Fecha Nacimiento",
      "U_parent" AS "Parentesco",
      "U_edad" as "Edad",
      "U_tdbenef" as "Tipo"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_CONT_BENEFI"
      WHERE "DocEntry" = TRIM('%idContrato%')
      AND "U_numdoc" = '%idBeneficiario%'
      AND "U_nombre" IS NOT NULL
      UNION ALL SELECT
      "U_numdoc",
      "U_nombre",
      NULL AS "Fecha Nacimiento",
      '' as "Parentesco",
      0 as "Edad",
      '' as "Tipo"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_BEN_REPATRI"
      WHERE "DocEntry" = TRIM('%idContrato%')
      AND "U_numdoc" = '%idBeneficiario%'
    </Query>

      AND  IFNULL("U_TransId",0) &lt;&gt; 0
      AND "DocEntry" = '%DocEntryCredito%'
      )
      AND  "Credit" > 0
    </Query>

    <Query ID="NEW_Retornar_ValorCondonadoCredito">
      SELECT SUM("Condonado")AS "VlrTotalCondonado"
      FROM
      (
      SELECT (
      CASE WHEN
      (
      SELECT IFNULL(TB1."U_VlrCondonado",0)
      FROM  "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" TB1
      WHERE   IFNULL("U_Motivo",'') &lt;&gt; ''
      AND "DocEntry" =  '%DocEntryCredito%'
      )> 0
      THEN
      (
      SELECT IFNULL(TB1."U_VlrCondonado",0)
      FROM  "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" TB1
      WHERE   IFNULL("U_Motivo",'') &lt;&gt; ''
      AND "DocEntry" =  '%DocEntryCredito%'
      )
      ELSE
      (
      SELECT  IFNULL(TB1."U_VlrPago",0)
      FROM  "@OK1_FINAN_CRED_LIN1" TB1
      WHERE   IFNULL("U_Motivo",'') &lt;&gt; ''
      AND "DocEntry" =  '%DocEntryCredito%'
      )
      END
      ) AS  "Condonado"
      FROM DUMMY
      )
    </Query>

    <Query ID="PuedeMarcarUsoRenunciaPaqueteLM">
      SELECT
      (
      SELECT Count ("U_IdLinea")
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM" T0
      WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntryPN%'
      AND  "U_IdLineaPB" = '%LineaPrenecesidad%'
      AND "U_tipLinea" = 'C'
      AND
      (
      (
      SELECT T2."U_CantDispo"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM" T2
      WHERE T2."DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
      AND  T2."U_IdLinea" = T0."U_IdLinea"
      )
      -%Cantidad%

      ) &gt;= 0
      )
      -
      (
      SELECT
      CASE WHEN
      (
      SELECT Count ("U_IdLinea")
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM" T0
      WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntryPN%'
      AND  "U_IdLineaPB" = '%LineaPrenecesidad%'
      AND "U_tipLinea" = 'C'
      ) >0
      THEN
      (
      SELECT Count ("U_IdLinea")
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM" T0
      WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntryPN%'
      AND  "U_IdLineaPB" = '%LineaPrenecesidad%'
      AND "U_tipLinea" = 'C'
      )
      ELSE
      1
      END  AS  "Cant" FROM DUMMY
      )
      AS "Cantidad" FROM DUMMY ;
    </Query>

    <Query ID="oldPuedeMarcarUsoRenunciaPaqueteLM">
      SELECT
      (
      SELECT Count ("U_IdLinea")
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM" T0
      WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntryPN%'
      AND  "U_IdLineaPB" = '%LineaPrenecesidad%'
      AND "U_tipLinea" = 'C'
      AND
      (
      (
      SELECT T1."U_Cant"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM" T1
      WHERE T1."DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
      AND  T1."U_IdLinea" = T0."U_IdLinea"
      )
      -
      (
      SELECT T2."U_CantDispo"
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM" T2
      WHERE T2."DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
      AND  T2."U_IdLinea" = T0."U_IdLinea"
      )

      ) &gt;= %Cantidad%
      )
      -
      (
      SELECT
      CASE WHEN
      (
      SELECT Count ("U_IdLinea")
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM" T0
      WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntryPN%'
      AND  "U_IdLineaPB" = '%LineaPrenecesidad%'
      AND "U_tipLinea" = 'C'
      ) >0
      THEN
      (
      SELECT Count ("U_IdLinea")
      FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM" T0
      WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntryPN%'
      AND  "U_IdLineaPB" = '%LineaPrenecesidad%'
      AND "U_tipLinea" = 'C'
      )
      ELSE
      1
      END  AS  "Cant" FROM DUMMY
      )
      AS "Cantidad" FROM DUMMY ;
    </Query>

      SELECT T0."DocEntry" FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" T1 ON T1."U_serieAP"= T0."Series"
      WHERE T0."U_IdFallec" = '%IDFallecido%'

      )
    </Query>

    <Query ID="ObtenerOrdenServicioFallecido">

      SELECT
      T0."DocEntry" "Número Interno",
      T0."U_IdContra" "Id Contratante",
      T0."U_NombreCo" "Contratante",
      T0."U_IdFallec" "Identificación Fallecido",
      T0."U_NombreFa" "Nombre Fallecido",
      T0."U_FechaFal" "Fecha Fallecimiento",
      (SELECT "Name" FROM "@OK1_SF_ESTLLAMADA" WHERE "Code" = T0."U_Estado") AS "Estado"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T0
      WHERE T0."U_IdFallec" = '%IdFallec%'

      ORDER BY T0."DocEntry" DESC

    </Query>

    <Query ID="ObtenerCamposCabeceraOSF" remarks="">
      SELECT COLUMN_NAME

      'U_Preparador','U_NCertDef','U_MCertDef','U_sedeVel', 'U_sedeVelNom','U_VLugar','U_nVLugar','U_multSalas','U_deptVel','U_ndeptVel','U_munVel','U_nmunVel','U_dirVel','U_VFecha','U_horVelEntr',
      'U_VFechaS','U_horVelSal','U_VSerVir','U_VSalaR','U_nVSalaR','U_multSalasR','U_VFechaR','U_horVelEntRe','U_VFechaSalR','U_horVelSalRe','U_VlabPltBaj','U_nVlabPltBaj','U_VFechaPltBaj',
      'U_horVelEntPltBaj','U_VFechaSalPltBaj','U_horVelSalPltBaj','U_tipoMisa','U_sedeMisa','U_sedeExeNom','U_ELugar','U_EDpto','U_nEDpto','U_EMuni','U_nEMuni','U_EFecha','U_EHora','U_horFiMisa',
      'U_dirMisa','U_PfiReli','U_tipoSacerd','U_sacerdMisa','U_nomSacerMisa','U_CoroMisa','U_catalFmisa','U_Homilia','U_misaConme','U_FechaTentMisaConm','U_docMisaConm','U_IUbic','U_nIUbic',
      'U_IDpto','U_nIDpto','U_IMuni','U_nIMuni','U_ILugar','U_nILugar','U_IFecha','U_IHora','U_sFecha','U_SHora','U_TUbicacion','U_SUbicacion','U_IdPropietario','U_NomPropietario','U_horVelSalExc',
      'U_VFechaSalExc', 'U_horVelEntExc', 'U_VFechaExc', 'U_VsalaExc', 'U_nVSalaExc', 'U_multSalasE', 'U_OfiReli'	);
    </Query>

    <Query ID="ObtenerConsultaValoresCabeceraOSF" remarks="">

      DO
      BEGIN

      DECLARE Columnas NCLOB;
      DECLARE Consulta NCLOB;

      SELECT STRING_AGG(CONCAT('"', "COLUMN_NAME"),'",') INTO Columnas
      FROM SYS.TABLE_COLUMNS
      WHERE SCHEMA_NAME = '%Esquema%' AND TABLE_NAME = '@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD';

      Columnas:=CONCAT(Columnas,'"');

      Consulta :=
      (
      ' SELECT ' || Columnas || ' FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" WHERE "DocEntry"= ' || '''%DocEntry%'''
      );

      SELECT Consulta AS Consulta FROM DUMMY;

      END;
    </Query>

      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T1 ON T0."U_OK1_SF_NSOL" = T1."DocEntry" AND T1."U_IdLinea"=T0."U_referencia"

    <Query ID="ActualizarHistoricoCondonacionPrenecesidad" remarks="">
      UPDATE "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM" SET "U_Condonacion"='%ValorCondonado%' WHERE "U_IdLinea" = '%LineaPrenecesidad%' AND "DocEntry" ='%DocEntryPrenecesidad%'
