    <Query ID="KilometrajeFinal" remarks="">
      DO
      BEGIN

      DECLARE DocEntryVehiculoPrincipal INTEGER;
      DECLARE DocEntryVehiculoAlternativo INTEGER;
      DECLARE CantidadResultados INTEGER;

      IF('%Vehiculo%' NOT LIKE '')
      THEN
      SELECT COUNT(TR."DocEntry") INTO CantidadResultados FROM
      (
      SELECT MAX(IFNULL(T0."U_kmFinal",'0')) km, T0."DocEntry"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA"  T0
      WHERE
      T0."U_vehiAsig" ='%Vehiculo%'
      GROUP BY T0."DocEntry"
      ) TR WHERE IFNULL(TR."KM",'0')!='0';
      IF (:CantidadResultados > 0)
      THEN
      SELECT TOP 1 TR."DocEntry" INTO DocEntryVehiculoPrincipal FROM
      (
      SELECT MAX(IFNULL(T0."U_kmFinal",'0')) km, T0."DocEntry"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA"  T0
      WHERE
      T0."U_vehiAsig" ='%Vehiculo%'
      GROUP BY T0."DocEntry"
      ) TR WHERE IFNULL(TR."KM",'0')!='0' AND TR."DocEntry" != '%DocEntry%'
      ORDER BY TR."DocEntry" DESC;

      ELSE
      SELECT '0' INTO DocEntryVehiculoPrincipal FROM DUMMY;
      END IF;
      END IF;

      IF('%Vehiculo%' NOT LIKE '')
      THEN
      SELECT COUNT(TR."DocEntry") INTO CantidadResultados FROM
      (
      SELECT MAX(IFNULL(T0."U_kmFinal2",'0')) km, T0."DocEntry"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA"  T0
      WHERE
      T0."U_vehi2Asig" = '%Vehiculo%'
      GROUP BY T0."DocEntry"
      ) TR WHERE IFNULL(TR."KM",'0')!='0';
      IF (:CantidadResultados > 0)
      THEN
      SELECT TOP 1 TR."DocEntry" INTO DocEntryVehiculoAlternativo FROM
      (
      SELECT MAX(IFNULL(T0."U_kmFinal2",'0')) km, T0."DocEntry"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA"  T0
      WHERE
      T0."U_vehi2Asig" = '%Vehiculo%'
      GROUP BY T0."DocEntry"
      ) TR WHERE IFNULL(TR."KM",'0')!='0' AND TR."DocEntry" != '%DocEntry%'
      ORDER BY TR."DocEntry" DESC;
      ELSE
      SELECT '0' INTO DocEntryVehiculoAlternativo FROM DUMMY;
      END IF;
      END IF;

      IF (IFNULL(:DocEntryVehiculoPrincipal,'0') &gt;= IFNULL(:DocEntryVehiculoAlternativo,'0'))
      THEN
      SELECT T0."U_kmFinal" AS KM ,T0."DocEntry" FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA" T0
      WHERE T0."DocEntry"=DocEntryVehiculoPrincipal;
      ELSE
      SELECT T0."U_kmFinal2" AS KM,T0."DocEntry" FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA" T0
      WHERE T0."DocEntry"=DocEntryVehiculoAlternativo;
      END IF;
      END;
    </Query>

    <Query ID="DisponibilidadConductores" remarks="">
      SELECT "DocEntry" AS "Orden de traslado", (SELECT T0."CardName" FROM OCRD T0 WHERE T0."CardCode" = "U_cond2Asig") AS "Conductor Asignado", "U_fecIniProgra" AS "Fecha inicio", "U_horIniProgra" AS "Hora incial", "U_fecFinProgra" AS "Fecha final", "U_horFinProgra" AS "Hora final",
      (CASE WHEN ("U_horIniProgra" BETWEEN '%HoraInicial%' AND '%HoraFinal%')  OR ("U_horFinProgra" BETWEEN '%HoraInicial%' AND '%HoraFinal%') THEN 'Si' ELSE 'No' END) AS "Simultaneo"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA"
      WHERE ("U_fecIniProgra" BETWEEN '%FechaInicial%' AND '%FechaFinal%') AND ("U_fecFinProgra" BETWEEN '%FechaInicial%' AND '%FechaFinal%')
      AND "U_liberar" NOT LIKE 'Y' AND IFNULL("U_cond2Asig",'') != '' AND "U_estOT" NOT IN ('Cerrado', 'Anulado', 'Cancelado')

      UNION ALL

      SELECT "DocEntry" AS "Orden de traslado", (SELECT T0."CardName" FROM OCRD T0 WHERE T0."CardCode" = "U_condAsig") AS "Conductor Asignado", "U_fecIniProgra" AS "Fecha inicio", "U_horIniProgra" AS "Hora incial", "U_fecFinProgra" AS "Fecha final", "U_horFinProgra" AS "Hora final",
      (CASE WHEN ("U_horIniProgra" BETWEEN '%HoraInicial%' AND '%HoraFinal%')  OR ("U_horFinProgra" BETWEEN '%HoraInicial%' AND '%HoraFinal%') THEN 'Si' ELSE 'No' END) AS "Simultaneo"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA"
      WHERE ("U_fecIniProgra" BETWEEN '%FechaInicial%' AND '%FechaFinal%') AND ("U_fecFinProgra" BETWEEN '%FechaInicial%' AND '%FechaFinal%')
      AND "U_liberar" NOT LIKE 'Y' AND IFNULL("U_condAsig",'') != '' AND "U_estOT" NOT IN ('Cerrado', 'Anulado', 'Cancelado')
    </Query>

    <Query ID="DisponibilidadVehiculos" remarks="">
      SELECT "DocEntry" AS "Orden de traslado", "U_vehi2Asig" AS "Vehiculo asignado", "U_fecIniProgra" AS "Fecha inicio", "U_horIniProgra" AS "Hora incial", "U_fecFinProgra" AS "Fecha final", "U_horFinProgra" AS "Hora final",
      (CASE WHEN ("U_horIniProgra" BETWEEN '%HoraInicial%' AND '%HoraFinal%')  OR ("U_horFinProgra" BETWEEN '%HoraInicial%' AND '%HoraFinal%') THEN 'Si' ELSE 'No' END) AS "Simultaneo"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA"
      WHERE ("U_fecIniProgra" BETWEEN '%FechaInicial%' AND '%FechaFinal%') AND ("U_fecFinProgra" BETWEEN '%FechaInicial%' AND '%FechaFinal%')
      AND "U_liberar" NOT LIKE 'Y' AND IFNULL("U_vehi2Asig",'') != ''  AND "U_estOT" NOT IN ('Cerrado', 'Anulado', 'Cancelado')

      UNION ALL

      SELECT "DocEntry" AS "Orden de traslado", "U_vehiAsig" AS "Vehiculo asignado", "U_fecIniProgra" AS "Fecha inicio", "U_horIniProgra" AS "Hora incial", "U_fecFinProgra" AS "Fecha final", "U_horFinProgra" AS "Hora final",
      (CASE WHEN ("U_horIniProgra" BETWEEN '%HoraInicial%' AND '%HoraFinal%')  OR ("U_horFinProgra" BETWEEN '%HoraInicial%' AND '%HoraFinal%') THEN 'Si' ELSE 'No' END) AS "Simultaneo"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA"
      WHERE ("U_fecIniProgra" BETWEEN '%FechaInicial%' AND '%FechaFinal%') AND ("U_fecFinProgra" BETWEEN '%FechaInicial%' AND '%FechaFinal%')
      AND "U_liberar" NOT LIKE 'Y' AND IFNULL("U_vehiAsig",'') != ''  AND "U_estOT" NOT IN ('Cerrado', 'Anulado', 'Cancelado')
    </Query>

    <Query ID="DisponibilidadConductoresCreacionOT" remarks="">
      SELECT TOP 1 * FROM (
      SELECT "DocEntry" AS "Orden de traslado", (SELECT T0."CardName" FROM OCRD T0 WHERE T0."CardCode" = "U_cond2Asig") AS "Conductor Asignado", "U_fecIniProgra" AS "Fecha inicio", "U_horIniProgra" AS "Hora incial", "U_fecFinProgra" AS "Fecha final", "U_horFinProgra" AS "Hora final",
      (CASE WHEN ("U_horIniProgra" &lt;= '%HoraFinal%')  AND ("U_horFinProgra" &gt;= '%HoraInicial%') THEN 'Si' ELSE 'No' END) AS "Simultaneo"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA"
      WHERE ("U_fecIniProgra" BETWEEN '%FechaInicial%' AND '%FechaFinal%') AND ("U_fecFinProgra" BETWEEN '%FechaInicial%' AND '%FechaFinal%')
      AND "U_liberar" NOT LIKE 'Y' AND IFNULL("U_cond2Asig",'') != '' AND "U_estOT" NOT IN ('Cerrado', 'Anulado', 'Cancelado')

      UNION ALL

      SELECT "DocEntry" AS "Orden de traslado", (SELECT T0."CardName" FROM OCRD T0 WHERE T0."CardCode" = "U_condAsig") AS "Conductor Asignado", "U_fecIniProgra" AS "Fecha inicio", "U_horIniProgra" AS "Hora incial", "U_fecFinProgra" AS "Fecha final", "U_horFinProgra" AS "Hora final",
      (CASE WHEN ("U_horIniProgra" &lt;= '%HoraFinal%')  AND ("U_horFinProgra" &gt;= '%HoraInicial%') THEN 'Si' ELSE 'No' END) AS "Simultaneo"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA"
      WHERE ("U_fecIniProgra" BETWEEN '%FechaInicial%' AND '%FechaFinal%') AND ("U_fecFinProgra" BETWEEN '%FechaInicial%' AND '%FechaFinal%')
      AND "U_liberar" NOT LIKE 'Y' AND IFNULL("U_condAsig",'') != '' AND "U_estOT" NOT IN ('Cerrado', 'Anulado', 'Cancelado')
      ) TR WHERE TR."Simultaneo" = 'Si' AND TR."Conductor Asignado" IN ((SELECT T0."CardName" FROM OCRD T0 WHERE T0."CardCode" = '%ConductorPrincipal%'),(SELECT T0."CardName" FROM OCRD T0 WHERE T0."CardCode" = '%ConductorApoyo%'))
      AND TR."Orden de traslado" != '%DocEntryOT%'
      ORDER BY TR."Orden de traslado" DESC
    </Query>

    <Query ID="DisponibilidadVehiculosCreacionOT" remarks="">
      SELECT TOP 1 * FROM (
      SELECT "DocEntry" AS "Orden de traslado", "U_vehi2Asig" AS "Vehiculo asignado", "U_fecIniProgra" AS "Fecha inicio", "U_horIniProgra" AS "Hora incial", "U_fecFinProgra" AS "Fecha final", "U_horFinProgra" AS "Hora final",
      (CASE WHEN ("U_horIniProgra" &lt;= '%HoraFinal%' )  AND ("U_horFinProgra" &gt;= '%HoraInicial%') THEN 'Si' ELSE 'No' END) AS "Simultaneo"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA"
      WHERE ("U_fecIniProgra" BETWEEN '%FechaInicial%' AND '%FechaFinal%') AND ("U_fecFinProgra" BETWEEN '%FechaInicial%' AND '%FechaFinal%')
      AND "U_liberar" NOT LIKE 'Y' AND IFNULL("U_vehi2Asig",'') != '' AND "U_estOT" NOT IN ('Cerrado', 'Anulado', 'Cancelado')

      UNION ALL

      SELECT "DocEntry" AS "Orden de traslado", "U_vehiAsig" AS "Vehiculo asignado", "U_fecIniProgra" AS "Fecha inicio", "U_horIniProgra" AS "Hora incial", "U_fecFinProgra" AS "Fecha final", "U_horFinProgra" AS "Hora final",
      (CASE WHEN ("U_horIniProgra" &lt;= '%HoraFinal%' )  AND ("U_horFinProgra" &gt;= '%HoraInicial%') THEN 'Si' ELSE 'No' END) AS "Simultaneo"
      FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA"
      WHERE ("U_fecIniProgra" BETWEEN '%FechaInicial%' AND '%FechaFinal%') AND ("U_fecFinProgra" BETWEEN '%FechaInicial%' AND '%FechaFinal%')
      AND "U_liberar" NOT LIKE 'Y' AND IFNULL("U_vehiAsig",'') != '' AND "U_estOT" NOT IN ('Cerrado', 'Anulado', 'Cancelado')

      ORDER BY TR."Orden de traslado" DESC
    </Query>
    <Query ID="consultarOrdenLaboratorioDirectas">
      SELECT "DocEntry" AS "Nº Orden",
      "U_fechaCreaOL" AS "Fecha",
      "U_horaCreaOL" AS "Hora",
      "U_idFallec" AS "Id. Fallecido",
      "U_nombreFalle" AS "Nombre Fallecido",
      (SELECT USER_CODE FROM OUSR WHERE USERID = "UserSign") AS "Usuario",
      "U_estadoOL"  AS "Estado" FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENLAB" WHERE "U_TipoOrden" = 'Directa' AND
      "U_estadoOL" NOT IN ('CERRADA', 'CANCELADA', 'ANULADA') AND
      "U_vinculadoOSF" = 'N'
      ORDER BY "DocEntry" DESC

    </Query>

    </Query>

    <Query ID="EstadosOT" remarks="">

      SELECT REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'Abierto') AS ESTADO, REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'Abierto') AS ESTADO FROM DUMMY


      UNION ALL


      SELECT REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'Programado') , REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'Programado') FROM DUMMY


      UNION ALL


      SELECT REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'En Proceso') , REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'En Proceso')  FROM DUMMY


      UNION ALL


      SELECT REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'Ejecutado') , REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'Ejecutado') FROM DUMMY


      UNION ALL


      SELECT REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'Cerrado') , REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'Cerrado') FROM DUMMY


      UNION ALL


      SELECT REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'Cancelado') , REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'Cancelado') FROM DUMMY


      UNION ALL


      SELECT REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'Anulado') , REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'Anulado')  FROM DUMMY

    </Query>

    <Query ID="verificarLineasOrdenesObligatoriasOC">
      SELECT DISTINCT T1."U_IdLinea",T1."U_ItemCode"
      FROM   "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T1
      ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
      LEFT JOIN OITM T2
      ON T1."U_ItemCode" = T2."ItemCode"
      WHERE  IFNULL(T2."U_OK1_SF_ObligaOC", 'N') = 'Y'
      AND IFNULL(T1."U_Documento", '') = ''
      AND IFNULL(T1."U_docOrd", '') = ''
      AND IFNULL(T1."U_Renuncia", 'N') = 'N'
      AND T0."DocEntry" = '%numOSF%'
      AND T1."U_Codigo" IN ( SELECT  "Code" FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC"
      WHERE "U_traslado" = 'N') AND "U_tipOrden" = 'OC'
    </Query>

    <Query ID="verificarLineasOrdenesObligatoriasOS">
      SELECT DISTINCT T1."U_IdLinea",T1."U_ItemCode"
      FROM   "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T1
      ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
      LEFT JOIN OITM T2
      ON T1."U_ItemCode" = T2."ItemCode"
      WHERE  IFNULL(T2."U_OK1_SF_ObligaOC", 'N') = 'Y'
      AND IFNULL(T1."U_Documento", '') = ''
      AND IFNULL(T1."U_docOrd", '') = ''
      AND IFNULL(T1."U_Renuncia", 'N') = 'N'
      AND T0."DocEntry" = '%numOSF%'
      AND T1."U_Codigo" IN ( SELECT  "Code" FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC"
      WHERE "U_traslado" = 'N') AND "U_tipOrden" = 'OS'
    </Query>

    <Query ID="UbicacionAsignadaOP">

      T1."DocEntry"
      FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" T0
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_PQC_OPARELM_LIN" T1 ON T0."DocEntry" =T1."DocEntry"

    </Query>

      FROM OITB T0  INNER JOIN OITM T1 ON T0."ItmsGrpCod" = T1."ItmsGrpCod" WHERE T1."ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="existen_OPVinculadosaOSFEnEstadoErrado">
      SELECT T1."DocEntry" FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARELM_LIN" T0 INNER JOIN  "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" T1 ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
      WHERE T1."U_NoOSF" = '%nroOSF%'
      AND  IFNULL("U_LineaSF",'0') != '0'
      AND T0."U_NoContr_Prenec" != T1."U_NumContr"
      AND T1."U_Estado" != '01'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="existen_DocumentosOPVinculados">
      SELECT T1."DocEntry" FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARELM_LIN" T0 INNER JOIN  "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" T1 ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
      WHERE T1."U_NoOSF" =  '%nroOSF%'
      AND  IFNULL("U_LineaSF",'0') != '0'
      AND T0."U_NoContr_Prenec" != T1."U_NumContr"
      AND
      (
      IFNULL("U_Documento", '') != ''
      OR IFNULL("U_docOrd", '') != ''
      OR IFNULL("U_NoFactura", '') != ''
      OR IFNULL("U_Pagado", '') != ''
      OR IFNULL("U_detUbi", '') != ''
      OR IFNULL("U_Uso", 'N') = 'Y'
      OR IFNULL("U_Renuncia", 'N') = 'Y'
      )
    </Query>
    <Query ID="eliminar_lineaCedidaVinculadaOP">
      DELETE FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARELM_LIN" WHERE "DocEntry" IN (
      SELECT T0."DocEntry" FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARELM_LIN" T0 INNER JOIN "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" T1 ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
      WHERE T1."U_NoOSF" =  '%nroOSF%'
      ) AND IFNULL("U_LineaSF",'0') = '%idLinea%'
    </Query>
    <Query ID="consultar_OPCedidaVinculada">

      WHERE T1."U_NoOSF" = '%nroOSF%' AND IFNULL("U_LineaSF",'0') = '%idLinea%'
    </Query>

<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<TQueries>
	<Queries>

		<Query ID="BuscarActividadesLaboratorio">
			SELECT T0."ClgCode" AS "Código", CASE T0."Action" WHEN 'C' THEN 'Llamada' WHEN 'M' THEN 'Reunion' WHEN 'T' THEN 'Tarea' WHEN 'E' THEN 'Nota' ELSE 'Otro' END AS "Actividad", T1."U_NAME" AS "Asignado a", T0."Recontact" AS "Fecha", T0."Details" AS "Comentario"
			FROM OCLG T0 INNER JOIN OUSR T1
			ON T0."AttendUser" = T1.USERID
			WHERE T0."U_DocEntry" = '%idSolicitud%' AND "CntctType" = (SELECT "Code" FROM OCLT WHERE "U_tipoAct" = 'L')
		</Query>

		<Query ID="old_elementosOrdenLaboratorioListaMateriales">
			SELECT
			T0."Code" AS "Articulo",
			T0."Qauntity" As "Cantidad",
			T3."ItemName" As "NombreArticulo",
			T3."CardCode" AS "Proveedor",
			T3."InvntItem" AS "InvntItem",
			T0."ToWH" AS "WhsCode",
			(SELECT "OnOrder" FROM OITW WHERE "ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%' AND "WhsCode" =T0."ToWH") AS "Pedido",
			(SELECT "OnHand" FROM OITW WHERE "ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%' AND "WhsCode" =T0."ToWH") AS "Stock",
			(SELECT "IsCommited" FROM OITW WHERE "ItemCode"= '%ItemCode%' AND "WhsCode" =T0."ToWH") AS "Comprometido",

			T3."U_OK1_SF_ELEM" AS "Elemento",
			(SELECT "Name" FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" WHERE "Code" = T3."U_OK1_SF_ELEM")AS "NomElemento",
			(SELECT "U_cofre" FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" WHERE "Code" = T3."U_OK1_SF_ELEM")AS "Cofre"

			FROM OITT T0
			INNER JOIN OITM T3 ON T0."Code" = T3."ItemCode"
			WHERE "U_tipListSF" = 'L'
			AND T0."Code"= '%ItemCode%'

			union

			(SELECT
			T0."Code"AS "Articulo",
			T0."Quantity"As "Cantidad",
			T3."ItemName" As "NombreArticulo",
			T3."CardCode" AS "Proveedor",
			T3."InvntItem" AS "InvntItem",
			T0."Warehouse" AS "WhsCode",
			(SELECT "OnOrder" FROM OITW WHERE "ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%' AND "WhsCode" =T0."Warehouse") AS "Pedido",
			(SELECT "OnHand" FROM OITW WHERE "ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%' AND "WhsCode" =T0."Warehouse") AS "Stock",
			(SELECT "IsCommited" FROM OITW WHERE "ItemCode"= '%ItemCode%' AND "WhsCode" =T0."Warehouse") AS "Comprometido",
			T3."U_OK1_SF_ELEM" AS "Elemento",
			(SELECT "Name" FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" WHERE "Code" = T3."U_OK1_SF_ELEM")AS "NomElemento",
			(SELECT "U_cofre" FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" WHERE "Code" = T3."U_OK1_SF_ELEM")AS "Cofre"

			FROM ITT1 T0
			INNER JOIN OITM T3 ON T0."Code" = T3."ItemCode"
			WHERE T0."Father"= '%ItemCode%')
		</Query>


		<Query ID="elementosOrdenLaboratorioListaMateriales">
			SELECT
			T0."Code" AS "Articulo",
			T0."Qauntity" As "Cantidad",
			T3."ItemName" As "NombreArticulo",
			T3."CardCode" AS "Proveedor",
			T3."InvntItem" AS "InvntItem",
			T0."ToWH" AS "WhsCode",
			(SELECT "OnOrder" FROM OITW WHERE "ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%' AND "WhsCode" =T0."ToWH") AS "Pedido",
			(SELECT "OnHand" FROM OITW WHERE "ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%' AND "WhsCode" =T0."ToWH") AS "Stock",
			(SELECT "IsCommited" FROM OITW WHERE "ItemCode"= '%ItemCode%' AND "WhsCode" =T0."ToWH") AS "Comprometido",

			T3."U_OK1_SF_ELEM" AS "Elemento",
			(SELECT "Name" FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" WHERE "Code" = T3."U_OK1_SF_ELEM")AS "NomElemento",
			(SELECT "U_cofre" FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" WHERE "Code" = T3."U_OK1_SF_ELEM")AS "Cofre"

			FROM OITT T0
			INNER JOIN OITM T3 ON T0."Code" = T3."ItemCode"
			WHERE "U_tipListSF" = 'L'
			AND T0."Code"= '%ItemCode%'

			UNION ALL

			( SELECT
			T0."Code"AS "Articulo",
			T0."Quantity"As "Cantidad",
			T3."ItemName" As "NombreArticulo",
			T3."CardCode" AS "Proveedor",
			T3."InvntItem" AS "InvntItem",
			T0."Warehouse" AS "WhsCode",
			(SELECT "OnOrder" FROM OITW WHERE "ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%' AND "WhsCode" =T0."Warehouse") AS "Pedido",
			(SELECT "OnHand" FROM OITW WHERE "ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%' AND "WhsCode" =T0."Warehouse") AS "Stock",
			(SELECT "IsCommited" FROM OITW WHERE "ItemCode"= '%ItemCode%' AND "WhsCode" =T0."Warehouse") AS "Comprometido",
			T3."U_OK1_SF_ELEM" AS "Elemento",
			(SELECT "Name" FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" WHERE "Code" = T3."U_OK1_SF_ELEM")AS "NomElemento",
			(SELECT "U_cofre" FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" WHERE "Code" = T3."U_OK1_SF_ELEM")AS "Cofre"

			FROM ITT1 T0
			INNER JOIN OITM T3 ON T0."Code" = T3."ItemCode"
			WHERE T0."Father"= '%ItemCode%')

		</Query>

		<Query ID="RetornarCantidadArticuloPadreListaMateriales">
			SELECT
			T0."Qauntity" As "Cantidad",
			FROM OITT T0
			INNER JOIN OITM T3 ON T0."Code" = T3."ItemCode"
			WHERE "U_tipListSF" = 'L'
			AND T0."Code"= '%ItemCode%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="RetornarCantidadArticuloHijoListaMateriales">
			SELECT
			T0."Quantity"As "Cantidad",
			FROM ITT1 T0
			INNER JOIN OITM T3 ON T0."Code" = T3."ItemCode"
			WHERE T0."Father"= '%ItemCode%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="RetornarCantidadArticuloServicioFunerario">
			SELECT
			T0."U_Cant"
			FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"   T0
			INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" T1 ON T0."U_Codigo" = T1."Code"
			LEFT JOIN  OITM T3 ON T0."U_ItemCode" = T3."ItemCode"
			WHERE T0."DocEntry" ='%DocEntry%'
			AND  T1."U_laboratorio" = 'Y'
			AND  T0."ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%'
			AND (IFNULL(T0."U_Renuncia",'N') = 'N' or  T0."U_Renuncia" IN('','N'))
			AND  T0."U_Cant" > 0
		</Query>

		<Query ID="old_RetornarCantidadArticuloServicioFunerario">
			SELECT
			T0."U_Cant"
			FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"   T0
			INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" T1 ON T0."U_Codigo" = T1."Code"
			LEFT JOIN  OITM T3 ON T0."U_ItemCode" = T3."ItemCode"
			WHERE T0."DocEntry" ='%DocEntry%'
			AND  T1."U_laboratorio" = 'Y'
			AND  T0."ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%'
			AND T0."U_Renuncia" IN( '','N',null)
			AND  T0."U_Cant" > 0
		</Query>



		<Query ID="RetornarCantidadArticuloOrdenlaboratorio">

			T1."OnHand"  As "Cantidad",
			T1."OnHand" AS "Stock",
			FROM OITM T0 INNER JOIN  OITW T1 ON T0."ItemCode" = T1."ItemCode" AND "WhsCode" = '%Almacen%'
			WHERE T0."ItemCode"='%ItemCode%'
		</Query>



		<Query ID="ArticuloElementosOrdenlaboratorio">
			SELECT
			T0."ItemCode" AS "Articulo",
			T1."OnHand"  As "Cantidad",
			T0."ItemName" As "NombreArticulo",
			T0."CardCode" AS "Proveedor",
			T0."InvntItem" AS "InvntItem",
			T1."WhsCode" AS "WhsCode",
			T1."OnOrder" AS "Pedido",
			T1."OnHand" AS "Stock",
			T1."IsCommited" AS "Comprometido",
			T0."U_OK1_SF_ELEM" AS "Elemento",
			(SELECT "Name" FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" WHERE "Code" = T0."U_OK1_SF_ELEM")AS "NomElemento",
			(SELECT "U_cofre" FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" WHERE "Code" = T0."U_OK1_SF_ELEM")AS "Cofre"

			FROM OITM T0 INNER JOIN  OITW T1 ON T0."ItemCode" = T1."ItemCode" AND "WhsCode" = '%Almacen%'
			WHERE T0."ItemCode"='%ItemCode%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Retornar_CC_ConvenioSF">
			SELECT
			<!--T0."U_uen",-->
			<!--T0."U_sucur",-->
			T0."U_conve",
			IFNULL(T0."U_cenCosto",''),
			IFNULL(T0."U_cenCosto1",''),
			IFNULL(T0."U_cenCosto2",''),
			IFNULL(T0."U_cenCosto3",'')
			<!--T0."U_conve"-->
			FROM "@OK1_EXE_CONTR_HEAD" T0
			INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T1 ON T1."DocEntry"=T0."DocEntry"
			WHERE T1."DocEntry" = '%NroSolicitudServicio%'

		</Query>



    <Query ID="RetornarSolicitudesLaboratoriopendientes">
      SELECT T0."ClgCode"   AS "NumSolicitudLab",
      CASE T0."Action"
      WHEN 'C' THEN 'Llamada'
      WHEN 'M' THEN 'Reunion'
      WHEN 'T' THEN 'Tarea'
      WHEN 'E' THEN 'Nota'
      ELSE 'Otro'
      END            AS "Actividad",
      T1."U_NAME"    AS "Asignado a",
      T0."Recontact" AS "Fecha",
      T0."Details"   AS "Comentario",
      T2."U_IdFallec" AS "IdFallecido",
      T2."U_NombreFa" AS "NombreFa",
      T0."U_DocEntry" AS "NumeroIntSF",
      T2."U_numListCh" AS "NumListCh",
      T2."U_VSede" AS "SedeServicio",
      (SELECT "name" FROM OCLA T2 WHERE "statusID" = T0."status") AS "Estado",
      T2."U_NCertDef" AS "NumeroCertificado",
      T0."U_tipoLab"AS "TipoLaboratorio",
      T0."U_laboratorio"AS "NomLaboratorio",
      T0."U_depOrigen"AS "DptoOrigen",
      T0."U_munOrigen"AS "MunOrigen",
      T0."U_direcOrg"AS "DirOrigen",
      T2."U_HomEsp" AS "HomEspecial",
      T2."U_crpoCust" AS "CpoCostodia",
      T2."U_Repatr" AS "Repatriacion",
      T0."AttendUser"   AS "AttendUser",
      T0."AssignedBy"   AS "AssignedBy",
      T2."U_CausaFal"   AS "CausaFal",
      T0."Notes"   AS "Notes",
      T2."U_deptVel"   AS "deptVel",
      T2."U_munVel"   AS "munVel",
      T2."U_dirVel"   AS "dirVel",
      T2."DocNum"
      ,T0."CntctSbjct"

      FROM   OCLG T0
      INNER JOIN OUSR T1 ON T0."AttendUser" = T1.USERID
      INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD"  T2  ON T0."U_DocEntry" = T2."DocEntry"
      WHERE T0."ClgCode" = '%IdActividad%'
    </Query>

		<Query ID="RetornarSolicitudesLaborartoriopendientes1">
			SELECT T0."ClgCode"   AS "NumSolicitudLab",
			CASE T0."Action"
			WHEN 'C' THEN 'Llamada'
			WHEN 'M' THEN 'Reunion'
			WHEN 'T' THEN 'Tarea'
			WHEN 'E' THEN 'Nota'
			ELSE 'Otro'
			END            AS "Actividad",
			T1."U_NAME"    AS "Asignado a",
			T0."Recontact" AS "Fecha",
			T0."Details"   AS "Comentario",
			T2."U_IdFallec" AS "IdFallecido",
			T2."U_NombreFa" AS "NombreFa",
			T0."U_DocEntry" AS "NumeroIntSF",
			T2."U_numListCh" AS "NumListCh",
			T2."U_VSede" AS "SedeServicio",
			(SELECT "name" FROM OCLA T2 WHERE "statusID" = T0."status") AS "Estado",
			T2."U_NCertDef" AS "NumeroCertificado"

			FROM   OCLG T0
			INNER JOIN OUSR T1 ON T0."AttendUser" = T1.USERID
			INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD"  T2  ON T0."U_DocEntry" = T2."DocEntry"
			WHERE "CntctType" =  (SELECT "Code" FROM   OCLT WHERE  "U_tipoAct" = 'L')
			AND T0."status" &lt;&gt; -3
		</Query>

		<Query ID="RetornarMensajeUsuarioAlerta">
			SELECT
			DISTINCT( T1."U_Usuario" ) AS "U_Usuario",
			T0."U_Mensaje"
			FROM "@OK1_SF_ALERTA" T0 INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_USUALERTA" T1 ON T0."Code" = T1."U_Alerta"
			WHERE T0."Code" = '%IdAlerta%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="retornarDatosFromAlertaCorregirDatosLaboratorio">
			SELECT
			CASE T0."Action"
			WHEN 'C' THEN 'Llamada'
			WHEN 'M' THEN 'Reunion'
			WHEN 'T' THEN 'Tarea'
			WHEN 'E' THEN 'Nota'
			ELSE 'Otro'
			END            AS "Actividad",
			T1."USER_CODE"    AS "Asignado a"

			FROM   OCLG T0
			INNER JOIN OUSR T1 ON T0."AttendUser" = T1.USERID
			WHERE "ClgCode" = '%IdSolicitudLab%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Retornar_InfoOrdenLaboratorioAnterior">
			SELECT
			"U_solicLab",
			"U_sedeSol",

			"U_TipoLaboratorio",
			"U_NombreLaboratorio",
			"U_DeptoLaboratorio",
			"U_MLugarOrigen",
			"U_DirOrigen",

			"U_numSolSF",
			"U_numListCheq",
			"U_idFallec",
			"U_nombreFalle",
			"U_numCert",

			"U_numContrReceCue",

			"U_sedeDest",
			"U_LugarDestino",
			"U_DirDestino",

			"U_CuerpoRepatriar",
			"U_CuerpCustodia",
			"U_HomenajeEspecial",
			"U_Tanatopraxia"
			FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENLAB"
			WHERE "DocNum" = '%NumeroOLAnterior%'
			AND   "DocNum" NOT IN ( SELECT  "U_NumOrdAnt"  FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENLAB" WHERE "U_TipoOrden" = 'Correccion Interna' OR   "U_TipoOrden" =  'Correccion Externa')
			<!--WHERE "DocEntry" = '%NumeroOLAnterior%'-->
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Retornar_Tanatologo">
			SELECT Count("U_Tanatologo") FROM  "@OK1_SF_TANATOPRALAB"
			WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntry%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Retornar_Ultimo_Tanatologo">
			SELECT "U_Tanatologo"
			FROM  "@OK1_SF_TANATOPRALAB"
			WHERE  "LineId"  = (SELECT  MAX ("LineId") FROM "@OK1_SF_TANATOPRALAB" WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntry%')
			AND  "DocEntry" = '%DocEntry%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="CantidaArticulosSerializado">
			SELECT  SUM (T0."Quantity"), T1."SysNumber" FROM ITl1  T0 INNER JOIN OSRN T1 ON T0."SysNumber"= T1."SysNumber"  AND T0."ItemCode" = T1. "ItemCode"
			WHERE  T0."ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%'
			Group by T1."SysNumber"
		</Query>

		<Query ID="ArticulosSerialesDisponibles">
			SELECT
			T3."ItemCode",
			T3."DistNumber",
			T3."SysNumber",
			T4."Cantidad"
			FROM OSRN T3
			LEFT JOIN (SELECT  SUM (T0."Quantity")AS "Cantidad", T1."SysNumber",T0."ItemCode" FROM ITl1  T0 INNER JOIN OSRN T1 ON T0."SysNumber"= T1."SysNumber"  AND T0."ItemCode" = T1. "ItemCode"
			WHERE  T0."ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%'
			Group by T1."SysNumber", T0."ItemCode") T4 ON T3."ItemCode" =T4."ItemCode" and  T4."SysNumber"= T3."SysNumber"
			WHERE  T4."ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%' AND  T4."Cantidad" > 0
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Retornar_SerieXSede">
			SELECT "U_serieOL" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR"
			WHERE "U_sede" = '%Sede%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Retornar_AlmacenXSede">
			SELECT "U_almacen" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR"
			WHERE "U_sede" = '%Sede%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Retornar_Nro_ControlRecepcionCuerpo">
			SELECT  IFNULL( MAX (CAST("U_numContrReceCue" AS NUMERIC)),0) +1  AS "NroControlCuerpo" FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENLAB"
			WHERE "U_fecReceCue" = '%FechaIngresoCuerpo%' AND "U_NombreLaboratorio" = '%Lab%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Retornar_AlmacenXUsuario">

			WHERE "U_Usuario" = '%User%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Retornar_ActividadesLaboratorio">
			SELECT
			T0."ClgCode" AS "Código",
			CASE T0."Action" WHEN 'C'
			THEN 'Llamada' WHEN 'M'
			THEN 'Reunion' WHEN 'T'
			THEN 'Tarea' WHEN 'E'
			THEN 'Nota'
			ELSE 'Otro'
			END AS "Actividad",
			T1."U_NAME" AS "Asignado a",
			T0."Recontact" AS "Fecha",
			T0."Details" AS "Comentario"
			FROM OCLG T0
			INNER JOIN OUSR T1 ON T0."AttendUser" = T1.USERID
			INNER JOIN OCLT T2 ON T2."Code"=T0."CntctType"
			WHERE
			<!--T2."U_tipoActiv"=2-->
			T2."U_tipoAct" = 'L'
			AND T0."U_EntryOrdenLab"=%NroOrdLab%
		</Query>

    <Query ID="Retornar_ActividadesLaboratorioGen">
		SELECT
		OCLG."ClgCode" AS "Código",
		CASE OCLG."Action" WHEN 'C'
		THEN 'Llamada' WHEN 'M'
		THEN 'Reunion' WHEN 'T'
		THEN 'Tarea' WHEN 'E'
		THEN 'Nota'
		ELSE 'Otro'
		END AS "Actividad",
		CASE
		WHEN OCLG."AssigneeTy" = '12' THEN OUSR."U_NAME"
		WHEN OCLG."AssigneeTy" = '171' THEN OHEM."firstName" || ' ' || OHEM."lastName"
		WHEN OCLG."AssigneeTy" = '234000033' 
		THEN
            CASE RCI1."ObjType"
                WHEN '12' THEN (SELECT "U_NAME" FROM OUSR WHERE "USERID"=RCI1."ObjCode")
                ELSE (SELECT OHEM."firstName" || ' ' || OHEM."lastName" FROM OHEM WHERE "empID"=RCI1."ObjCode")
            END
		WHEN  OCLG."AssigneeTy" = '-1' THEN 'Varios destinatarios'
		END AS "Asignado a",
		OCLG."Recontact" AS "Fecha",
		OCLG."Details" AS "Comentario"
		FROM "OCLG" OCLG
		LEFT JOIN "OUSR" OUSR ON OCLG."AttendUser" = OUSR."USERID" AND OCLG."AssigneeTy" = '12'
		LEFT JOIN "OHEM" OHEM ON OCLG."AttendEmpl" = OHEM."empID" AND OCLG."AssigneeTy" = '171'
		LEFT JOIN "RCI1" RCI1 ON OCLG."AttendReci" = RCI1."Code"  AND OCLG."AssigneeTy" = '234000033'
		WHERE OCLG."U_EntryOrdenLab" = '%NroOrdLab%'
		ORDER BY OCLG."ClgCode";
	</Query>

    <Query ID="Retornar_TipoActividadesLaborarorio">
			SELECT TOP 1 "CntctType"
			FROM OCLG AS T0
			INNER JOIN OCLT AS T1 ON T1."Code"=T0."CntctType" AND T1."U_tipoAct"='L'
		</Query>


		<Query ID="SN_PROVEDORES_TANATOLOGOS">
			SELECT "CardCode","CardName" FROM  OCRD
			WHERE "CardType"= 'S'
			AND "validFor" = 'Y'
			AND ("U_tipProv" =  'Tanatologo'
			OR  "U_tipProv" =  'Tanatologo Tercerizado'
			OR  "U_tipProv" =  'Multifuncional'
			OR  "U_tipProv" =  'Multifuncional Tercerizado')
			ORDER BY "CardName"
		</Query>

		<Query ID="ListasMateriales_tiposcuerpos">
			SELECT T0."Code",T1."ItemName" FROM OITT T0
			INNER JOIN OITM T1 ON T0."Code" = T1."ItemCode"
			WHERE T0."U_tipListSF" = 'L'
			ORDER BY T1."ItemName"
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Deptos_RecursosHumanos">
			SELECT "Code","Name" FROM OUDP
			ORDER BY "Name"
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Empleados_RecursosHumanosALL">
			SELECT  "empID" , "U_nombre" AS "Nombre" FROM OHEM WHERE "Active" = 'Y'
			ORDER BY "Nombre"
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Empleados_RecursosHumanos">
			SELECT  "empID" , "U_nombre" AS "Nombre" FROM OHEM
			WHERE "dept" = '%Depto%'
			ORDER BY "Nombre"
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Territorios_laboratorios">
			SELECT "territryID", "descript"
			FROM OTER WHERE "U_Tipo" = 'S'
			ORDER BY "descript"
		</Query>

    <Query ID="lABORATORIOS">
      SELECT "Code","Name" FROM "@OK1_SF_LABORATORIOS"  WHERE "U_act" = 'Y'
      ORDER BY  "Name"
    </Query>

    <Query ID="Retornar_Contratante_SF">

		</Query>

		<Query ID="ElementosDefectoLaboratorioFrom_SF">
			SELECT
			T0."U_Codigo",
			T0."U_Nombre",
			T0."U_ItemCode",
			T0."U_ItemName",
			T0."U_Uso",
			T0."LineId",
			T0."U_IdLinea",
			T0."DocEntry",
			T0."U_Renuncia",
			T1."U_laboratorio",
			T3."InvntItem",
			T0."U_ArtAdMe",
			T0."U_almacen",
			T0."U_tipOrden",
			T0."U_Cant",

			T0."U_NoContr_Prenec",
			T0."U_IdLineaPF",
			T0."U_Linea",
			T0."U_LineaName",
			T0."U_ItemCodePB",
			T0."U_LineaPadre",
			T0."U_tipLinea",
			T0."U_LineaPadrePN"


			FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"   T0
			INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" T1 ON T0."U_Codigo" = T1."Code"
			LEFT JOIN  OITM T3 ON T0."U_ItemCode" = T3."ItemCode"
			WHERE T0."DocEntry" ='%DocEntry%'
			AND  T1."U_laboratorio" = 'Y'
			AND (IFNULL(T0."U_Renuncia",'N') = 'N' or  T0."U_Renuncia" IN('','N'))
			ORDER BY T0."LineId"
		</Query>

		<Query ID="BorrarElementosDefectoLaboratorioFrom_SF">
			DELETE    FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDELEMLAB"   T0
			WHERE T0."DocEntry" ='%OrdLab%'
			AND T0."U_NroSF" ='%NroServicioFunerario%'
			AND IFNULL(T0."U_Uso",'N')   &lt;&gt;'Y'
			AND IFNULL(T0."U_ObligatorioSF",'N') = 'Y'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Existe_ListaMaterialesEnLaboratorio">
			SELECT  COUNT("Code") FROM  OITT WHERE "U_tipListSF" = 'L' AND "Code" = '%ItemCode%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="ArticulosComprometidosOrdenOrdenLaboratorio">
			SELECT
			T0."DocNum" AS "NroSolicitud",
			T2."WhsCode",
			T1."U_Cant" AS "CantSolicitada",
			T2."OnOrder" AS "Pedido",
			T2."OnHand" AS "Stock",
			T2."IsCommited" AS "Comprometido",
			T1."U_Documento" AS "NroOrden"
			FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENLAB" T0
			INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ORDELEMLAB" T1 ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
			INNER JOIN "OITW" T2 ON T1. "U_ItemCode" = T2."ItemCode"

			AND T1."U_almacen" =  '%Almacen%'
			WHERE (T0."U_estadoOL" &lt;&gt; 'CERRADA' OR  T0."U_estadoOL" &lt;&gt; 'CANCELADA')
			AND T1. "U_ItemCode" =  '%itemCode%'
			AND T0."DocEntry" &lt;&gt; '%docEntry%'
			AND IFNULL(T1."U_Documento" ,'')= ''
			AND 0."U_estadoOL" = 'ABIERTO'
			ORDER  BY T0."DocNum"
		</Query>
		<Query ID="ArticulosDisponiblesOrdenlaboratorio">
			SELECT IFNULL(Sum("OnHand"),0) AS "Cantidad"
			FROM   OITW
			WHERE  "ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%'
			AND "WhsCode" = '%Almacen%'
		</Query>


		<Query ID="oldArticulosDisponiblesOrdenlaboratorio">
			SELECT IFNULL(Sum("Cantidad"), 0) AS "Total"
			FROM   (SELECT IFNULL(Sum("OnHand"),0) AS "Cantidad"
			FROM   OITW
			WHERE  "ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%'
			AND "WhsCode" = '%Almacen%'
			UNION ALL
			SELECT IFNULL(Sum(IFNULL(T0."U_CantRetirar",0)),0) * -1 AS "Cantidad"
			FROM   "@OK1_SF_ORDELEMLAB" T0
			INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ORDENLAB" T1
			ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
			WHERE  T0."U_ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%'
			AND T0."U_almacen" = '%Almacen%'
			AND IFNULL(T0."U_Documento", '') = ''
			AND T0."DocEntry" &lt;&gt; '%DocEntry%'
			AND ( T1."U_estadoOL" NOT IN ('CERRADA','CANCELADA'))
			) TBLA
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Retornar_CC_ConvenioSF">
			SELECT
			T0."U_uen",
			T0."U_sucur",
			IFNULL(T0."U_cenCosto",''),
			IFNULL(T0."U_cenCosto1",''),
			IFNULL(T0."U_cenCosto2",''),
			IFNULL(T0."U_cenCosto3",''),
			T0."U_conve"
			FROM "@OK1_EXE_CONTR_HEAD" T0
			INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T1 ON T1."DocEntry"=T0."DocEntryelementosOrdenLaboratorioListaMateriales"
			WHERE T1."DocEntry" = '%NroSolicitudServicio%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="ActualizarSolicitudLaboratorio">
			UPDATE OCLG
			SET
			"U_motRech" = '%MotivoCancelacion%',
			"U_rechaz" = 'Y',
			"status" = -3,
			"Closed" = 'Y'
			WHERE  "ClgCode" = '%CodeActividad%'
		</Query>


		<Query ID="Retornar_CantidaElementosXActualizarFromSF">
			SELECT
			COUNT (T0."U_Codigo")
			FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"   T0
			INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" T1 ON T0."U_Codigo" = T1."Code"
			LEFT JOIN  OITM T3 ON T0."U_ItemCode" = T3."ItemCode"
			WHERE T0."DocEntry" ='%DocEntrySF%'
			AND  T1."U_laboratorio" = 'Y'
			AND (IFNULL(T0."U_Renuncia",'N') = 'N' or  T0."U_Renuncia" IN('','N'))
			AND T0."U_Codigo" NOT IN  (SELECT "U_Codigo" FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDELEMLAB"
			WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntryOrdLab%'
			AND IFNULL ( "U_NroSF",'')&lt;&gt; '')
			GROUP BY T0."U_Codigo"
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Retornar_ElementosXActualizarFromSF">
			SELECT
			T0."U_ItemCode",
			T0."U_IdLinea",
			T0."U_ArtAdMe",
			T0."U_tipOrden",
			T0."U_Codigo",
			T0."U_Nombre",
			T0."U_Cant",
			T0."U_NoContr_Prenec",
			T0."U_IdLineaPF",
			T0."U_Linea",
			T0."U_LineaName",
			T0."U_ItemCodePB",
			T0."U_LineaPadre",
			T0."U_tipLinea",
			T0."U_LineaPadrePN"

			FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"   T0
			INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" T1 ON T0."U_Codigo" = T1."Code"
			LEFT JOIN  OITM T3 ON T0."U_ItemCode" = T3."ItemCode"
			WHERE T0."DocEntry" ='%DocEntrySF%'
			AND  T1."U_laboratorio" = 'Y'
			AND (IFNULL(T0."U_Renuncia",'N') = 'N' or  T0."U_Renuncia" IN('','N'))

			AND T0."U_Codigo" NOT IN  (SELECT "U_Codigo" FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDELEMLAB"
			WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntryOrdLab%'
			AND IFNULL ( "U_NroSF",'')&lt;&gt; '')
			ORDER BY T0."LineId"
		</Query>


		<Query ID="Retornar_INFO_ElementoSF">
			SELECT
			T0."U_ArtAdMe",
			T0."U_tipOrden",
			T0."U_Codigo",
			T0."U_ItemCode",
			T0."U_Cant"

			FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"   T0
			WHERE T0."DocEntry" ='%NroSF%'
			AND  T0."U_Codigo" = '%elemento%'
			AND  T0."U_IdLinea" = '%lineaSF%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Insertar_Registro_OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HIST">
			INSERT INTO  "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HIST"
			(
			"DocEntry",
			"LineId",
			"Object",
			"U_objeto",
			"U_IdOrden",
			"U_fecRegisto",
			"U_IdUsuario",
			"U_Codigo",
			"U_ItemCode",
			"U_Cant"
			)
			VALUES
			(
			'%DocEntry%',
			'%LineId%',
			'EXE_PRENEC_FINAN',
			'%U_objeto%',
			'%U_IdOrden%',
			'%U_fecRegisto%',
			'%U_IdUsuario%',
			'%U_Codigo%',
			'%U_ItemCode%',
			%U_Cant%
			)
		</Query>

		<Query ID="retornar_DocEntry">
			SELECT IFNULL(MAX("DocEntry"),0) + 1 AS "DocEntry" FROM   "%Tabla%"
			WHERE "DocEntry" = '%Docentry%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="retornar_LineId">
			SELECT IFNULL(MAX("LineId"),0) + 1 AS "LineId" FROM   "%Tabla%"
			WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntry%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="ActualizarUsoServicioFunerario">
			UPDATE "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
			SET  "U_Uso"= '%Uso%'
			WHERE "DocEntry" = '%NroSF%'
			AND  "U_IdLinea" = '%NumeroLineaSF%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Actualizar_EliminarUsoTablaPrenecesidad">
			DELETE FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HIST"
			WHERE "U_IdOrden" = '%U_IdOrden%'
			AND "U_objeto" = '%U_objeto%'
			AND "U_ItemCode" = '%U_ItemCode%'
			<!--AND "U_Linea" = '%U_Linea%'-->
		</Query>

		<Query ID="retornarSysSerialArticulo">
			SELECT "SysSerial"
			FROM "OSRI"
			WHERE "IntrSerial" = '%IntrSerial%'
			AND "ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%'
			AND "WhsCode" = '%WhsCode%'
			AND "Status" = 0
		</Query>

		<Query ID="retornarSysSerialArticuloForCancelacion">
			SELECT "SysSerial"
			FROM "OSRI"
			WHERE "IntrSerial" = '%IntrSerial%'
			AND "ItemCode" = '%ItemCode%'
			AND "WhsCode" = '%WhsCode%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="RetornarServicioFunerariosTipoPrenecesidad">
			SELECT   COUNT ("DocEntry") FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD"
			WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntry%'
			AND "U_TipoSrv" = '5'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="existenLlamadasAsociadasOrdenLaboratorio">
			select IFNULL (COUNT("callID"),0) AS "llamada" from OSCL
			WHERE  "U_EntryOrdenLaboratorio" = '%DocEntryOrdLab%'
		</Query>


		<Query ID="Retornar_InfoTipificacionesLaboratorio">
			SELECT "U_origen","U_tipoPqr","U_pqr","U_proceso","U_subProceso","U_asunto","U_UsuarioNotificar"
			FROM "@OK1_SF_TIPIFPQRLAB"
			WHERE "U_tipOrdenLab" = '%TipoOrdenLab%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Retornar_IDTipoLLamada">
			SELECT "callTypeID" FROM  OSCT  where "Name" = '%TipoLlamada%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="guardarDispoRecurso">
			CALL "OK1_SF_DISPONIBILIDAD_RECURSO"('%nroDoc%', '%CodeRecurso%', '%ObjectType%', '%fechaIni%', '%horaIni%', '%fechaFin%',
			'%horaFin%', '%tipoRecuros%', '%disponible%', '%consulta%');
		</Query>

		<Query ID="verificarDispoRecurso">
			CALL "OK1_SF_DISPONIBILIDAD_RECURSO"('%nroDoc%', '%CodeRecurso%', '%ObjectType%', '%fechaIni%', '%horaIni%', '%fechaFin%',
			'%horaFin%', '%tipoRecuros%', '%disponible%', '%consulta%');
		</Query>

		<Query ID="liberarRecurso">
			CALL "OK1_SF_DISPONIBILIDAD_RECURSO"('%nroDoc%', '%CodeRecurso%', '%ObjectType%', '%fechaIni%', '%horaIni%', '%fechaFin%',
			'%horaFin%', '%tipoRecuros%', '%disponible%', '%consulta%');
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Retornar_PrenecesidadSF">
			SELECT
			T0."U_NoContr_Prenec",
			T0."U_IdLineaPF"
			FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"   T0
			WHERE T0."DocEntry" ='%DocEntrySF%'
			AND "U_IdLinea" = '%lineaSF%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Retornar_Info_Laboratorio">
			SELECT "U_dep","U_mun","U_dir" FROM "@OK1_SF_LABORATORIOS"
			WHERE "Code" ='%CodeLaboratorio%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Retornar_USER_CODE_From_USER">
			select "USER_CODE" from  OUSR  WHERE "USERID" = '%USERID%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Retornar_solicitudLaboratorioDB">
			select "U_solicLab" from  "@OK1_SF_ORDENLAB"  WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntry%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Retornar_TipoSrv">
			select "U_TipoSrv" from "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntry%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Retornar_NroContratoServicio">
			select "U_NumContr"
			from "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntry%'
		</Query>


		<Query ID="Retornar_Convenio_From_PRFIN_HEAD">
			select
			"U_conve",
			"U_sucur",
			IFNULL("U_cenCosto",''),
			IFNULL("U_cenCosto1",''),
			IFNULL("U_cenCosto2",''),
			IFNULL("U_cenCosto3",'')
			from "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD" WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntry%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Retornar_Convenio_From_PRENE_HEAD">
			select
			"U_conve",
			"U_sucur",
			IFNULL("U_cenCosto",''),
			IFNULL("U_cenCosto1",''),
			IFNULL("U_cenCosto2",''),
			IFNULL("U_cenCosto3",'')
			from "@OK1_EXE_PRENE_HEAD" WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntry%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Retornar_Convenio_From_CONTR_HEAD">
			select
			"U_conve",
			"U_sucur",
			IFNULL("U_cenCosto",''),
			IFNULL("U_cenCosto1",''),
			IFNULL("U_cenCosto2",''),
			IFNULL("U_cenCosto3",'')
			from "@OK1_EXE_CONTR_HEAD" WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntry%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="UpdateNroCertificadoYTanatologoOSF">
			UPDATE "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD"
			SET "U_NCertDef"='%NroCert%', "U_Preparador"=(SELECT TOP 1 T0."U_nomTanatologo" FROM "@OK1_SF_TANATOPRALAB" T0 INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_TIPPROCTANAC" T1
			ON TO_ALPHANUM(T0."U_Proceso")=T1."Name" AND T1."U_Tana"='Y'
			WHERE T0."DocEntry"='%DocEntryOL%' ORDER by T0."LineId" DESC)
			WHERE "DocEntry"='%docEntryOSF%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="Retornar_Convenio_CC_From_Servicio">
			SELECT
			T0."U_conve",
			IFNULL(T0."U_cenCosto",''),
			IFNULL(T0."U_cenCosto1",''),
			IFNULL(T0."U_cenCosto2",''),
			IFNULL(T0."U_cenCosto3",'')
			FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T0
			WHERE T0."DocEntry" = '%DocEntry%'
		</Query>
    <Query ID="VerDisponibilidadTanatologo">
      SELECT T0."DocEntry" AS "Orden de Laboratorio", T0."LineId" AS "Linea",
      T0."U_TipoTanatologo" AS "Tipo de Tanatólogo", T0."U_Tanatologo" AS "Doc. Tanatólogo", T0."U_nomTanatologo" AS "Nombre",
      T0."U_FechaIniPrograma" AS "Fecha Inicio Programación", T0."U_HoraIniPrograma" AS "Hora Inicio Programación", T0."U_FechaFinPrograma" AS "Fecha Fin Programación",
      T0."U_HoraFinPrograma" AS "Hora Fin Programación", T0."U_Proceso" AS "Proceso" FROM "@OK1_SF_TANATOPRALAB"  T0 INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ORDENLAB" T1 ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
      WHERE T0."U_FechaIniPrograma" = '%Fecha%' AND "U_estadoOL" NOT IN ('CERRADA','CANCELADA')
    </Query>

    <Query ID="ExisteSolicitudLabServiciosCerrados">
			SELECT
			IFNULL (COUNT (T2."U_Estado"),0) AS "Estado"
			FROM OCLG T0
			INNER JOIN OUSR T1 ON T0."AttendUser" = T1.USERID
			INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T2 ON T0."U_DocEntry" = T2."DocEntry"
			WHERE T0."ClgCode" = '%NroSolicitud%'
			AND "CntctType" = (SELECT
			"Code"
			FROM OCLT
			WHERE "U_tipoAct" = 'L')
			AND T2."U_Estado" = 10

		</Query>

		<Query ID="ExisteServiciosCerradosDocEntrySF">
			select
			IFNULL (COUNT ("U_Estado"),0) AS "Estado"
			from "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD"
			where "DocEntry" = '%DocEntrySolicitud%'
			and "U_Estado" = 10

		</Query>

		<Query ID="ExisteServiciosCerradosDocNumOL">
			SELECT IFNULL (COUNT (T1."U_Estado"),
			0) AS "Estado"
			FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENLAB" T0 INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T1 ON T0."U_numSolSF" = T1."DocEntry"
			WHERE T0."DocNum" = '%DocNumOL%'
			AND T1."U_Estado" = 10

		</Query>

		<Query ID="retornarArticuloCofreSF">
			SELECT "U_ItemCode"
			FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T0
			INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" T1 ON T0."U_Codigo" = T1."Code"
			LEFT JOIN OITM T3 ON T0."U_ItemCode" = T3."ItemCode"
			WHERE T0."DocEntry" ='%DocEntrySF%'
			AND T1."U_laboratorio" = 'Y'
			AND T1."U_cofre" = 'Y'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="retornarOrdenesWhitSolicitudes">
			SELECT  IFNULL(MAX("DocEntry"),0)AS "DocEntry"  FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENLAB"
			WHERE  "U_solicLab" = '%solicitud%'
			AND  "U_estadoOL" &lt;&gt; 'CANCELADA'
			AND  "DocEntry" &lt;&gt; '%DocEntry%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="retornarOrdenesWhitTipoOrden">
			SELECT  IFNULL(MAX("DocEntry"),0)AS "DocEntry"  FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENLAB"
			WHERE  "U_TipoOrden" = '%tipoOrden%'
			AND  "U_estadoOL" &lt;&gt; 'CANCELADA'
			AND  "DocEntry" &lt;&gt; '%DocEntry%'
			AND  "U_numSolSF" = '%DocEntrySF%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="retornarCantidadDocEntry">
			select COUNT("DocEntry")AS "Cant" from "@OK1_SF_ORDENLAB" WHERE "DocEntry" ='%DocEntry%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="RetornarCentroCostoFromElementoPreservacionSF">
			SELECT "U_cenCosto", "U_cenCosto1","U_cenCosto2","U_cenCosto3" FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
			WHERE "U_IdLinea" =
			(
			SELECT "U_ID_Linea" FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDELEMLAB"
			WHERE  "U_Codigo" =
			(
			SELECT TOP 1 T0."Code" from "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" T0
			INNER JOIN  "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T1 ON T0."Code" = T1."U_Codigo"
			WHERE T0."U_cofre" = 'N'
			AND   T0."U_laboratorio" = 'Y'
			AND T1."U_Codigo" IN (SELECT "U_Codigo" FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" WHERE "DocEntry"  =%DocEntrySF% )
			)
			AND "DocEntry" = %DocEntryOL%
			AND  IFNULL("U_ID_Linea",'0') &lt;&gt; 0
			AND "U_NroSF" = '%DocEntrySF%'
			)
			AND "DocEntry" =%DocEntrySF%
		</Query>

		<Query ID="retornarElementoPreservacion">
			SELECT TOP 1 T0."Code" from "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" T0
			INNER JOIN  "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T1 ON T0."Code" = T1."U_Codigo"
			WHERE T0."U_cofre" = 'N'
			AND   T0."U_laboratorio" = 'Y'
			AND T1."U_Codigo" IN (SELECT "U_Codigo" FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" WHERE "DocEntry"  =%DocEntrySF%)
		</Query>

		<Query ID="retornarCentroCostoFromElementoSF">
			SELECT "U_cenCosto", "U_cenCosto1","U_cenCosto2","U_cenCosto3" FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
			WHERE "U_IdLinea" = '%U_IdLineaOL%'
			AND "DocEntry" =%DocEntrySF%
			AND "U_Codigo"&lt;&gt;
			(
			SELECT TOP 1 T0."Code" from "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" T0
			INNER JOIN  "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T1 ON T0."Code" = T1."U_Codigo"
			WHERE T0."U_cofre" = 'N'
			AND   T0."U_laboratorio" = 'Y'
			AND T1."U_Codigo" IN (SELECT "U_Codigo" FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" WHERE "DocEntry"  =%DocEntrySF% ))
		</Query>

		<Query ID="retornarElementoTipoCuerpo">
			SELECT
			TOP 1 T3."U_OK1_SF_ELEM" AS "Elemento"
			FROM ITT1 T0
			INNER JOIN OITM T3 ON T0."Code" = T3."ItemCode"
			WHERE T0."Father"= '%ItemCode%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="retornarCofreConfirmadoFromSF">
			select "U_confirmCofre", "U_ItemCode" FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
			WHERE  "DocEntry" ='%DocEntry%'
			and "U_IdLinea" = '%U_IdLinea%'
		</Query>

		<Query ID="existeCancelacionSalidaMercancia" remarks="">
			SELECT
			T0."DocNum" as "DocNum"
			FROM "OIGN" T0
			INNER JOIN "IGN1" T1 ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"

			WHERE T0."U_OK1_SF_NOL" = '%NOL%'
			AND T1."U_linea" ='%Linea%'
			AND T1."ItemCode" ='%itemCode%'
			AND T0."U_OK1_SF_EntrySM" = '%entrySalidaMercancia%'

		</Query>

		<Query ID="validacionSolicitudTipoLaboratorio" remarks="">
			SELECT "CardCode" FROM "OCLG"
			WHERE "ClgCode"='%Code%'  AND "CntctType" = (SELECT "Code" FROM OCLT WHERE "U_tipoAct" = 'L')
		</Query>

		<Query ID="ObtenerSerieSedeLaboratorio" remarks="">
			SELECT T0."U_serieOL" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" T0
			INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_LABORATORIOS" T1 ON T0."U_serieSI"=T1."U_Serie_SI" AND T1."Code"='%Laboratorio%'
		</Query>
		
		<Query ID="PermisoReabrirOL" remarks="">
			SELECT T0."U_AbrirOL" FROM "@OK1_SF_PERMISOS" T0
			WHERE T0."U_Usuario"='%CodigoUsuario%'
		</Query>
		
		<Query ID="EstadoOSF" remarks="">
			SELECT "U_Estado" FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntryOSF%'
		</Query>
		
		<Query ID="EstadoActualOL" remarks="">
			SELECT "U_estadoOL" FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENLAB" WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntryOL%'
		</Query>
		
		<Query ID="EstadosOL" remarks="">
			SELECT REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'ABIERTO') AS ESTADO, REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'ABIERTO') AS ESTADO FROM DUMMY


			UNION ALL


			SELECT REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'PENDIENTE') , REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'PENDIENTE') FROM DUMMY


			UNION ALL


			SELECT REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'EJECUTADO') , REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'EJECUTADO')  FROM DUMMY


			UNION ALL


			SELECT REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'CERRADO PENDIENTE DE USO') , REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'CERRADO PENDIENTE DE USO') FROM DUMMY


			UNION ALL


			SELECT REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'CERRADA') , REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'CERRADA') FROM DUMMY


			UNION ALL


			SELECT REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'CANCELADA') , REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'CANCELADA') FROM DUMMY


			UNION ALL


			SELECT REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'ANULADA') , REPLACE((SELECT * FROM DUMMY),'X', 'ANULADA') FROM DUMMY
			
		</Query>
		
		<Query ID="UsosOL" remarks="">
			
			SELECT "U_Uso" FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDELEMLAB" WHERE "DocEntry"='%DocEntryOL%' AND "U_Uso" = 'Y'
			
		</Query>
    <Query ID="EstadoOP" remarks="">
      SELECT "U_Estado" FROM "@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD" WHERE "DocEntry" = '%DocEntryOP%'
    </Query>
	<Query ID="ActualizarDatosSM" remarks="">
			
			UPDATE "@OK1_SF_ORDELEMLAB" SET "U_Documento" = '%DocEntryDocumento%', "U_NroSM" = '%NumeroDocumento%' WHERE "DocEntry"='%DocEntry%' AND "LineId"='%NumeroLinea%'
			
		</Query>
  </Queries>

	</Conditions>
</TQueries>

<Procedures>
  <Procedure ID="OK1_SF_DocumentacionIncompleta">
    CREATE PROCEDURE "OK1_SF_DocumentacionIncompleta" AS
    BEGIN
    UPDATE "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD"
    SET    "U_Estado" = '9'
    WHERE  "DocEntry" IN (SELECT DISTINCT T0."DocEntry"
    FROM   "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T0
    INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T1
    ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
    LEFT JOIN OITM T2
    ON T1."U_ItemCode" = T2."ItemCode"
    WHERE  ( IFNULL(T2."InvntItem", 'N') = 'Y'
    OR IFNULL(T2."U_OK1_SF_ObligaOC", 'N') = 'Y' )
    AND IFNULL(T1."U_Documento", '') = ''
    AND IFNULL(T1."U_docOrd", '') = ''
    AND T0."U_Estado" = '1'
    AND T0."UpdateDate" &lt; ADD_DAYS(CURRENT_DATE, -3));
    END;
  </Procedure>
  <Procedure ID="OK1_SF_CerradoPendienteFacturar">
    CREATE PROCEDURE "OK1_SF_CerradoPendienteFacturar" AS
    BEGIN
    UPDATE "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" SET    "U_Estado" = '4' WHERE  "DocEntry" IN
    (SELECT DISTINCT T0."DocEntry"
    FROM   "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T0
    WHERE T0."DocEntry"NOT IN
    ( SELECT DISTINCT T0."DocEntry"
    FROM   "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T0
    INNER JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T1
    ON T0."DocEntry" = T1."DocEntry"
    LEFT JOIN OITM T2
    ON T1."U_ItemCode" = T2."ItemCode"
    WHERE
    (IFNULL(T2."InvntItem", 'N') = 'Y'
    OR IFNULL(T2."U_OK1_SF_ObligaOC", 'N') = 'Y')
    AND
    IFNULL(T1."U_Documento", '') = ''
    AND IFNULL(T1."U_docOrd", '') = ''
    AND T0."U_Estado" = '1'
    AND T0."UpdateDate" &lt; ADD_DAYS(CURRENT_DATE, -3)
    )
    AND T0."U_Estado" = '1'
    AND T0."UpdateDate" &lt; ADD_DAYS(CURRENT_DATE, -3));
    END;
  </Procedure>
