  <Procedure ID="OK1_addInExequialDatosSerFun">
    CREATE PROCEDURE "OK1_addInExequialDatosSerFun"(IN FechaIni DATE,
    IN FechaFin DATE,
    IN Schemas NVARCHAR(50)) AS
    BEGIN DECLARE temp_var_0 INTEGER
    ;
    SELECT
    (SELECT
    count(TABLE_NAME)
    FROM TABLE_COLUMNS
    WHERE TABLE_NAME = 'DATOSERFUN_TEMP'
    AND SCHEMA_NAME=:Schemas)
    INTO temp_var_0
    FROM DUMMY
    ;

    IF :temp_var_0 > 0 THEN
    DROP TABLE "DATOSERFUN_TEMP";
    END
    IF;

    CREATE TABLE "DATOSERFUN_TEMP" (
    "NumSolicitud" INTEGER,
    "U_NumContr" NVARCHAR(200),
    "EstadoSoli" NVARCHAR(100),
    "TipoServicio" NVARCHAR(300),
    "NivelServicio" NVARCHAR(300),
    "U_Pagare" NVARCHAR(1000),
    "U_observ" NVARCHAR(2000),
    "U_Plan" NVARCHAR(1000),
    "U_conve" NVARCHAR(300),
    "U_nomcon" NVARCHAR(200),
    "User" NVARCHAR(155),
    "U_IdContra" NVARCHAR(200),
    "U_NombreCo" NVARCHAR(400),
    "U_DirContr" NVARCHAR(400),
    "U_Ciudad" NVARCHAR(300),
    "U_TelContr" NVARCHAR(200),
    "U_EmailContr" NVARCHAR(200),
    "EstratoContr" INTEGER,
    "BarrioContr" NVARCHAR(200),
    "U_FechaSol" DATE,
    "Sala" NVARCHAR(300),
    "Exequias" NVARCHAR(800),
    "HoraExequias" NVARCHAR(5000),
    "HoraInhumacion" NVARCHAR(5000),
    "CampoSanto" NVARCHAR(800),
    "Destino" NVARCHAR(90),
    "U_sucur" NVARCHAR(300),
    "U_IdFallec" NVARCHAR(200),
    "U_NombreFa" NVARCHAR(400),
    "U_Ubicacio" NVARCHAR(500),
    "Profesion" NVARCHAR(100),
    "EstadoCivil" NVARCHAR(100),
    "Parentesco" NVARCHAR(200),
    "U_Edad" NVARCHAR(3),
    "U_FechaNac" DATE,
    "U_FechaFal" DATE,
    "U_NomPadre" NVARCHAR(100),
    "U_NomMadre" NVARCHAR(100),
    "U_NomCony" NVARCHAR(100),
    "U_NCertDef" NVARCHAR(30),
    "Causafall" NVARCHAR(30),
    "U_Genero" NVARCHAR(1),
    "U_MCertDef" NVARCHAR(100),
    "Nacido" NVARCHAR(30),
    "Fallecido" NVARCHAR(30),
    "NumServicio" INTEGER,
    "Elemento1" NVARCHAR(100),
    "Elemento2" NVARCHAR(100),
    "Elemento3" NVARCHAR(100),
    "Elemento4" NVARCHAR(100),
    "Elemento5" NVARCHAR(100),
    "Elemento6" NVARCHAR(100),
    "Elemento7" NVARCHAR(100),
    "Elemento8" NVARCHAR(100),
    "Valor1" INTEGER,
    "Valor2" INTEGER,
    "Valor3" INTEGER,
    "Valor4" INTEGER,
    "Valor5" INTEGER,
    "Valor6" INTEGER,
    "Valor7" INTEGER,
    "Valor8" INTEGER,
    "Descuento1" INTEGER,
    "Descuento2" INTEGER,
    "Descuento3" INTEGER,
    "Descuento4" INTEGER,
    "Descuento5" INTEGER,
    "Descuento6" INTEGER,
    "Descuento7" INTEGER,
    "Descuento8" INTEGER,
    "ValorFac1" INTEGER,
    "ValorFac2" INTEGER,
    "ValorFac3" INTEGER,
    "ValorFac4" INTEGER,
    "ValorFac5" INTEGER,
    "ValorFac6" INTEGER,
    "ValorFac7" INTEGER,
    "ValorFac8" INTEGER,
    "U_Subtotal" INTEGER,
    "U_TotalDesc" INTEGER,
    "U_TotalIVA" INTEGER,
    "U_TotalDoc" NUMERIC(19,6)
    )
    ;
    INSERT
    INTO "DATOSERFUN_TEMP"( SELECT
    T0."DocNum" "Nº Solicitud",
    T0."U_NumContr" "Contrato",
    (SELECT "Name" FROM "@OK1_SF_ESTLLAMADA" WHERE "Code" = T0."U_Estado") "Estado Solicitud",
    (SELECT
    "Name"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_TIPOSERVICIO"
    WHERE "Code" = T0."U_TipoSrv") "Tipo Servicio",
    (SELECT
    "Name"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_NIVELSERV"
    WHERE "Code" = T0."U_NivServ") "Nivel Servicio",
    T0."U_Pagare",
    T1."U_observ",
    T0."U_Plan",
    T1."U_conve",
    T1."U_nomcon",
    (select
    "U_NAME"
    FROM ousr
    where "USERID" = T0."UserSign") "User",
    T0."U_IdContra",
    T0."U_NombreCo",
    T0."U_DirContr",
    T0."U_Ciudad",
    T0."U_TelContr",
    ( SELECT "U_addInFaElectronica_email_contacto_FE"   FROM   OCRD   WHERE  "CardCode" = T0."U_IdContra")"Email Contratante",
    (SELECT "U_estrato" FROM OCRD WHERE "CardCode" = T0."U_IdContra")"Estrato Contratante",
    (SELECT "U_Barrio" FROM OCRD WHERE "CardCode" = T0."U_IdContra")"Barrio Contratante",
    T0."U_FechaSol",
    (SELECT
    "Name"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_SALAVELAC"
    WHERE "Code" = T0."U_VLugar") "Sala",
    (SELECT
    "U_Nombre"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_IGLESIAS"
    WHERE "Code" = T0."U_ELugar") "Exequias",
    (REPLACE(T0."U_EHora",
    '.',
    ''))"Hora Exequias",
    (REPLACE(T0."U_IHora",
    '.',
    ''))"Hora Inhumacion",
    (SELECT
    "Name"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_CEMENTERIOS"
    WHERE "Code" = T0."U_IUbic") "Campo Santo",
    (CASE T0."U_ILugar" WHEN 'B'
    THEN 'BOVEDA' WHEN 'L'
    THEN 'LOTE' WHEN 'C'
    THEN 'CREMACION'
    ELSE NULL
    END )"Destino",
    T1."U_sucur",
    T0."U_IdFallec",
    T0."U_NombreFa",
    T0."U_Ubicacio",
    (SELECT
    "Name"
    FROM "@OK1_EXE_OCUP"
    WHERE "Code" = T0."U_Ocup") "Profesion",
    (SELECT
    "Name"
    FROM "@OK1_EXE_ESTADOCIVIL"
    WHERE "Code" = T0."U_EstCivil") "Estado Civil",
    (SELECT
    "Name"
    FROM "@OK1_EXE_PARENTESCOS"
    WHERE "Code" = T0."U_Parentes") "Parentesco",
    T0."U_Edad",
    T0."U_FechaNac",
    T0."U_FechaFal",
    T0."U_NomPadre",
    T0."U_NomMadre",
    T0."U_NomCony",
    T0."U_NCertDef",
    (SELECT
    "Name"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_CAUSAFALL"
    WHERE "Code" = T0."U_CausaFal") "Causa fall",
    T0."U_Genero",
    T0."U_MCertDef",
    (SELECT
    "Name"
    FROM "@BPCO_MU"
    WHERE "Code" = T0."U_MuniNac") "Nacido",
    (SELECT
    "Name"
    FROM "@BPCO_MU"
    WHERE "Code" = T0."U_CiudadFa") "Fallecido",
    T0."DocNum" "N° Servicio",
    (SELECT
    "U_Nombre"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 1 )"Elemento 1",
    (SELECT
    "U_Nombre"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 2 )"Elemento 2",
    (SELECT
    "U_Nombre"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 3 )"Elemento 3",
    (SELECT
    "U_Nombre"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 4 )"Elemento 4",
    (SELECT
    "U_Nombre"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 5 )"Elemento 5",
    (SELECT
    "U_Nombre"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 6 )"Elemento 6",
    (SELECT
    "U_Nombre"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 7 )"Elemento 7",
    (SELECT
    "U_Nombre"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 8 )"Elemento 8",
    (SELECT
    "U_Valor"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 1 )"Valor 1",
    (SELECT
    "U_Valor"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 2 )"Valor 2",
    (SELECT
    "U_Valor"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 3 )"Valor 3",
    (SELECT
    "U_Valor"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 4 )"Valor 4",
    (SELECT
    "U_Valor"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 5 )"Valor 5",
    (SELECT
    "U_Valor"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 6 )"Valor 6",
    (SELECT
    "U_Valor"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 7 )"Valor 7",
    (SELECT
    "U_Valor"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 8 )"Valor 8",
    (SELECT
    "U_VlrDto"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 1 )"Descuento 1",
    (SELECT
    "U_VlrDto"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 2 )"Descuento 2",
    (SELECT
    "U_VlrDto"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 3 )"Descuento 3",
    (SELECT
    "U_VlrDto"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 4 )"Descuento 4",
    (SELECT
    "U_VlrDto"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 5 )"Descuento 5",
    (SELECT
    "U_VlrDto"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 6 )"Descuento 6",
    (SELECT
    "U_VlrDto"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 7 )"Descuento 7",
    (SELECT
    "U_VlrDto"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 8 )"Descuento 8",
    (SELECT
    "U_ValorFac"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 1 )"Valor Fac 1",
    (SELECT
    "U_ValorFac"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 2 )"Valor Fac 2",
    (SELECT
    "U_ValorFac"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 3 )"Valor Fac 3",
    (SELECT
    "U_ValorFac"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 4 )"Valor Fac 4",
    (SELECT
    "U_ValorFac"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 5 )"Valor Fac 5",
    (SELECT
    "U_ValorFac"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 6 )"Valor Fac 6",
    (SELECT
    "U_ValorFac"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 7 )"Valor Fac 7",
    (SELECT
    "U_ValorFac"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN"
    WHERE "DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND "LineId" = 8 )"Valor Fac 8",
    T0."U_Subtotal",
    T0."U_TotalDesc",
    T0."U_TotalIVA",
    T0."U_TotalDoc"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" T0
    LEFT JOIN "@OK1_EXE_CONTR_HEAD" T1 ON T0."U_NumContr" = CAST(T1."DocEntry" AS NVARCHAR(20))
    LEFT JOIN "@OK1_SF_ELEM_LIN" T2 ON T2."DocEntry" = T0."DocEntry"
    AND T2."U_Codigo" = 'EP0006'
    WHERE T0."U_FechaSol" BETWEEN :FechaIni AND :FechaFin
    ORDER BY T0."DocNum" )
    ;
    END;
  </Procedure>
  <Procedure ID="OK1_addInSrvFun_BfSolicitudesAnteriores">
    CREATE PROCEDURE "OK1_addInSrvFun_BfSolicitudesAnteriores" (IN p_Sede varchar(30),IN p_TipoServ varchar(1)) AS
    BEGIN
    IF (p_TipoServ = 'C') THEN
    SELECT
    T0."DocEntry",
    (SELECT "SeriesName" FROM NNM1 WHERE "Series" = T0."Series") AS "Series",
    T0."DocNum",
    T0."U_VSede",
    T0."U_VSedeNom",
    CASE  T0."U_tipSolicitud" WHEN  'N' THEN 'NUEVO' WHEN 'C' THEN 'CAMBIO' WHEN 'B' THEN 'BASADO EN' ELSE 'NUEVO' END AS "Tipo Solicitud",
    T0."U_SolAnte",
    CASE  T0."U_TipoSrv" WHEN '1' THEN 'Previsión Exequial' WHEN '2' THEN 'Particular' WHEN '3' THEN 'Reembolsos' WHEN '4' THEN 'De Convenio Nacional' WHEN '5' THEN 'Prenecesidad' END AS "Tipo Servicio",
    T0."U_EntConCl",
    CASE T0."U_TipPrest" WHEN '1' THEN 'Directo'  WHEN '2' THEN 'Convenio Nacional'  WHEN '3' THEN 'Convenios de Provincia'  WHEN '4' THEN 'Tomado de Otra Entidad' END AS "Tipo Prestación",
    T0."U_EntConPr",
    CASE T0."U_NivServ" WHEN '1' THEN 'INICIAL'  WHEN '2' THEN 'FINAL'  WHEN '3' THEN 'COMPLETO'  WHEN '4' THEN 'REEMBOLSOS'  WHEN '5' THEN 'TRASLADO'  WHEN '6' THEN 'OTRAS VENTAS'  WHEN '7' THEN 'MASCOTAS' END AS "Nivel Servicio",
    CASE T0."U_TipoCubr" WHEN '1' THEN 'Integral'  WHEN '2' THEN 'Tradicional'  WHEN '3' THEN 'Particular' END  AS "Tipo Cubrimiento",
    T0."U_IdContra",
    T0."U_NombreCo", T0."U_NumContr", T0."U_Plan", T0."U_IdFallec", T0."U_NombreFa",T0."U_FechaNac", T0."U_FechaFal", T0."U_FechaSol", T0."U_HoraFall",T0."U_Usuario","U_Estado" FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD"  T0
    WHERE "U_VSede" != p_Sede AND T0."Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieOrdenSF" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR") ORDER BY  T0."DocEntry" DESC;
    ELSE
    SELECT
    T0."DocEntry",
    (SELECT "SeriesName" FROM NNM1 WHERE "Series" = T0."Series") AS "Series",
    T0."DocNum",
    T0."U_VSede",
    T0."U_VSedeNom",
    CASE  T0."U_tipSolicitud" WHEN  'N' THEN 'NUEVO' WHEN 'C' THEN 'CAMBIO' WHEN 'B' THEN 'BASADO EN' ELSE 'NUEVO' END AS "Tipo Solicitud",
    T0."U_SolAnte",
    CASE  T0."U_TipoSrv" WHEN '1' THEN 'Previsión Exequial' WHEN '2' THEN 'Particular' WHEN '3' THEN 'Reembolsos' WHEN '4' THEN 'De Convenio Nacional' WHEN '5' THEN 'Prenecesidad' END AS "Tipo Servicio",
    T0."U_EntConCl",
    CASE T0."U_TipPrest" WHEN '1' THEN 'Directo'  WHEN '2' THEN 'Convenio Nacional'  WHEN '3' THEN 'Convenios de Provincia'  WHEN '4' THEN 'Tomado de Otra Entidad' END AS "Tipo Prestación",
    T0."U_EntConPr",
    CASE T0."U_NivServ" WHEN '1' THEN 'INICIAL'  WHEN '2' THEN 'FINAL'  WHEN '3' THEN 'COMPLETO'  WHEN '4' THEN 'REEMBOLSOS'  WHEN '5' THEN 'TRASLADO'  WHEN '6' THEN 'OTRAS VENTAS'  WHEN '7' THEN 'MASCOTAS' END AS "Nivel Servicio",
    CASE T0."U_TipoCubr" WHEN '1' THEN 'Integral'  WHEN '2' THEN 'Tradicional'  WHEN '3' THEN 'Particular' END  AS "Tipo Cubrimiento",
    T0."U_IdContra",
    T0."U_NombreCo", T0."U_NumContr", T0."U_Plan", T0."U_IdFallec", T0."U_NombreFa",T0."U_FechaNac", T0."U_FechaFal", T0."U_FechaSol", T0."U_HoraFall",T0."U_Usuario", T0."U_Estado" FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD"  T0
    WHERE T0."Series" IN (	SELECT "U_serieCotizSF" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE "U_sede" = p_Sede UNION ALL SELECT "U_serieAP" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE "U_sede" =p_Sede )
    AND (T0."U_Estado" = '1' OR (T0."U_Estado" = '6' AND T0."U_MotCierre" IN (SELECT "Code" FROM "@OK1_SF_MOTCANCSERV" WHERE "U_supFecVenc" = 'Y')));
    END IF;
    END;
  </Procedure>
  <Procedure ID="OK1_addInSrvFun_BfArticulosCubiertos">
    CREATE PROCEDURE "OK1_addInSrvFun_BfArticulosCubiertos" (
    IN p_NumConvenio nvarchar(11),
    IN p_CodPlan nvarchar(50),
    IN p_Elemento    nvarchar(50),
    IN p_Sede        nvarchar(30),
    IN p_LineaPren   nvarchar(30),
    IN p_NumContratoL nvarchar(30),
    IN p_NivelServicio nvarchar(30),
    IN p_TipoLinea nvarchar(1),
    IN p_ItemFather nvarchar(50),
    IN p_ItemSon nvarchar(50),
    IN p_lineaElemento nvarchar(15),
    IN p_fecha Date,
    IN p_SedeC      nvarchar(30)
    ) AS

    BEGIN
    DECLARE v_convenio NVARCHAR(11);
    DECLARE v_convenioPrenec NVARCHAR(11);
    DECLARE v_plan     NVARCHAR(50);
    DECLARE v_planPrenec     NVARCHAR(50);
    DECLARE v_TipContrato   NVARCHAR(30);
    DECLARE v_NumContrato   NVARCHAR(30);
    DECLARE v_esBasico   NVARCHAR(1);
    DECLARE v_esSala   NVARCHAR(1);
    DECLARE v_gstPrecios   NVARCHAR(1);

    IF (:p_NumContratoL != '')  THEN

    v_TipContrato = 'Y';
    v_NumContrato = :p_NumContratoL;

    SELECT (SELECT "U_plan" FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD" WHERE "DocEntry" = v_NumContrato) INTO v_planPrenec FROM DUMMY;
    SELECT (SELECT "U_conve" FROM "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_HEAD" WHERE "DocEntry" = v_NumContrato) INTO v_convenioPrenec FROM DUMMY;

    ELSE

    v_TipContrato = 'N';
    v_plan = :p_CodPlan;

    END IF;


    IF (:p_NumConvenio = '') THEN
    v_convenio = p_NumConvenio;

    IF (v_TipContrato = 'N')THEN
    SELECT (SELECT IFNULL("U_elemBasico",'N') FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" WHERE "Code" = p_Elemento) INTO v_esBasico FROM DUMMY;
    SELECT (SELECT IFNULL("U_SalaVel",'N') FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" WHERE "Code" = p_Elemento) INTO v_esSala FROM DUMMY;

    IF (v_esBasico = 'Y')THEN
    SELECT "U_ItemCode" AS "Código de artículo",
    "U_ItemName" AS "Descripción del artículo",
    IFNULL("U_vlrVtaArt",0)  AS "Valor Venta Articulo",
    IFNULL("U_vlrExce",0)  AS "Valor Excedente"
    FROM   "@OK1_EXE_VIGPLNSPRAR" T2
    INNER JOIN OITT T3 ON  T2."U_ItemCode"= T3."Code"
    WHERE  T2."Code" = v_plan
    || '-'
    ||
    (
    SELECT     CAST("U_IdLinea" AS NVARCHAR)
    FROM       "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEFEC" T0
    INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEM" T1
    ON         T0."Code"= T1."Code"
    WHERE      T1."U_codConv" = v_convenio
    AND        T1."U_codPlan" = v_plan
    AND        :p_fecha BETWEEN T0."U_fecini" AND T0."U_fecfin" AND T0."U_nivServ" =  :p_NivelServicio  limit 1)
    AND  (T3."U_sedeSF" = :p_Sede OR IFNULL(T3."U_sedeSF",'') = '')
    AND    T2."U_elemento" = :p_Elemento
    AND  T2."U_ItemCode" IN (SELECT "ItemCode" FROM OITM WHERE  "validFor" = 'Y') ORDER BY T2."U_vlrVtaArt" ASC;
    ELSE
    IF (p_TipoLinea != 'C') THEN
    IF (v_esSala = 'Y')THEN
    SELECT "U_ItemCode" AS "Código de artículo",
    "U_ItemName" AS "Descripción del artículo",
    IFNULL("U_vlrVtaArt",0)  AS "Valor Venta Articulo",
    IFNULL("U_vlrExce",0)  AS "Valor Excedente"
    FROM   "@OK1_EXE_VIGPLNSPRAR" T2
    WHERE  T2."Code" = v_plan
    || '-'
    ||
    (
    SELECT     CAST("U_IdLinea" AS NVARCHAR)
    FROM       "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEFEC" T0
    INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEM" T1
    ON         T0."Code"= T1."Code"
    WHERE      T1."U_codConv" = v_convenio
    AND        T1."U_codPlan" = v_plan
    AND        :p_fecha BETWEEN T0."U_fecini" AND T0."U_fecfin" AND T0."U_nivServ" =  :p_NivelServicio  limit 1)
    AND    T2."U_elemento" = :p_Elemento
    AND  T2."U_ItemCode" IN (SELECT "ItemCode" FROM OITM WHERE  "validFor" = 'Y')
    AND T2."U_ItemCode" IN (SELECT "U_ItemCode" FROM "@OK1_SF_SALAVELAC" WHERE "U_Sede" = :p_Sede  OR "U_Sede" = :p_SedeC)
    ORDER BY T2."U_vlrVtaArt" ASC;
    ELSE
    SELECT "U_ItemCode" AS "Código de artículo",
    "U_ItemName" AS "Descripción del artículo",
    IFNULL("U_vlrVtaArt",0)  AS "Valor Venta Articulo",
    IFNULL("U_vlrExce",0)  AS "Valor Excedente"
    FROM   "@OK1_EXE_VIGPLNSPRAR" T2
    WHERE  T2."Code" = v_plan
    || '-'
    ||
    (
    SELECT     CAST("U_IdLinea" AS NVARCHAR)
    FROM       "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEFEC" T0
    INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEM" T1
    ON         T0."Code"= T1."Code"
    WHERE      T1."U_codConv" = v_convenio
    AND        T1."U_codPlan" = v_plan
    AND        :p_fecha BETWEEN T0."U_fecini" AND T0."U_fecfin" AND T0."U_nivServ" =  :p_NivelServicio  limit 1)
    AND    T2."U_elemento" = :p_Elemento
    AND  T2."U_ItemCode" IN (SELECT "ItemCode" FROM OITM WHERE  "validFor" = 'Y') ORDER BY T2."U_vlrVtaArt" ASC;
    END IF;
    ELSE
    SELECT T1."Code" AS "Código de artículo",
    (SELECT "ItemName" FROM   OITM WHERE  "ItemCode" = T1."Code") AS "Descripción del artículo",
    0 AS "Valor Venta Articulo",
    0  AS "Valor Excedente"
    FROM OITT T0  INNER JOIN ITT1 T1 ON T0."Code" = T1."Father"
    WHERE T0."Code" = :p_ItemFather AND T1."U_OK1_SF_CODELEM" = :p_Elemento AND T1."Code" = :p_ItemSon
    AND  T1."Code" IN (SELECT "ItemCode" FROM OITM WHERE  "validFor" = 'Y');
    END IF;
    END IF;
    ELSE

    SELECT
    T0."ItemCode"            AS "Número de artículo",
    T0."ItemName"            AS "Descripción del artículo",
    T0."U_lineaElem"         AS "Linea del Elemento"
    FROM OITM T0 WHERE
    T0."U_OK1_SF_ELEM" = :p_Elemento
    AND  T0."U_lineaElem" = (SELECT "U_Linea" FROM  "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM"  WHERE  "DocEntry" = :p_NumContratoL AND "U_IdLinea" = :p_LineaPren)
    AND T0."ItemCode" IN (SELECT "ItemCode" FROM OITM WHERE  "validFor" = 'Y')

    UNION ALL

    SELECT
    T0."ItemCode"            AS "Número de artículo",
    T0."ItemName"            AS "Descripción del artículo",
    T0."U_lineaElem"         AS "Linea del Elemento"
    FROM OITM T0 INNER JOIN ITM1 T1 ON T0."ItemCode" = T1."ItemCode"  INNER JOIN  "@OK1_EXE_VIGPLNSPRAR" T2 ON T0."ItemCode" = T2."U_ItemCode"
    WHERE  "PriceList" =  (SELECT "U_lstPrePubli" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE "U_sede" = :p_Sede)
    AND  T0."U_OK1_SF_ELEM" = :p_Elemento
    AND  T1."Price"   &lt;= (SELECT  "U_Valor" FROM   "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM" WHERE   "DocEntry" = :p_NumContratoL AND "U_IdLinea" = :p_LineaPren )
    AND  T0."U_lineaElem" != (SELECT "U_Linea" FROM  "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM"  WHERE  "DocEntry" = :p_NumContratoL AND "U_IdLinea" = :p_LineaPren)
    AND T0."ItemCode" IN (SELECT "ItemCode" FROM OITM WHERE  "validFor" = 'Y')
    AND  T2."Code" = v_convenioPrenec
    || '-'
    || v_planPrenec
    || '-'
    ||
    (
    SELECT     CAST("U_IdLinea" AS NVARCHAR)
    FROM       "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEFEC" T0
    INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEM" T1
    ON         T0."Code"= T1."Code"
    WHERE      T1."U_codConv" = v_convenioPrenec
    AND        T1."U_codPlan" = v_planPrenec
    AND        :p_fecha BETWEEN T0."U_fecini" AND T0."U_fecfin" AND T0."U_nivServ" =  :p_NivelServicio  limit 1)
    AND  T2."U_vlrVtaArt"  = 0

    UNION ALL

    SELECT "U_ItemCode" AS "Código de artículo",
    "U_ItemName" AS "Descripción del artículo",
    T0."U_lineaElem"         AS "Linea del Elemento"
    FROM   "@OK1_EXE_VIGPLNSPRAR" T2 INNER JOIN OITM T0 ON T0."ItemCode" = T2."U_ItemCode"
    WHERE  T2."Code" =  v_convenioPrenec
    || '-'
    || v_planPrenec
    || '-'
    ||
    (
    SELECT     CAST("U_IdLinea" AS NVARCHAR)
    FROM       "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEFEC" T0
    INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEM" T1
    ON         T0."Code"= T1."Code"
    WHERE      T1."U_codConv" = v_convenioPrenec
    AND        T1."U_codPlan" = v_planPrenec
    AND        :p_fecha BETWEEN T0."U_fecini" AND T0."U_fecfin" AND T0."U_nivServ" =  :p_NivelServicio  limit 1)
    AND    T2."U_elemento" = :p_Elemento
    AND  T0."U_lineaElem" != (SELECT "U_Linea" FROM  "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM"  WHERE   "DocEntry" = :p_NumContratoL AND "U_IdLinea" = :p_LineaPren )
    AND  T2."U_vlrVtaArt"   &lt;= (SELECT  "U_Valor" FROM   "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM" WHERE    "DocEntry" = :p_NumContratoL AND "U_IdLinea" = :p_LineaPren )
    AND  T2."U_ItemCode" IN (SELECT "ItemCode" FROM OITM WHERE  "validFor" = 'Y')
    AND  T2."U_vlrVtaArt"  > 0;

    END IF;
    ELSE
    v_convenio = p_NumConvenio;
    IF (v_TipContrato = 'N')THEN
    SELECT (SELECT IFNULL("U_elemBasico",'N') FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" WHERE "Code" = p_Elemento) INTO v_esBasico FROM DUMMY;
    SELECT (SELECT IFNULL("U_SalaVel",'N') FROM "@OK1_EXE_ELEPROTECC" WHERE "Code" = p_Elemento) INTO v_esSala FROM DUMMY;

    IF (v_esBasico = 'Y')THEN
    SELECT "U_ItemCode" AS "Código de artículo",
    "U_ItemName" AS "Descripción del artículo",
    IFNULL("U_vlrVtaArt",0)  AS "Valor Venta Articulo",
    IFNULL("U_vlrExce",0)  AS "Valor Excedente"
    FROM   "@OK1_EXE_VIGPLNSPRAR" T2
    INNER JOIN OITT T3 ON  T2."U_ItemCode"= T3."Code"
    WHERE  T2."Code" = v_convenio
    || '-'
    || v_plan
    || '-'
    ||
    (
    SELECT     CAST("U_IdLinea" AS NVARCHAR)
    FROM       "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEFEC" T0
    INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEM" T1
    ON         T0."Code"= T1."Code"
    WHERE      T1."U_codConv" = v_convenio
    AND        T1."U_codPlan" = v_plan
    AND        :p_fecha BETWEEN T0."U_fecini" AND T0."U_fecfin" AND T0."U_nivServ" =  :p_NivelServicio  limit 1)
    AND  (T3."U_sedeSF" = :p_Sede OR IFNULL(T3."U_sedeSF",'') = '')
    AND    T2."U_elemento" = :p_Elemento
    AND  T2."U_ItemCode" IN (SELECT "ItemCode" FROM OITM WHERE  "validFor" = 'Y') ORDER BY T2."U_vlrVtaArt" ASC;
    ELSE
    IF (p_TipoLinea != 'C') THEN
    IF (v_esSala = 'Y')THEN
    SELECT "U_ItemCode" AS "Código de artículo",
    "U_ItemName" AS "Descripción del artículo",
    IFNULL("U_vlrVtaArt",0)  AS "Valor Venta Articulo",
    IFNULL("U_vlrExce",0)  AS "Valor Excedente"
    FROM   "@OK1_EXE_VIGPLNSPRAR" T2
    WHERE  T2."Code" = v_convenio
    || '-'
    || v_plan
    || '-'
    ||
    (
    SELECT     CAST("U_IdLinea" AS NVARCHAR)
    FROM       "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEFEC" T0
    INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEM" T1
    ON         T0."Code"= T1."Code"
    WHERE      T1."U_codConv" = v_convenio
    AND        T1."U_codPlan" = v_plan
    AND        :p_fecha BETWEEN T0."U_fecini" AND T0."U_fecfin" AND T0."U_nivServ" =  :p_NivelServicio  limit 1)
    AND    T2."U_elemento" = :p_Elemento
    AND  T2."U_ItemCode" IN (SELECT "ItemCode" FROM OITM WHERE  "validFor" = 'Y')
    AND T2."U_ItemCode" IN (SELECT "U_ItemCode" FROM "@OK1_SF_SALAVELAC" WHERE "U_Sede" = :p_Sede  OR "U_Sede" = :p_SedeC)
    ORDER BY T2."U_vlrVtaArt" ASC;
    ELSE
    SELECT "U_ItemCode" AS "Código de artículo",
    "U_ItemName" AS "Descripción del artículo",
    IFNULL("U_vlrVtaArt",0)  AS "Valor Venta Articulo",
    IFNULL("U_vlrExce",0)  AS "Valor Excedente"
    FROM   "@OK1_EXE_VIGPLNSPRAR" T2
    WHERE  T2."Code" = v_convenio
    || '-'
    || v_plan
    || '-'
    ||
    (
    SELECT     CAST("U_IdLinea" AS NVARCHAR)
    FROM       "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEFEC" T0
    INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEM" T1
    ON         T0."Code"= T1."Code"
    WHERE      T1."U_codConv" = v_convenio
    AND        T1."U_codPlan" = v_plan
    AND        :p_fecha BETWEEN T0."U_fecini" AND T0."U_fecfin" AND T0."U_nivServ" =  :p_NivelServicio  limit 1)
    AND    T2."U_elemento" = :p_Elemento
    AND  T2."U_ItemCode" IN (SELECT "ItemCode" FROM OITM WHERE  "validFor" = 'Y') ORDER BY T2."U_vlrVtaArt" ASC;
    END IF;
    ELSE
    SELECT T1."Code" AS "Código de artículo",
    (SELECT "ItemName" FROM   OITM WHERE  "ItemCode" = T1."Code") AS "Descripción del artículo",
    0 AS "Valor Venta Articulo",
    0  AS "Valor Excedente"
    FROM OITT T0  INNER JOIN ITT1 T1 ON T0."Code" = T1."Father"
    WHERE T0."Code" = :p_ItemFather AND T1."U_OK1_SF_CODELEM" = :p_Elemento AND T1."Code" = :p_ItemSon
    AND  T1."Code" IN (SELECT "ItemCode" FROM OITM WHERE  "validFor" = 'Y');
    END IF;
    END IF;


    ELSE

    SELECT
    T0."ItemCode"            AS "Número de artículo",
    T0."ItemName"            AS "Descripción del artículo",
    T0."U_lineaElem"         AS "Linea del Elemento"
    FROM OITM T0 WHERE
    T0."U_OK1_SF_ELEM" = :p_Elemento
    AND  T0."U_lineaElem" = (SELECT "U_Linea" FROM  "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM"  WHERE  "DocEntry" = :p_NumContratoL AND "U_IdLinea" = :p_LineaPren)
    AND T0."ItemCode" IN (SELECT "ItemCode" FROM OITM WHERE  "validFor" = 'Y')

    UNION ALL

    SELECT
    T0."ItemCode"            AS "Número de artículo",
    T0."ItemName"            AS "Descripción del artículo",
    T0."U_lineaElem"         AS "Linea del Elemento"
    FROM OITM T0 INNER JOIN ITM1 T1 ON T0."ItemCode" = T1."ItemCode"  INNER JOIN  "@OK1_EXE_VIGPLNSPRAR" T2 ON T0."ItemCode" = T2."U_ItemCode"
    WHERE  "PriceList" =  (SELECT "U_lstPrePubli" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE "U_sede" = :p_Sede)
    AND  T0."U_OK1_SF_ELEM" = :p_Elemento
    AND  T1."Price"   &lt;= (SELECT  "U_Valor" FROM   "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM" WHERE   "DocEntry" = :p_NumContratoL AND "U_IdLinea" = :p_LineaPren )
    AND  T0."U_lineaElem" != (SELECT "U_Linea" FROM  "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM"  WHERE  "DocEntry" = :p_NumContratoL AND "U_IdLinea" = :p_LineaPren)
    AND T0."ItemCode" IN (SELECT "ItemCode" FROM OITM WHERE  "validFor" = 'Y')
    AND  T2."Code" = v_convenioPrenec
    || '-'
    || v_planPrenec
    || '-'
    ||
    (
    SELECT     CAST("U_IdLinea" AS NVARCHAR)
    FROM       "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEFEC" T0
    INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEM" T1
    ON         T0."Code"= T1."Code"
    WHERE      T1."U_codConv" = v_convenioPrenec
    AND        T1."U_codPlan" = v_planPrenec
    AND        :p_fecha BETWEEN T0."U_fecini" AND T0."U_fecfin" AND T0."U_nivServ" =  :p_NivelServicio  limit 1)
    AND  T2."U_vlrVtaArt"  = 0

    UNION ALL

    SELECT "U_ItemCode" AS "Código de artículo",
    "U_ItemName" AS "Descripción del artículo",
    T0."U_lineaElem"         AS "Linea del Elemento"
    FROM   "@OK1_EXE_VIGPLNSPRAR" T2 INNER JOIN OITM T0 ON T0."ItemCode" = T2."U_ItemCode"
    WHERE  T2."Code" =  v_convenioPrenec
    || '-'
    || v_planPrenec
    || '-'
    ||
    (
    SELECT     CAST("U_IdLinea" AS NVARCHAR)
    FROM       "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEFEC" T0
    INNER JOIN "@OK1_EXE_VIGENELEM" T1
    ON         T0."Code"= T1."Code"
    WHERE      T1."U_codConv" = v_convenioPrenec
    AND        T1."U_codPlan" = v_planPrenec
    AND        :p_fecha BETWEEN T0."U_fecini" AND T0."U_fecfin" AND T0."U_nivServ" =  :p_NivelServicio  limit 1)
    AND    T2."U_elemento" = :p_Elemento
    AND  T0."U_lineaElem" != (SELECT "U_Linea" FROM  "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM"  WHERE   "DocEntry" = :p_NumContratoL AND "U_IdLinea" = :p_LineaPren )
    AND  T2."U_vlrVtaArt"   &lt;= (SELECT  "U_Valor" FROM   "@OK1_EXE_PRFIN_ELEM" WHERE    "DocEntry" = :p_NumContratoL AND "U_IdLinea" = :p_LineaPren )
    AND  T2."U_ItemCode" IN (SELECT "ItemCode" FROM OITM WHERE  "validFor" = 'Y')
    AND  T2."U_vlrVtaArt"  > 0;

    END IF;
    END IF;


    END;
  </Procedure>

  <Procedure ID="OK1_LCH_VALIDACIONES">
    CREATE PROCEDURE "OK1_LCH_VALIDACIONES"
    (IN servFun numeric,IN lisCheq numeric,IN tipListCheq numeric, IN tipoForm varchar(1)) AS
    BEGIN
    DECLARE CANT_OLAB integer;
    DECLARE CANT_OTRA integer;
    DECLARE NUM_TRASLADO integer;
    DECLARE NUM_LABORATORIO integer;
    DECLARE INICIO_PROCESO VARCHAR(1);
    DECLARE LINEAS integer;


    IF(tipoForm='T') THEN
    IF(tipListCheq=1) THEN
    CREATE GLOBAL TEMPORARY TABLE "ORDESNES_TRASLADO" (Entry INTEGER);
    TRUNCATE TABLE "ORDESNES_TRASLADO";
    INSERT INTO "ORDESNES_TRASLADO"
    (SELECT "U_DocOrdenTsl" FROM "@OK1_SF_LISCHEQ_TRA" WHERE "DocEntry"=lisCheq);
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO CANT_OTRA FROM "ORDESNES_TRASLADO";

    WHILE CANT_OTRA &lt;&gt; 0 DO
    SELECT(SELECT TOP 1 Entry FROM "ORDESNES_TRASLADO") INTO NUM_TRASLADO FROM DUMMY;
    UPDATE "@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA"
    SET "U_kmInicial"=(select "U_OK1_SF_KILOMINI" from "@OK1_SF_LISCHEQ_TRA" where "DocEntry"=lisCheq and "U_DocOrdenTsl"=NUM_TRASLADO),
    "U_kmFinal"=(select "U_OK1_SF_KILOMFIN" from "@OK1_SF_LISCHEQ_TRA" where "DocEntry"=lisCheq and "U_DocOrdenTsl"=NUM_TRASLADO)
    WHERE "DocEntry"=NUM_TRASLADO AND "U_nroOrdenSF"=servFun;
    DELETE FROM "ORDESNES_TRASLADO"
    WHERE Entry = (SELECT TOP 1 Entry FROM "ORDESNES_TRASLADO");
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO CANT_OTRA FROM "ORDESNES_TRASLADO";
    END WHILE;
    DROP TABLE "ORDESNES_TRASLADO";
    END IF;
    END IF;

    IF(tipoForm='L') THEN
    END IF;

    IF(tipoForm='C') THEN
    END IF;

    END;
  </Procedure>
  <Procedure ID="OK1_addInsSrvFun_SuperaFechaVencimientoCotizacion">
    CREATE PROCEDURE "OK1_addInsSrvFun_SuperaFechaVencimientoCotizacion" AS
    BEGIN
    DECLARE CANT_SERV integer;
    DECLARE v_motCierre varchar(30);
    DECLARE v_estadoOrden varchar(1);

    CREATE GLOBAL TEMPORARY TABLE "SYSTEM"."CANT_SERV_FechaServ" (Entry INTEGER);
    TRUNCATE TABLE "SYSTEM"."CANT_SERV_FechaServ";
    INSERT INTO "SYSTEM"."CANT_SERV_FechaServ" (SELECT "DocEntry" FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" WHERE "U_FechaVencCotz" = ADD_DAYS(CURRENT_DATE,-1)  AND "U_Estado"  = '1' AND "Series" IN (SELECT "U_serieCotizSF" FROM "@OK1_SF_SERIESXSUCUR" WHERE "U_sede" = "U_VSede") ORDER BY "DocEntry" );

    SELECT 1 INTO CANT_SERV FROM DUMMY;

    WHILE CANT_SERV  &lt;&gt; 0 DO
    SELECT (SELECT "Code" FROM "@OK1_SF_MOTCANCSERV" WHERE "U_supFecVenc" = 'Y') INTO v_motCierre FROM DUMMY;
    SELECT (SELECT "U_Estado" FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" WHERE "DocEntry" = (SELECT TOP 1 Entry FROM "SYSTEM"."CANT_SERV_FechaServ")) INTO v_estadoOrden FROM DUMMY;

    IF (:v_estadoOrden   =  '1') THEN
    UPDATE "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" SET "U_Estado" = '6',"U_MotCierre" = v_motCierre WHERE "DocEntry" = (SELECT TOP 1 Entry FROM "SYSTEM"."CANT_SERV_FechaServ");
    INSERT INTO "@OK1_SF_LOGALELLAMAD" ("DocEntry", "LineId","VisOrder","Object", "U_Fecha", "U_Descrip", "U_Usuario","U_Hora")
    VALUES ((SELECT TOP 1 Entry FROM "SYSTEM"."CANT_SERV_FechaServ"), (SELECT IFNULL(MAX("LineId") + 1, 1) FROM "@OK1_SF_LOGALELLAMAD" WHERE "DocEntry" = (SELECT TOP 1 Entry FROM "SYSTEM"."CANT_SERV_FechaServ")),(SELECT IFNULL(MAX("VisOrder") + 1, 1) FROM "@OK1_SF_LOGALELLAMAD" WHERE "DocEntry" = (SELECT TOP 1 Entry FROM "SYSTEM"."CANT_SERV_FechaServ")),'OK1_SF_LLAMADA', CURRENT_DATE,'Se cierra la cotización porque supera la fecha de vencimiento.', 'SISTEMA',REPLACE(SUBSTRING(CAST(CURRENT_TIME AS NVARCHAR),0,5),':',''));
    END IF;

    DELETE FROM "SYSTEM"."CANT_SERV_FechaServ" WHERE Entry = (SELECT TOP 1 Entry FROM "SYSTEM"."CANT_SERV_FechaServ");
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO CANT_SERV FROM "SYSTEM"."CANT_SERV_FechaServ";
    END WHILE;

    TRUNCATE TABLE "SYSTEM"."CANT_SERV_FechaServ";
    DROP TABLE "SYSTEM"."CANT_SERV_FechaServ";
    END;
  </Procedure>

  <Procedure ID="OK1_SF_DISPONIBILIDAD_RECURSO">
    CREATE PROCEDURE "OK1_SF_DISPONIBILIDAD_RECURSO"
    (IN nroDoc varchar(10),IN CodeRecurso varchar(50), IN ObjectType varchar(30), IN fechaIni varchar(20), IN horaIni varchar(4), IN fechaFin varchar(20),
    IN horaFin varchar(4), IN tipoRecuros varchar (2), IN disponible varchar(1), IN consulta varchar(200)) AS
    v_sql varchar(200);
    cant integer;
    BEGIN

    IF(consulta = 'guardarDispoRecurso') THEN
    SELECT count("DocEntry") INTO cant FROM "@OK1_SF_DISP_RECURSO" WHERE "U_nroDoc" = nroDoc AND  "U_ObjectType" = ObjectType AND "U_CodeRecurso" = CodeRecurso;
    if(:cant  &lt; 1) THEN
    v_sql := ''''|| nroDoc || ''',''' || CodeRecurso ||''','''|| ObjectType || ''',''' || fechaIni || ''',''' ||	horaIni || ''',''' || fechaFin || ''',''' || horaFin || ''','''  || tipoRecuros ||
    ''',''' ||	disponible || ''' ';

    EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'insert into "@OK1_SF_DISP_RECURSO"
    (	"DocEntry",
    "DocNum",
    "U_nroDoc",
    "U_CodeRecurso",
    "U_ObjectType",
    "U_fechaIni",
    "U_horaIni",
    "U_fechaFin",
    "U_horaFin",
    "U_tipoRecurso",
    "U_dispo")
    (
    select
    (SELECT IFNULL(MAX("DocEntry"), 0) + 1 FROM "@OK1_SF_DISP_RECURSO" ),
    (SELECT IFNULL(MAX("DocEntry"), 0) + 1 FROM "@OK1_SF_DISP_RECURSO" ),'
    || :v_sql ||
    'from dummy
    );';
    ELSE
    EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'UPDATE "@OK1_SF_DISP_RECURSO"
    SET "U_nroDoc" = ''' || nroDoc || ''', "U_CodeRecurso" = ''' || CodeRecurso || ''', "U_ObjectType" = ''' || ObjectType ||
    ''', "U_fechaIni" = ''' || fechaIni || ''', "U_horaIni" = ''' || horaIni || ''', "U_fechaFin" = ''' || fechaFin ||
    ''', "U_horaFin" = ''' || horaFin ||
    ''' WHERE "U_nroDoc" = '''||nroDoc ||''' AND  "U_ObjectType" = '''|| ObjectType ||''' AND "U_CodeRecurso" = '''|| CodeRecurso || '''';
    END IF;
    ELSEIF(consulta = 'verificarDispoRecurso') THEN
    SELECT "U_nroDoc", "U_ObjectType", "U_tipoRecurso"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_DISP_RECURSO"
    WHERE ((fechaIni BETWEEN "U_fechaIni" AND "U_fechaFin")
    OR (fechaFin BETWEEN "U_fechaIni" AND "U_fechaFin")
    OR ("U_fechaIni" > fechaIni AND "U_fechaFin"  &lt; fechaFin))
    AND IFNULL("U_CodeRecurso", '') = CodeRecurso
    AND "U_dispo"  = 'N'
    AND "U_tipoRecurso" = tipoRecuros
    AND "U_nroDoc" NOT IN (nroDoc);
    ELSEIF(consulta = 'liberarRecurso') THEN
    SELECT count("DocEntry") INTO cant FROM "@OK1_SF_DISP_RECURSO" WHERE "U_nroDoc" = nroDoc AND  "U_ObjectType" = ObjectType;
    IF(:cant > 1) THEN
    UPDATE "@OK1_SF_DISP_RECURSO" SET "U_dispo" = 'Y' WHERE "U_nroDoc" = nroDoc AND "U_ObjectType" = ObjectType  AND "U_CodeRecurso"  = CodeRecurso;
    END IF;
    END IF;
    END;
  </Procedure>
  <Procedure ID="oldOK1_SF_DISPONIBILIDAD_RECURSO">
    CREATE PROCEDURE "OK1_SF_DISPONIBILIDAD_RECURSO"
    (IN nroDoc varchar(10),IN CodeRecurso varchar(50), IN ObjectType varchar(30), IN fechaIni TIMESTAMP, IN horaIni varchar(4), IN fechaFin TIMESTAMP,
    IN horaFin varchar(4), IN tipoRecuros varchar (2), IN disponible varchar(1), IN consulta varchar(200)) AS
    v_sql varchar(200);
    cant integer;
    BEGIN

    IF(consulta = 'guardarDispoRecurso') THEN
    SELECT count("DocEntry") INTO cant FROM "@OK1_SF_DISP_RECURSO" WHERE "U_nroDoc" = nroDoc AND  "U_ObjectType" = ObjectType AND "U_CodeRecurso" = CodeRecurso;
    if(:cant  &lt; 1) THEN
    v_sql := ''''|| nroDoc || ''',''' || CodeRecurso ||''','''|| ObjectType || ''',''' || fechaIni || ''',''' ||	horaIni || ''',''' || fechaFin || ''',''' || horaFin || ''','''  || tipoRecuros ||
    ''',''' ||	disponible || ''' ';

    DELETE FROM "@OK1_SF_DISP_RECURSO"  WHERE  "U_nroDoc" = nroDoc AND  "U_ObjectType" = ObjectType AND "U_tipoRecurso" = tipoRecuros;

    EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'insert into "@OK1_SF_DISP_RECURSO"
    (	"DocEntry",
    "DocNum",
    "U_nroDoc",
    "U_CodeRecurso",
    "U_ObjectType",
    "U_fechaIni",
    "U_horaIni",
    "U_fechaFin",
    "U_horaFin",
    "U_tipoRecurso",
    "U_dispo")
    (
    select
    (SELECT IFNULL(MAX("DocEntry"), 0) + 1 FROM "@OK1_SF_DISP_RECURSO" ),
    (SELECT IFNULL(MAX("DocEntry"), 0) + 1 FROM "@OK1_SF_DISP_RECURSO" ),'
    || :v_sql ||
    'from dummy
    );';
    ELSE
    EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'UPDATE "@OK1_SF_DISP_RECURSO"
    SET "U_nroDoc" = ''' || nroDoc || ''', "U_CodeRecurso" = ''' || CodeRecurso || ''', "U_ObjectType" = ''' || ObjectType ||
    ''', "U_fechaIni" = ''' || fechaIni || ''', "U_horaIni" = ''' || horaIni || ''', "U_fechaFin" = ''' || fechaFin ||
    ''', "U_horaFin" = ''' || horaFin ||
    ''' WHERE "U_nroDoc" = '''||nroDoc ||''' AND  "U_ObjectType" = '''|| ObjectType ||''' AND "U_CodeRecurso" = '''|| CodeRecurso || '''';
    END IF;
    ELSEIF(consulta = 'verificarDispoRecurso') THEN
    SELECT "U_nroDoc", "U_ObjectType", "U_tipoRecurso"
    FROM "@OK1_SF_DISP_RECURSO"
    WHERE ((fechaIni BETWEEN "U_fechaIni" AND "U_fechaFin")
    OR (fechaFin BETWEEN "U_fechaIni" AND "U_fechaFin")
    OR ("U_fechaIni" > fechaIni AND "U_fechaFin"  &lt; fechaFin))
    AND IFNULL("U_CodeRecurso", '') = CodeRecurso
    AND "U_dispo"  = 'N'
    AND "U_tipoRecurso" = tipoRecuros
    AND "U_nroDoc" NOT IN (nroDoc);
    ELSEIF(consulta = 'liberarRecurso') THEN
    SELECT count("DocEntry") INTO cant FROM "@OK1_SF_DISP_RECURSO" WHERE "U_nroDoc" = nroDoc AND  "U_ObjectType" = ObjectType;
    IF(:cant >= 1) THEN
    UPDATE "@OK1_SF_DISP_RECURSO" SET "U_dispo" = 'Y' WHERE "U_nroDoc" = nroDoc AND "U_ObjectType" = ObjectType;
    END IF;
    END IF;
    END;
  </Procedure>

  <Procedure ID="OK1_addInsSrvFun_CalcularFechaMisaConmemorativa">
    CREATE PROCEDURE "OK1_addInsSrvFun_CalcularFechaMisaConmemorativa" (IN p_DocOSF INTEGER) AS
    BEGIN
    DECLARE v_fecha DATE;
    DECLARE v_diaSemana NVARCHAR(20);

    SELECT (SELECT ADD_MONTHS("U_EFecha",1) FROM "@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD" WHERE "DocEntry" = p_DocOSF) INTO v_fecha FROM DUMMY;
    SELECT (SELECT DAYNAME(v_fecha) FROM DUMMY) INTO v_diaSemana FROM DUMMY;

    WHILE v_diaSemana != 'SUNDAY' DO
    SELECT (SELECT ADD_DAYS(v_fecha,1) FROM DUMMY) INTO v_fecha FROM DUMMY;
    SELECT (SELECT DAYNAME(v_fecha) FROM DUMMY) INTO v_diaSemana FROM DUMMY;
    END WHILE;

    SELECT  v_fecha FROM  DUMMY;

    END;
  </Procedure>
  <Procedure ID="OK1_SF_CAMPOSLCH">
    CREATE PROCEDURE "OK1_SF_CAMPOSLCH"(IN Tipo NVARCHAR(1)) AS
    BEGIN
    IF :Tipo = 'C'
    THEN
    SELECT "AliasID", "Descr" FROM CUFD WHERE "TableID" = '@OK1_SF_LISCHEQ_HEAD';
    END IF;
    IF :Tipo = 'L'
    THEN
    SELECT "AliasID", "Descr" FROM CUFD WHERE "TableID" = '@OK1_SF_LISCHEQ_LAB';
    END IF;
    IF :Tipo = 'T'
    THEN
    SELECT "AliasID", "Descr" FROM CUFD WHERE "TableID" = '@OK1_SF_LISCHEQ_TRA';
    END IF;
    END;
  </Procedure>
  <Procedure ID="OK1_SF_CAMPOSMODEXE">
    CREATE PROCEDURE "OK1_SF_CAMPOSMODEXE"(IN Tipo NVARCHAR(1)) AS
    BEGIN
    IF :Tipo = 'S'
    THEN
    SELECT "AliasID", "Descr" FROM CUFD WHERE "TableID" = '@OK1_SF_LLAMADA_HEAD';
    END IF;
    IF :Tipo = 'P'
    THEN
    SELECT "AliasID", "Descr" FROM CUFD WHERE "TableID" = '@OK1_PQC_OPARQ_HEAD';
    END IF;
    IF :Tipo = 'T'
    THEN
    SELECT "AliasID", "Descr" FROM CUFD WHERE "TableID" = '@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA';
    END IF;
    IF :Tipo = 'L'
    THEN
    SELECT "AliasID", "Descr" FROM CUFD WHERE "TableID" = '@OK1_SF_ORDENLAB';
    END IF;
    END;
  </Procedure>
  <Procedure ID="OK1_addInSrvFun_ActualizarOT">
    CREATE PROCEDURE "OK1_addInSrvFun_ActualizarOT" AS
    BEGIN
    DECLARE CANT_OT integer;
    DECLARE v_numOT integer;
    DECLARE v_numSol integer;
    DECLARE v_tipoTraslOrg NVARCHAR(100);
    DECLARE v_tipoTraslDes NVARCHAR(100);


    CREATE GLOBAL TEMPORARY TABLE "SYSTEM"."TB_Ordenes" (Entry INTEGER, SolTras INTEGER);
    TRUNCATE TABLE "SYSTEM"."TB_Ordenes";

    INSERT INTO "SYSTEM"."TB_Ordenes" (SELECT "DocEntry","U_numSolTras" FROM "@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA"  T0
    WHERE (((T0."U_tipoTraslOrg" IS NULL OR  T0."U_tipoTraslOrg" = '') AND T0."U_direcOrg" IS NOT NULL) OR
    ((T0."U_tipoTraslDes" IS NULL OR  T0."U_tipoTraslDes" = '') AND T0."U_direcDest" IS NOT NULL))
    AND ("U_numSolTras" IS NOT NULL AND TRIM("U_numSolTras") != '') ORDER BY "DocEntry");

    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO CANT_OT FROM "SYSTEM"."TB_Ordenes";
    WHILE CANT_OT  &lt;> 0 DO
    SELECT(SELECT TOP 1 Entry FROM "SYSTEM"."TB_Ordenes" ORDER BY Entry) INTO v_numOT  FROM DUMMY;
    SELECT(SELECT TOP 1 SolTras FROM "SYSTEM"."TB_Ordenes" ORDER BY Entry) INTO v_numSol  FROM DUMMY;
    SELECT(SELECT "U_tipoTraslOrg" FROM OCLG WHERE "ClgCode" = v_numSol) INTO v_tipoTraslOrg  FROM DUMMY;
    SELECT(SELECT "U_tipoTraslDes" FROM OCLG WHERE "ClgCode" = v_numSol) INTO v_tipoTraslDes  FROM DUMMY;


    UPDATE "@OK1_SF_ORDENTRA"
    SET "U_tipoTraslOrg" = v_tipoTraslOrg,
    "U_tipoTraslDes" = v_tipoTraslDes
    WHERE "DocEntry" = v_numOT;

    DELETE FROM "SYSTEM"."TB_Ordenes" WHERE Entry = (SELECT TOP 1 Entry FROM "SYSTEM"."TB_Ordenes"  ORDER BY Entry);
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO CANT_OT FROM "SYSTEM"."TB_Ordenes";
    END WHILE;

    TRUNCATE TABLE "SYSTEM"."TB_Ordenes";
    DROP TABLE "SYSTEM"."TB_Ordenes";
    END;
  </Procedure>
  <Procedure ID="OK1_addInSrvFun_BfCalculoBaseIVA">
    CREATE PROCEDURE "OK1_addInSrvFun_BfCalculoBaseIVA" (
    IN p_ItemCode nvarchar(50),
    IN p_valor double,
    IN p_cant double
    ) AS

    BEGIN
    DECLARE v_valorCalc double;
    DECLARE v_porcIVA double;
    DECLARE v_vlrIVA double;

    SELECT (SELECT (SELECT "Rate" FROM OSTC WHERE "Code" = "TaxCodeAR") AS "Rate" FROM OITM WHERE "ItemCode" = p_ItemCode) INTO v_porcIVA FROM DUMMY;
    v_vlrIVA = 1 + (v_porcIVA/100);
    v_valorCalc = p_valor / v_vlrIVA;

    select IFNULL(ROUND(v_valorCalc/p_cant,0,ROUND_CEILING),0) FROM DUMMY;

    END;
  </Procedure>
  <Procedure ID="OK1_addInSrvFun_BfSalaVelacion">
    CREATE PROCEDURE "OK1_addInSrvFun_BfSalaVelacion" (IN p_multSede varchar(1), IN p_sede varchar(50)) AS
    BEGIN
    IF (p_multSede = 'Y') THEN
    SELECT "Code", "Name" FROM "@OK1_SF_SALAVELAC"
    WHERE  "U_act" = 'Y' ORDER BY "Name";
    ELSE
    SELECT "Code", "Name" FROM "@OK1_SF_SALAVELAC"
    WHERE "U_Sede" = p_sede AND "U_act" = 'Y' ORDER BY "Name";
    END IF;
    END;
  </Procedure>
</Procedures>
